Motivation: 扩散模型记忆能力强引发隐私担忧，现有DP训练虽提供形式化保证但牺牲样本质量。作者转向一个互补问题：采样过程本身会带来多少隐私损失。直接分析现代神经去噪器困难，因其对单个训练样本的敏感度一般不可计算。

Method: 构造并分析剪枝的、隐私可记账的经验去噪器（clipped privacy-accountable empirical denoiser），作为Bayes最优去噪器的免训练估计器，逐步敏感度可显式计算，使每个去噪步骤成为高斯机制。在每步都由所有训练样本贡献时用高斯DP记账；在每次生成样本仅用随机子采样训练数据块时用f-DP权衡函数。以隐私损失分布记账器给出认证上界，并用基于累积量的Edgeworth近似作为快速诊断。进一步分析去噪轨迹上的隐私损失。

Result: 隐私损失沿去噪轨迹增长，在语义内容形成的关键窗口附近急剧上升；Edgeworth近似在部分区域可靠、部分区域偏反保守。在CIFAR-10和CelebA-64上验证：通过形成阶段使用私有经验去噪器、随后切换到公共神经去噪器的混合采样，可改善隐私-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的隐私不仅取决于训练过程，还取决于采样阶段的设计选择，如子采样、停止时间与混合去噪策略。这为采样期隐私保护提供了新的分析与设计视角。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as the foundation of modern generative systems, yet their high memorization capacity raises privacy concerns. While differentially private (DP) training provides formal guarantees, it comes at a cost in sample quality. In this work, we study a complementary question: how much privacy loss is incurred during the sampling process itself? Directly analyzing modern neural denoisers is challenging because their sensitivity to a single training example is generally intractable. To overcome this challenge, we construct and analyze a clipped privacy-accountable empirical denoiser, a training-free estimator of the Bayes-optimal denoiser, whose per-step sensitivity can be computed explicitly, allowing each denoising step to be a Gaussian mechanism. This perspective enables privacy accounting under Gaussian DP when each training example contributes to each denoising step, and under $f$-DP trade-off functions when each generated sample uses a single randomly subsampled block of the training data. We report every $(\varepsilon, δ)$ guarantee as a certified upper bound from a privacy loss distribution accountant, and use a cumulant-based Edgeworth approximation as a fast diagnostic, characterizing where it is reliable and where it becomes anti-conservative. We also analyze the privacy loss along the denoising trajectory and show that it grows steeply near the critical window in which semantic content is formed. This observation motivates hybrid samplers that use the private empirical denoiser through the formative steps where it remains a faithful proxy for the neural denoiser, then switch to a public neural denoiser for the remaining refinement. Our results on CIFAR-10 and CelebA-64 show that the privacy of diffusion models depends not only on the training procedure, but also on sampling-time design choices such as subsampling, stopping time, and hybrid denoising.

</details>


### [70] [EEG-Based Classification of Hypnotic Susceptibility Using Two-Dimensional Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27738)
*Krzysztof Wrobel,Grzegorz M. Wojcik,Filip Postepski,Andrzej Kawiak,Katarzyna Zemla,Grzegorz Sedek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究用2D CNN对最少预处理脑电信号进行分类，以客观评估催眠易感性；前额/额区在LOSO交叉验证下泛化最佳（集成后准确率0.738），枕区在随机划分下表现优异但在LOSO下崩溃，提示受试者特异的脑电指纹效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导性意象（GI）是临床常用的心理放松技术，但患者是否真正进入放松状态缺乏客观评估手段；同时希望避免人工特征提取，检验深度学习在最少预处理脑电上的可行性。

Method: 62名男性被试（32对照、30催眠），256通道密集阵列采集GI与认知任务条件下的脑电；采用二维CNN直接处理仅经带通滤波与z-score归一化的信号；滑动窗口框架在7个解剖脑区、124个10秒时间窗上评估最多868个独立模型；先做片段级随机划分筛选，再用留一被试交叉验证（LOSO）获得受试者独立性能，并以K=10窗口集成。

Result: 随机划分下最佳单窗口模型准确率0.82；LOSO下前部脑区泛化更好，额区平均准确率0.646（AUC 0.692）；K=10窗口集成将前额区提升至平均准确率0.738；随机筛选中占优的枕区在LOSO下降至接近随机水平，反映受试者特异的脑电指纹而非真实组间差异。

Conclusion: 带通滤波加z-score归一化即可支撑具竞争力的无人工特征脑电分类；前部皮层区域是催眠易感性最具泛化性的神经标记；随机划分评估会严重高估性能，应采用LOSO等受试者独立验证。

Abstract: Guided Imagery (GI) is a mind-based relaxation technique widely used in clinical and therapeutic settings. Objectively assessing whether a patient has entered a relaxed state remains a challenge for clinicians. This study investigates electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of hypnotic susceptibility using two-dimensional Convolutional Neural Networks (2D CNNs) applied to minimally preprocessed signals, without manual feature extraction. Data were collected from 62 male participants (32 Control, 30 Hypno) using a 256-channel dense array amplifier during Guided Imagery or cognitive task conditions. A sliding-window framework evaluated up to 868 independent models across seven anatomically defined brain regions and 124 ten-second time windows. Segment-level random-split screening was followed by Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) cross-validation to obtain subject-independent performance estimates, and an ensemble approach combined predictions across K = 10 windows. The best single-window model achieved accuracy of 0.82 on the random split. Under LOSO, anterior regions generalised better across subjects: the frontal region achieved mean accuracy of 0.646 (AUC: 0.692). An ensemble of K = 10 windows further improved the prefrontal region to mean accuracy of 0.738. The occipital region, dominant in random-split screening, collapsed to near-chance under LOSO, revealing subject-specific EEG fingerprinting rather than genuine group differences. Bandpass filtering and z-score normalisation suffice for competitive EEG classification without manual feature extraction. Anterior cortical regions are identified as the most generalisable neural markers of hypnotic susceptibility.

</details>


### [71] [Constant Step Sizes for Muon-Type Spectral Optimizers on Strongly Convex Quadratics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27747)
*Pratik Jawanpuria,Bhabatosh Garai,Bamdev Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在强凸矩阵二次目标上研究常数步长的谱优化器（Muon类），用"增益"与"长度"两个量刻画更新，将其分为三种收敛区域，并揭示Muon实用变体分属三区、取决于归一化与缩放方式而非极分解近似多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等谱优化器用谱变换后的梯度更新权重矩阵，但在非凸、噪声与步长调度等混杂因素下难以孤立分析更新本身的作用，需要简化设定来厘清谱更新的本质行为。

Method: 在强凸矩阵二次目标上分析常数步长谱优化器，用两个量刻画更新：增益（沿各奇异方向放大梯度的因子）与长度（更新的范数），据此划分三种情形：有界增益、增益无界但长度趋零、长度不趋于零。

Result: (i) 有界增益时迭代类似预条件梯度下降，步长由最大曲率决定即可收敛；(ii) 增益无界但长度趋零时仍收敛，可容许步长由总曲率决定；(iii) 长度不趋零时，对几乎所有初始化不收敛。Muon的实用变体落在全部三种区域，且只取决于归一化与缩放方式，与逼近极分解因子的多项式无关；实验验证了这些结论。

Conclusion: 增益与长度是区分谱优化器收敛行为的关键量；Muon变体的收敛性由归一化/缩放决定而非多项式近似，为设计与调参提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Muon and related optimizers update a weight matrix with a spectrally transformed gradient. In this work, we study such spectral optimizers with a constant step size on strongly convex matrix quadratics, which isolate the effect of the update from the confounds of nonconvexity, noise, and step size schedules. We analyze an update using two quantities: the gain, which is the factor by which it amplifies the gradient along each singular direction, and the length, which is the norm of the update. These two quantities place the popular spectral updates in three regimes with different behavior: (i) with a bounded gain, the iterates behave like preconditioned gradient descent and converge for step sizes set by the largest curvature; (ii) with an unbounded gain but a vanishing length, they still converge, with the admissible step sizes set by the total curvature instead; and (iii) with a non-vanishing length, they do not converge for almost every initialization. Interestingly, the practical variants of Muon fall in all three regimes, depending only on how the update is normalized and scaled and not on the polynomial that approximates the polar factor. Experiments validate these findings.

</details>


### [72] [Detection of Memorization in Diffusion Models Through Thermodynamic Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27754)
*Iljung Kim,Kangwoo Park,Yung-Kyun Noh,Daniel D. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对扩散模型中"记忆化"的逆向过程给出解析刻画，并提出一个基于热力学奇点的判据，用于区分正确学习分布与记忆训练样本的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型正确学习分布应逆转密度函数上的扩散过程，但实际常出现"记忆化"失败，即逆向过程退化为从单个训练样本出发的扩散。现有研究缺乏对记忆化逆向过程的解析理解及可靠检测手段。

Method: 提供对记忆化逆向过程的解析表征，并构造一个能够检测相关热力学奇点的判据。

Result: 该判据在多种旨在促进或抑制记忆化的神经网络模型上，均能可靠区分正确学习的网络与发生记忆化的网络。

Conclusion: 通过热力学奇点检测可有效识别扩散模型的记忆化失效，为诊断分布学习质量提供了理论与实用工具。

Abstract: In diffusion models, proper distributional learning entails reversing a diffusion process over underlying density functions, whereas a key failure is memorization, in which the reverse process instead learns diffusion from individual training samples. We provide an analytical characterization of such memorized reverse processes, along with a criterion that detects the associated thermodynamic singularity. The proposed criterion reliably distinguishes between properly learned and memorizing networks across a range of neural network models designed to promote or mitigate memorization.

</details>


### [73] [AC/DC on a Budget - Alternating Sparse Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27758)
*Rahul Nittala,Advait Gadhikar,Tom Jacobs,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析交替稠密-稀疏训练中稠密阶段的作用机制，提出ARC方法，以零初始化掩码参数、缩短稠密阶段、用稀疏重连阶段替换稠密阶段三项改进，在更低的FLOPs下达到或超越基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进神经网络稀疏化方法依赖交替的稠密与稀疏训练阶段；稠密阶段计算开销大但对性能至关重要，因此需要理解其性能增益来源并降低其成本。

Method: 实证分析发现：被掩码参数在重新引入时会获得不成比例的大梯度，从而驱动关键的掩码重连与参数符号翻转。基于此提出三项创新：(1) 在稠密阶段对掩码参数零初始化以促进相关符号翻转；(2) 缩短稠密阶段；(3) 用稀疏重连阶段替代稠密阶段，仅更新活跃掩码及一小部分高梯度的掩码外权重。

Result: 在CIFAR100和ImageNet上，ARC在仅为基线一小部分计算成本的情况下匹配AC/DC性能，并建立了优于标准稀疏到稀疏算法的性能-FLOPs前沿；在OpenWebText上使用GPT-2的实验进一步显示验证困惑度和平均零样本性能均有提升。

Conclusion: 稠密阶段的核心价值来自掩码参数的重新引入所引发的梯度驱动符号翻转与掩码重连；通过针对性地保留这一效应，可在显著降低计算成本的同时维持甚至提升稀疏化模型性能。

Abstract: State-of-the-art neural network sparsification relies on alternating dense and sparse training phases. While computationally expensive, dense phases are critical for performance. Our empirical analysis reveals the mechanics behind this gain: upon reintroduction, previously masked parameters receive disproportionately large gradients that drive essential mask rewiring and parameter sign-flipping. Building on these insights, we optimize the performance-to-FLOPs Pareto frontier through three innovations: (1) zero-initializing masked parameters during dense phases to promote relevant sign flips, (2) shortening dense phases, and (3) replacing the dense phase entirely with a sparse rewiring phase that updates only the active mask and a small, high-gradient fraction of out-of-mask weights. Evaluated on CIFAR100 and ImageNet, our variant ARC (Alternating Rewiring Compression) matches baseline AC/DC performance at a fraction of the computational cost, establishing a superior performance-to-FLOPs frontier also compared to standard sparse-to-sparse algorithms. Experiments with GPT-2 on OpenWebText further show improvements in validation perplexity and average zero-shot performance

</details>


### [74] [Foundation-Style Pretraining for Scalable and Transferable Distributed PV Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27763)
*Dayin Chen,Xiaodan Shi,Xin Xu,Zhongyue Su,Shibo Zhu,Ruizhe Deng,Dongxiao Zhang,Yuntian Chen,Jinyue Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向分布式光伏的领域基础模型式预训练框架，将NWP辅助预测建模为序列补全任务，用混合重构目标在340站预训练，实现对139个空间留出站的零样本6小时至7天预测并优于各类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式光伏站点数量激增且地理分散，现有方法多为单站点或特定预测时长定制，新站点接入需重复训练与维护，难以直接复用到未见站点，存在可扩展性缺口。

Method: 构建领域专用基础模型式预训练框架，从多站点、多预测时长的异构光伏数据学习共享预测模型。将NWP辅助预测形式化为序列补全问题，统一历史光伏功率、气象观测、未来NWP预报与时间编码；设计并系统比较三种预训练目标：预测对齐重构、通用重构与混合重构（融合运行结构与随机序列重构）。实验使用479个分布式光伏站，340站用于模型开发，139个空间留出站用于零样本评估。

Result: 混合重构模型（MR）在6小时至7天各预测时长上，优于代表性站点专用模型与共享模型基线，以及通用时序模型（含近期大规模预训练基础模型）；在预测精度、NWP退化条件与下游适配三方面表现最均衡。预训练模型可直接部署于新站点，并可在本地数据可用后按区域运行目标微调。

Conclusion: 多站点多时长预训练可支撑分布式光伏集群的可扩展预测范式，实现零样本部署与后续本地化适配，缓解逐站训练维护负担。

Abstract: The rapid growth of distributed photovoltaic (PV) generation is increasing the demand for accurate forecasting across large numbers of geographically dispersed stations. Existing approaches are commonly developed for individual sites or specific forecasting horizons, requiring repeated training and maintenance as new stations are integrated and limiting direct reuse at previously unseen sites. To address this scalability gap, this paper presents a domain-specific foundation-style pretraining framework that learns a shared forecasting model from heterogeneous multi-site and multi-horizon PV data. NWP-assisted forecasting is formulated as a sequence-completion problem that unifies historical PV power, meteorological observations, future numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts, and time encodings. Three pretraining objectives are designed and systematically evaluated, including forecast-aligned reconstruction, generic reconstruction, and mixed reconstruction. The mixed objective combines the operational structure of PV forecasting with random sequence reconstruction and provides the most balanced performance across forecasting accuracy, degraded NWP conditions, and downstream adaptation. Experiments on 479 distributed PV stations use 340 stations for model development and 139 spatially held-out stations for zero-shot evaluation. Across horizons from 6 hours to 7 days, the multi-site pretrained MR model outperforms representative station-specific and shared baselines, as well as generalist time-series models, including a recent large-scale pretrained foundation model. The pretrained model can also be directly deployed at new PV stations and subsequently adapted to region-specific operational objectives when local data become available, providing a scalable forecasting paradigm for large distributed PV fleets.

</details>


### [75] [Engram Memory Network: Brain-Inspired Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27770)
*Hyunjun Kim,Myoung Hoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出受人类记忆系统启发的Engram Memory Network (EMN)，用Hopfield式记忆模块迭代检索原型，提升原型类可解释模型在多种数据集上的解释质量与分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原型式可解释AI模型虽分类精度尚可，但在特征各异的数据集上解释质量下降（原型与输入的相关性/相似性不足），且缺乏标准化的原型解释评测体系。

Method: 借鉴认知神经科学中人类记忆系统编码输入相关信息以支持识别的机制，模拟腹侧视觉通路与下颞叶皮层；核心为Hopfield启发的记忆模块，迭代检索代表性原型，记忆精炼后的隐表示同时用于重构与分类，促使所选原型捕获输入相关信息；并提出三方面评测：感知相似度（LPIPS、DISTS）、子类对齐（超越监督标签的细粒度类别匹配）、信息保留（NMI）。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、MNIST、HAM10000、CUB-200上，平均视觉相似度提升17.06%，子类对齐提升104.57%，NMI提升42.42%，同时平均分类精度达87.62%，为所有对比方法中最高。

Conclusion: 神经科学启发的解释机制能有效提升原型式模型的可解释性与可靠性，并建立了多维度原型解释评测范式。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes decision-making. In response, prototype-based models in explainable AI have attracted growing attention for their ability to retrieve visually similar examples as faithful explanations. Although these models achieve competitive classification accuracy, they often exhibit degraded explanation quality across datasets with varying characteristics, limiting their practical applicability. We draw on insights from cognitive neuroscience, particularly the human memory system, which encodes input-relevant information into representations that support recognition. We propose an Engram Memory Network (EMN), a model that emulates the ventral visual stream and inferior temporal cortex in the human brain. Central to this model is a Hopfield-inspired memory module that iteratively retrieves representative prototypes. These memory-refined latents are used for both reconstruction and classification, encouraging the selected prototypes to capture input-relevant information across diverse datasets. To address the lack of standardized evaluation for prototype explanations, we assess three complementary aspects: perceptual similarity (LPIPS and DISTS) for perceived resemblance, subclass alignment, measuring whether prototypes match the input's fine-grained category beyond supervised labels, and information preservation (NMI), measuring how much input information is captured by selected prototypes. Experiments show that EMN improves visual similarity by 17.06%, subclass alignment by 104.57%, and NMI by 42.42% on average across CIFAR-10/100, MNIST, HAM10000, and CUB-200, while achieving the highest average classification accuracy (87.62%) among all methods. These findings underscore the effectiveness of neuroscience-inspired explanation mechanisms in advancing the interpretability and reliability of prototype-based models.

</details>


### [76] [How Much Do $\ell_1$ Attacks Need Concentrated Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27773)
*Melika Shirian,Kianoosh Vadaei,Arshia Hemmat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在全局 ℓ1 预算中施加逐坐标上限（精确在环投影），发现极端集中并非 ℓ1 攻击成功的必要条件：限制单点最大改动后成功率基本保持，而扰动结构显著改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局 ℓ1 预算只约束总改动量，不约束改动的空间分布，因此无法刻画攻击是否依赖少数极端像素。作者希望检验"攻击成功有多少来自大坐标改动"，从而把扰动集中度作为 ℓ1 攻击评估的独立维度。

Method: 在原有 ℓ1 预算外增加逐坐标上限，通过精确的循环内投影（in-loop projection）同时满足联合约束；不修改 APGD-ℓ1、SLIDE、FMN-ℓ1 的目标函数，仅扫描上限值。与事后裁剪（post-hoc clipping）对比，并在 18 种标准模型设置及 ImageNet 上比较成功率、峰值幅度、有效支撑集规模、LPIPS/DISTS。

Result: 上限≤32/255 时，相对各方法原生无上限基线，攻击成功率中位数保留 97%；峰值幅度中位数下降 80%，有效支撑集规模中位数近乎增至三倍。在匹配实际 ℓ1 消耗的条件下，加帽扰动结构仍显著不同；ImageNet 上部分上限设置下 LPIPS 与 DISTS 更低。循环内约束优化比事后裁剪最多多保留 88 个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: 扰动集中度是 ℓ1 攻击评估中与预算互补、且依赖模型与攻击类型的维度；约束需在优化内精确执行。合适的上限因攻击与模型而异，对抗训练模型的集中度敏感度更异质。

Abstract: A global $\ell_1$ budget limits how much an adversary may change an image, not how that change is distributed. We ask how much of an $\ell_1$ attack's observed success remains when large coordinate changes are restricted. We add a per-coordinate cap to the budget, enforce the joint constraint by exact in-loop projection, and sweep the cap for APGD-$\ell_1$, SLIDE, and FMN-$\ell_1$ without altering their objectives. Across 18 standard-model settings, caps of at most $32/255$ retain a median $97\%$ of attack success relative to each method's native uncapped baseline, while median peak magnitude falls by $80\%$ and median effective support nearly triples. At matched realized $\ell_1$, capped perturbations remain structurally distinct; separate ImageNet comparisons also show lower LPIPS and DISTS at selected caps. Direct in-loop constrained optimization preserves up to $88$ percentage points more attack success than post-hoc clipping, highlighting the importance of constraint enforcement. Suitable caps nevertheless vary across attacks and models, and adversarially trained classifiers exhibit more heterogeneous concentration sensitivity. These results establish perturbation concentration as a complementary, model- and attack-dependent axis of $\ell_1$ attack evaluation.

</details>


### [77] [Foundations of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27789)
*Tianyu Zhao,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该技术笔记从几何近似、谱分析与约束优化三个相互衔接的视角解释经验缩放律，并讨论涌现/顿悟与测试时缩放，最后以视觉、分子预测及SE(3)等变情形为例说明领域坐标如何改变资源比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验缩放律刻画了验证损失与模型规模、训练数据量及算力的关系，为资源分配提供定量依据，但缺乏对其中机制的统一解释，本文旨在给出理论性说明。

Method: 三条联动分析：(1) 几何近似，将空间分辨率、内在维度与光滑性联系到函数误差，并由损失函数决定误差向任务风险的转化；(2) 谱分析，结合特征可见性、协方差特征值与目标对齐度，刻画新增数据如何降低未解析预测质量；(3) 约束优化，导出参数—数据平衡与训练—推理权衡。另对涌现与grokking区分评分阈值与学习动力学，对测试时缩放区分候选覆盖与选定答案准确率。

Result: 在视觉与分子预测应用中展示了领域特定坐标如何改变资源比较；SE(3)案例量化了商空间近似收益对维度的依赖，并比较了优化后的算力前沿，同时给出构象覆盖与相关样本精度的独立分析。

Conclusion: 缩放律可由几何近似、谱分解与约束优化统一解释；涌现、grokking与测试时缩放需区分阈值效应与动力学、覆盖与选择两类机制；领域几何（如等变性）显著影响最优资源分配。

Abstract: Empirical scaling laws relate validation loss to model size, training exposure and compute, providing a quantitative basis for allocating resources. This technical note explains these relationships through three linked analyses. Geometric approximation connects spatial resolution, intrinsic dimension and smoothness to function error; the loss function determines how that error transfers to task risk. Spectral analysis combines feature visibility, covariance eigenvalues and target alignment to describe how additional data reduce unresolved predictive mass. Constrained optimization then gives the parameter-data balance and the training-inference trade-off. We also examine emergence and grokking, separating scoring thresholds from learning dynamics, and test-time scaling, separating candidate coverage from selected-answer accuracy. Applications to vision and molecular prediction show how domain-specific coordinates change the resource comparison. An $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ case study quantifies the dimension dependence of quotient-space approximation gains and compares optimized compute frontiers, alongside separate analyses of conformational coverage and correlated-sample precision.

</details>


### [78] [SliMOO: Interpretable Multi-Objective Evolutionary Search for LLM Depth Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27812)
*Guanchen Li,Yixing Xu,Xuanwu Yin,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SliMOO：面向LLM深度剪枝的可解释多目标进化搜索框架，通过任务-层重要性图作先验，结合NSGA-II的Pareto感知搜索获得多任务权衡更优的剪枝掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规模增长带来高昂服务成本，深度剪枝可在无需硬件特定支持的情况下降低显存与时延；但观察到不同Transformer层对任务的贡献存在显著异质性，现有深度剪枝方法忽视该异质性，易过拟合单一领域或基准，因此应将深度剪枝建模为多目标优化而非单分数排序问题。

Method: SliMOO先通过单层移除探测构建任务-层重要性图，给出任务共享层与任务专属层的可解释视图并作为搜索先验；随后在任务-层先验引导下生成候选剪枝掩码，以各任务上相对稠密模型的偏差作为评分，经NSGA-II环境选择进行Pareto感知进化搜索。

Result: 在Llama-3.1与Qwen3系列上，多任务权衡一致优于规则式、贪心与标量化基线；如Llama-3.1-70B在25%稀疏度下，数学域平均从24.33%提升至34.40%，代码域平均从35.03%提升至39.14%。

Conclusion: 将LLM深度剪枝表述为多目标优化问题，并借助可解释的任务-层先验与Pareto搜索，可更有效保持广泛能力，实现优于现有基线方法的任务间权衡。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more powerful, their scale makes them costly to serve. Depth pruning offers a practical route to efficiency by directly reducing memory and latency without hardware-specific support. However, we observe that transformer layers exhibit markedly different task-dependent layer contributions, whereas existing depth-pruning methods ignore this heterogeneity and thus tend to optimize for a narrow domain or benchmark rather than preserve broad capability. This suggests that LLM depth pruning should be formulated as a multi-objective optimization problem rather than a single-score ranking problem.
  We propose SliMOO, an interpretable multi-objective evolutionary search framework for LLM depth pruning. SliMOO first uses single-layer removal probing to construct a task-layer importance map, which provides an interpretable view of task-shared and task-specific layers and serves as a search prior. It then performs a Pareto-aware evolutionary search for pruning masks, where candidates are generated under the guidance of the task-layer prior, scored by their deviation from the dense model on each task, and retained through NSGA-II environmental selection. Across the Llama-3.1 and Qwen3 model families, SliMOO consistently achieves stronger multi-task trade-offs than rule-based, greedy, and scalarized baselines. For example, on Llama-3.1-70B at 25% sparsity, it improves the math-domain average from 24.33% to 34.40% and the code-domain average from 35.03% to 39.14%. Code will be made available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/SliMOO.

</details>


### [79] [From Generic to Dedicated: A Novel Optimizer for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27835)
*Yongyi Wu,Zheng Wang,Sen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出优化器SPIN，通过插值"可塑性"序列（Polyak-Ruppert平均）与"稳定性"序列（Primal平均）显式解耦稳定-可塑性，可无缝替换SGD/AdamW并提升在线持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习（OCL）在严格单遍约束下学习非平稳数据流，易发生灾难性遗忘。现有工作多从数据、结构、优化策略入手，但优化器本身仍直接沿用SGD、AdamW等通用方法，未针对OCL的稳定-可塑性需求专门设计。

Method: 将Polyak-Ruppert平均重新解释为"可塑性"引擎、Primal平均作为"稳定性"锚点，据此提出SPIN优化器：在快速更新的"可塑性"序列与自适应的"稳定性"序列之间进行插值，实现稳定与可塑性的显式解耦。理论上证明SPIN隐含逆Hessian近似，提供Tikhonov正则化以抑制单样本Hessian估计的噪声。SPIN以现有方法产生的梯度为输入，可直接替换标准优化器。

Result: 在多种OCL方法上以SPIN替换标准优化器均获得一致且显著的性能提升，包括具有挑战性的无回放（rehearsal-free）设定及严格内存约束场景。

Conclusion: 优化器层面的稳定-可塑性解耦是提升OCL性能的有效且通用途径，SPIN作为即插即用模块可广泛兼容现有OCL方法。

Abstract: Online Continual Learning (OCL) requires models to learn from non-stationary data streams under a strict single-pass constraint, making them highly susceptible to catastrophic forgetting. While existing studies have explored various strategies from data, architecture, and optimization perspectives, the optimizer often directly uses generic approaches such as SGD and AdamW. In this work, we reveal a new link between optimization dynamics and OCL needs by recasting Polyak-Ruppert averaging as the engine of "plasticity" and Primal averaging as the anchor of "stability". This inspires our novel optimizer, SPIN (Stability-Plasticity INterpolation), which explicitly decouples plasticity and stability by interpolating between a fast-moving "plasticity" sequence and an adaptive "stability" sequence. We theoretically demonstrate that SPIN implicitly employs an inverse Hessian approximation, providing crucial Tikhonov regularization to damp noisy, single-sample Hessian estimates and mitigate forgetting. More importantly, SPIN can be seamlessly integrated into existing OCL methods by taking resulted gradients as input and replacing standard optimizers. Extensive experiments show that using SPIN instead of standard optimizers yields consistent and significant performance gains across multiple OCL approaches, including the challenging rehearsal-free setting and under strict memory constraints.

</details>


### [80] [Elicitation or Expansion? Demystifying Gains from Agent Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27889)
*Younghyun Kim,Wooseok Seo,Minseo Kim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjae Lee,Donghoon Kim,Hyung Il Koo,Jaehyung Kim,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统区分了harness优化带来的性能增益究竟来自“诱发”（elicitation，提高已可达任务的成功率）还是“扩展”（expansion，扩大可解任务范围），并借助pass@k分析比较优化前后harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: harness优化在无需更新模型权重的情况下显著提升语言模型智能体性能，但仅凭朴素成功率无法刻画增益性质：是更可靠地完成原本可达的任务，还是扩展了可解任务范围。这一区分对递归自我改进与演化搜索至关重要，但目前研究不足。

Method: 借鉴强化学习文献中比较训练前后模型的pass@k分析框架，比较优化后的harness与其优化前版本；评估可达任务覆盖范围的变化，将harness优化与模型规模扩展及更多推理时计算进行比较，并考察增益随任务难度的变化。

Result: 在静态预测任务与动态任务上发现：在较大尝试预算下，harness相对更强干预手段的pass@1优势可能发生反转；聚合增益会掩盖其在困难任务上的不足。

Conclusion: harness优化的增益需要更细粒度的诊断，不能仅依赖聚合成功率；pass@k视角有助于揭示增益是诱发还是扩展，对递归自我改进与演化搜索具有指导意义。

Abstract: Recent studies in harness optimization have shown substantial performance gains in language model agents without updating model weights. A well-designed harness more effectively leverages the underlying model's capabilities by shaping context, tool use, and execution workflows. However, naïve success rates alone do not fully characterize whether optimized harness yields more reliable success on tasks already within reach (elicitation) or a broader range of solvable tasks (expansion). This distinction is crucial for recursive self-improvement and evolutionary search, as expansion may provide a broader range of successful experiences to push the capability boundaries. Despite the growing significance, this distinction remains underexplored in harness optimization. We conduct a systematic analysis to study the effect of gains from harness optimization. Building on pass@k analyses in the reinforcement learning (RL) literature that compare models before and after RL training, we compare optimized harnesses with their pre-optimization counterparts. Specifically, we assess changes in reachable task coverage, compare harness optimization with model scaling and increased inference-time compute, and examine how gains vary with task difficulty. Across static prediction and dynamic tasks, we find that pass@1 advantages of harness over stronger interventions can reverse at larger attempt budgets and aggregate gains conceal shortfalls on hard tasks, underscoring the need for a fine-grained diagnosis.

</details>


### [81] [Physical-Variable-Informed Deep Learning for Robust Diagnosis in Industrial Fluid Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27891)
*Yu Sha,Junqi Tao,Dixin Zhou,Horst Stoecker,Domagoj Vnucec,Andreas Widl,Kai Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyNet——一种物理变量引导的深度学习框架，将物理变量作为全局工况约束、响应信号作为局部诊断证据，通过物理-响应交叉注意力机制实现无需严格时间对齐的交互，在四个基准（三个真实空化数据集与一个公开两相流数据集）上全部指标最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业流体输运系统的监测依赖异构传感流，但响应信号随工况变化，且与物理变量在采样率、观测时长和数据粒度上不一致，导致鲁棒故障诊断困难。

Method: 提出PhyNet框架：将物理变量视为全局运行约束、响应信号视为局部诊断证据；设计物理-响应交叉注意力机制，把序列级物理变量token与可学习的全局响应token相连，从而在不要求严格时间对齐的情况下实现跨模态交互。

Result: 在四个基准（三个真实空化数据集、一个公开两相流数据集）上，PhyNet在所有评估指标上均取得最佳性能；类别级分析及注意力、掩码分析显示其对模糊状态的判别能力提升，并表明物理变量能有效约束局部响应的解释。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种面向实际部署的异构工业环境鲁棒诊断方法，验证了以物理变量约束响应信号解释的有效性。

Abstract: Industrial fluid transport systems are monitored through heterogeneous sensing streams, but robust fault diagnosis remains difficult because response signals vary with operating conditions and differ from physical variables in sampling rate, observation duration and data granularity. To address these challenges, we propose a physical-variable-informed deep learning framework (PhyNet) for robust fault diagnosis under real-world sampling heterogeneity. PhyNet treats physical variables as global operating constraints and response signals as local diagnostic evidence. Its physical-to-response cross-attention mechanism connects sequence-level physical-variable tokens with a learnable global response token, enabling interaction without strict temporal alignment. Across four benchmarks, including three real-world cavitation datasets and one public two-phase-flow dataset, PhyNet achieves the best performance across all evaluated metrics. Class-wise analyses, together with attention and masking analyses, show improved discrimination of ambiguous states and indicate that physical variables meaningfully constrain local response interpretation. These findings demonstrate a deployment-oriented approach to robust diagnosis in heterogeneous industrial environments.

</details>


### [82] [The Shape of the DPO Hyperparameter Landscape: What to Measure, How It Moves with Scale, and When Not to Transfer It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27895)
*Hannan Mahadik,David Salinas,Jacek Golebiowski,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模密集超参数网格实验系统刻画了DPO（直接偏好优化）的超参数景观，揭示了最优学习率随模型规模变化、超参数无法跨训练数据集迁移、验证损失是下游性能的劣质代理等关键结论，并提出用短程候选训练与中间检查点降低超参数选择成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO广泛用于语言模型后训练，但其性能高度依赖学习率、参考策略正则系数β等超参数。实践中超参数如何随模型规模、训练步数变化，以及应依据何种指标进行选择，尚缺乏系统认识，导致调参成本高且缺乏可迁移性指导。

Method: 在同一模型族的多个规模上使用密集超参数网格做大规模DPO训练实验；利用中间检查点分析最优超参数随模型规模与更新步数的演化；在静态与动态基准上评估，比较不同基准对超参数选择的信号强度；并对比多种主流超参数优化策略在该场景下的表现。

Result: 最优学习率随模型规模变化；超参数不能跨训练数据集迁移；验证损失与下游基准最优超参数仅呈弱相关，因而是超参数优化的劣质代理；短程候选训练可显著降低超参数选择的计算成本；同时识别出对超参数选择信号最强的基准类型。

Conclusion: DPO超参数必须针对目标模型、数据集和评估目标专门调优，不能依赖默认值或跨设定迁移；应基于下游基准而非验证损失进行选择，并借助短程候选训练与中间检查点降低成本。作者公开全部训练配置与评估结果以支持可复现分析。

Abstract: Direct preference optimisation (DPO) is widely used for language model post-training, but its performance depends strongly on the choice of its hyperparameters, such as the learning rate and the reference-policy regularisation parameter $β$. To better understand the DPO hyperparameter landscape, we conduct extensive experiments using a dense hyperparameter grid across different model scales from the same family. This allows us to analyse how the optimal DPO hyperparameters evolve with model size and the number of update steps, with intermediate checkpoints providing an inexpensive signal to accelerate the hyperparameter optimisation process. Furthermore, by evaluating our models on both static and dynamic benchmarks, we identify which benchmarks provide the strongest signal for hyperparameter selection. Finally, we investigate how different state-of-the-art hyperparameter optimisation strategies perform in this setting. Our experiments show that the optimal learning rate varies with model size and that hyperparameters are not transferable across training datasets. We also find that validation loss is a poor proxy for hyperparameter optimisation, as hyperparameters that minimize validation loss only weakly correlate with optimal hyperparameters for downstream benchmarks. Therefore, DPO hyperparameters need to be tuned specifically for the target model, dataset, and evaluation objective. We also show that short candidate training runs can substantially reduce the cost of this selection. We release all our training configurations and evaluation results to support reproducible analysis of selection quality and training cost.

</details>


### [83] [Near-Duplicate Image Selection Transfers Preferences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27943)
*Jaeyoung Kim,Eunseok Kim,Dongsuk Jang,Seunghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在源照片与人工标题固定的条件下，仅通过“选择近重复图像变体”这一去重式操作，就能使教师模型（curator）的偏好传递给通过图像-标题训练的学生模型，并在无图像的读出中表现为目标相对偏好偏移，且可跨代传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有“潜意识学习”（subliminal learning）研究表明模型可通过不显含偏好的数据传递偏好；本文追问更弱、更隐蔽的通道：当图像内容几乎相同、文本标题完全固定时，仅靠从8个近重复变体中按标题似然挑选其一，是否仍能传递偏好。

Method: 对每张照片构造8个近重复变体，curator模型按标题似然（不使用目标特定提示）选择其一；学生模型在curator的选择上训练，curator与学生共享同一基座检查点。评估用无图像读出：比较目标词与备选词的log-likelihood ratio。设置预设判定规则、目标轮换（猫头鹰→骆驼、橡树、政治人物）、数量匹配的随机重选对照、新源数据银行与重新训练的curator复现，以及线性探针分类选择臂。

Result: 在预设判定规则下，猫头鹰目标在LLaVA-1.5、Qwen2.5-VL、Qwen3-VL、Gemma 4中出现方向性偏移；目标轮换后偏移随之转向新目标。猫头鹰学生模型在3个模型中优于所有数量匹配的随机重选学生模型，在LLaVA-1.5中优于60个中的55个；Qwen3-VL在新源库与重新训练的curator上复现。贪婪选择在Qwen3-VL中使偏移达10.4个百分点，其他模型0.5–1.3个百分点；第一代学生可通过标题将偏好传给第二代（LLaVA-1.5、Qwen3-VL）。线性探针识别选择臂的准确率最高59.7%（随机50%）。

Conclusion: 即使数据内容近乎相同、源照片与标题固定，近重复副本间的去重式选择仍能导致学生模型目标相对读出发生系统性偏移，并可跨代传播；提示近重复数据筛选流程本身即构成一种需警惕的偏好传递通道。偏移幅度受数据集规模、学习率与训练预算影响，线性探针仅略高于随机，说明该信号较隐蔽。

Abstract: Subliminal learning is the phenomenon in which a model transmits its preferences to another model through data that do not explicitly express them. As models increasingly curate training data for other models, we ask whether this transfer can arise from choosing among near-duplicate images while keeping the source photographs and human-written captions fixed. For each photograph, a curator selects one of eight near-duplicate variants by caption likelihood, without target-specific prompts. Students trained on target-preferring curators' selections show higher mean target-versus-alternatives log-likelihood ratios in an image-free readout than students trained on neutral selections. Under a prespecified decision rule, this directional shift appears for an owl target in LLaVA-1.5, Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL, and Gemma 4, with curators and students initialized from the same base checkpoint, and target rotations move it toward camels, oak trees, and a political figure. The owl target-selected student also scores above all count-matched random-reselection students in three models and above 55 of 60 in LLaVA-1.5, and Qwen3-VL shows the shift again on a new source bank with newly trained curators. Its size depends on dataset size, learning rate, and training budget; greedy choices among the eight options move toward the target by 10.4 percentage points in Qwen3-VL and 0.5-1.3 points elsewhere. First-generation students also pass the owl preference to a second generation through captions without explicit owl mentions in LLaVA-1.5 and Qwen3-VL. Thus even a deduplication-style choice among near-identical copies can shift students' target-relative readouts with source photographs and captions fixed; the tested linear probes classify the selecting arm with accuracies up to 59.7% (chance 50%).

</details>


### [84] [Unsupervised Head Network Personalization for Split Computing via Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27952)
*Yutaro Horikawa,Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效的Split Computing头网络训练方法，结合联邦学习与知识蒸馏，在移动端仅用少量非IID无标签数据训练替换头网络，并引入个性化提升精度，通信需求降低95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SC中为适配移动端有限带宽与算力，常将头网络替换为更轻量的网络，但移动端难以准备足量高质量有标签数据，且用户数据含隐私信息导致数据聚合困难。

Method: 利用联邦学习与知识蒸馏，在不暴露数据的前提下，用移动设备上少量非IID、无标签数据训练头网络；并引入个性化机制。

Result: 在仅本方法受数据约束的条件下，精度与基线相当；移动端通信需求降低95%，相比原始模型精度仅下降3–5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提方法可在隐私受限、数据稀缺条件下有效训练SC头网络，兼顾精度与通信效率。

Abstract: Split Computing (SC) is a distributed inference method designed for balancing computational load and reducing latency by partitioning a neural network into head and tail networks and deploying them across both a mobile device and a server, respectively. Due to the limited bandwidth and computational power of mobile devices, the head network is often replaced with a neural network that requires lower computational and communication resources. However, it is challenging to prepare a sufficient quantity and quality of labeled data on the mobile device side for training the replacement head network. Furthermore, the data prepared by mobile users often contains sensitive privacy information, making data aggregation difficult. In this paper, we propose a data-efficient head network training method for SC. This method utilizes federated learning and knowledge distillation to train the head network using a small amount of non-IID and unlabeled data from mobile devices, ensuring that the training datasets are not exposed. Moreover, by introducing personalization, we enhance the accuracy to levels comparable to or exceeding those of existing methods. The experimental evaluations demonstrate that, despite data constraints imposed only on our method, it achieves accuracy comparable to the baselines. Furthermore, it significantly reduces the communication requirements by 95% on mobile devices, with only a 3-5 percentage point drop in accuracy from the original model.

</details>


### [85] [On Johnson-Lindenstrauss Transforms for Byzantine-Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27989)
*Aleksandr Shestakov,Vladimir Aletov,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将 Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) 变换引入拜占庭鲁棒分布式优化，利用其近似保内积/保距离的几何性质对工作节点消息进行压缩，并按与可信参考梯度的对齐程度加权，从而在降低通信成本的同时维持鲁棒聚合，并给出理论分析与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中数据与模型规模持续增长，分布式优化成为必要手段，但通信开销是瓶颈；压缩机制可降低通信成本。JL 变换实现简单且具有强几何保证（高概率近似保持内积与成对距离），因此作者希望借助该性质在拜占庭鲁棒优化中低成本地评估工作节点消息的可靠性。

Method: 在分布式优化框架中对工作节点消息施加 JL 变换实现降维压缩；利用 JL 变换近似保内积的性质，在压缩域内计算各消息与可信参考梯度的对齐程度，并据此为工作节点消息分配权重，实现拜占庭鲁棒聚合。

Result: 理论上证明 JL 变换的几何保持性使压缩后的对齐度量仍能支撑鲁棒加权机制的有效性；实验结果表明该方法在实际中可行，验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: JL 变换可作为连接通信压缩与拜占庭鲁棒性的有效工具，在减少通信开销的同时保留判定消息可信度所需的关键几何信息。

Abstract: The growing scale of data and models in machine learning calls for efficient optimization methods. Distributed optimization addresses this challenge, with compression mechanisms helping to reduce communication costs. A classical tool for dimensionality reduction is the Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) transform, which is simple to implement and offers strong geometric guarantees. In particular, JL transforms approximately preserve inner products and pairwise distances between vectors with high probability. In this paper, we exploit this property in Byzantine-robust optimization, where worker messages are weighted according to their alignment with a trusted reference gradient. We further support our theoretical findings with experimental results.

</details>


### [86] [When Momentum Helps Polyak--Ruppert Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27990)
*Alexandra Suvroikova,Darina Dvinskikh,Yuriy Dorn,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了Polyak–Ruppert平均使SGD与动量法达到相同渐近协方差的收敛速率与可并行化程度，通过在线–离线桥接将尾部均值与经验风险最小化(ERM)间差距分解为中心、协方差与形状误差；结果显示SGD需m≫κ²轮而动量法仅需m≫κ，临界批大小从Θ(√N/κ)提升至Θ(√N/κ)级别，重量球与Nesterov在协方差误差上可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polyak–Ruppert平均已知能让SGD与动量法共享相同渐近协方差，但缺乏对有限次运行收敛速度、可并行化上限的定量刻画，也缺少对所达到估计与离线ERM之间差距的结构性理解。

Method: 针对带加性噪声的强凸二次问题：(1) 证明在线–离线桥：尾部平均样本上的ERM与任意在线过程的尾部平均查询点仅差一步牛顿步；(2) 对SGD、重量球、常动量Nesterov，将该边界项差距分解为中心误差、协方差误差与形状误差；(3) 用必要充分条件与Berry–Esseen均匀界分析各误差；(4) 扩展到重尾/状态依赖噪声与梯度裁剪，并给出非线性Nesterov平均与ERM的一阶比较。

Result: 在最不弯曲方向上中心误差主导：SGD需m≫κ²、动量法仅需m≫κ，样本量为N时临界批大小由Θ(√N/κ)增至Θ(√N/κ)；协方差误差区分两类动量法——重量球沿最陡方向的相对方差超额为Θ(κ^{3/2}/m)，而Nesterov在所有方向仅为O(√κ/m)；形状误差由一致于κ与批大小的Berry–Esseen界控制，与算法无关，因此当另两项误差消失时置信区间有效。

Conclusion: 动量加速不仅体现在渐近协方差相同，更体现在有限样本下中心误差速率的改善与临界批大小的提升；误差分解为并行化与置信区间构造提供了明确判据，并在重尾噪声、状态依赖噪声、裁剪及非线性Nesterov情形下保持可分析的框架。

Abstract: Polyak--Ruppert averaging gives SGD and momentum methods the same asymptotic covariance. We characterize how fast finite runs reach it and how far they can be parallelized. For strongly convex quadratics with additive noise, we prove an online--offline bridge: the empirical risk minimizer on the samples of a tail average is one Newton step from the tail-averaged query point of any online procedure. For SGD, heavy ball, and Nesterov's method with constant momentum, this step is a boundary term at the two ends of the tail and splits the gap to the offline estimator into center, covariance, and shape errors. Necessary and sufficient conditions show that, on the least curved direction, the center error sets the pace: SGD needs $m\ggκ^2$ rounds but momentum only $m\ggκ$, where $κ$ is the condition number, so at sample size $N$ the critical batch size grows from $Θ(\sqrt{N}/κ)$ to $Θ(\sqrt{N/κ})$. The covariance error separates the momentum methods: heavy ball has relative variance excess $Θ(κ^{3/2}/m)$ along the stiffest direction, whereas Nesterov has relative error $O(\sqrtκ/m)$ in every direction. The shape error is method-independent by a Berry--Esseen bound uniform in $κ$ and batch size, so confidence intervals are valid when the other two errors vanish. We further examine how the conclusions change under heavy-tailed or state-dependent noise and clipping, and establish a first-order comparison with ERM for nonlinear Nesterov averaging under explicit conditions.

</details>


### [87] [RANGER: Retrieval Agent for Navigating Graphs with Semantic Evaluation Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28010)
*Artem Mikhaylov,Pavel Konoplev,Nikolay Efanov,Artyom Sorokin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RANGER混合方法：训练时让LLM作为"导师"为RL智能体提供稠密语义奖励，引导基于Transformer策略与GRPO训练的知识图谱导航智能体，在多跳问答检索中优于经典图搜索RAG和启发式奖励RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳KG问答需在结构化环境中精确多步导航：LLM擅长语义理解与推理但难以在结构化图空间中精确导航；RL智能体擅长学导航策略，却难设计语义上有意义的奖励函数。二者互补但缺乏深度融合。

Method: 构建LLM-mentor + RL-agent的混合框架。RL智能体采用Transformer-based policy，用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练，在知识图谱上学习导航策略；训练期间由LLM评估智能体所遍历路径的语义相关性，生成稠密、语义感知的奖励信号，使智能体行为对齐查询的信息需求。该Retrieval Agent for Navigating Graphs with Semantic Evaluation Rewards（RANGER）被嵌入完整问答流水线进行评估。

Result: 在开放问答数据集上，RANGER优于采用经典图搜索的检索增强基线系统，也优于使用启发式奖励训练的RL智能体；NLP指标与LLM-as-a-Judge评分均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将LLM深度集成进RL训练回路可获得更有效、语义更对齐的检索策略，为复杂推理任务提供可行路径，说明LLM语义评价可替代手工启发式奖励来塑造RL导航行为。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models and Reinforcement Learning offers a promising direction for complex sequential decision-making tasks, such as multi-hop question answering over knowledge graphs. While LLMs provide strong semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities, they often struggle with precise, multi-step navigation in structured environments. Conversely, RL agents excel at learning navigation policies but are hindered by the difficulty of designing semantically meaningful reward functions. This paper introduces a novel hybrid approach in which an LLM serves as a mentor to an RL agent during training by providing dense, semantically informed reward signals. The RL agent, built on a Transformer-based policy and trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization, learns to navigate a knowledge graph to retrieve relevant information. The LLM evaluates the semantic relevance of the agent's traversed paths, generating reward signals that align the agent's behavior with the information needs expressed in a query. We integrate this Retrieval Agent for Navigating Graphs with Semantic Evaluation Rewards (RANGER) into a complete question-answering pipeline and evaluate it on the open question answering datasets. Experimental results show that the proposed approach outperforms both a baseline retrieval-augmented system with classical graph search and one using an RL agent trained with heuristic rewards, achieving a significant increase in NLP metrics and LLM-as-a-Judge scores. Our findings demonstrate that deep integration of LLMs into the RL training loop yields more effective and semantically aligned retrieval policies for complex reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [88] [When Does Training on Synthetic Data Provably Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28036)
*Leroy Odunlami,Tobias Riedlinger,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究合成数据训练的二元分类器在何种条件下能迁移到真实分布，给出支持集匹配下的有限样本超额误差界，揭示奇异情形下合成风险无法控制真实误差，并在Lipschitz条件下恢复以"真实样本落在合成支撑外的概率"为量的迁移保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量训练数据是机器学习的主要瓶颈，促使人工生成数据的使用日益增多，但合成数据训练的模型能否可靠迁移到真实分布缺乏理论刻画，尤其在真实分布存在合成分布未覆盖区域时的失效机制尚不清晰。

Method: 在共享条件标签分布假设下进行理论分析：先考虑真实协变量分布关于合成分布绝对连续、密度比受控的情形，推导有限样本超额误差界；再分析部分与完全奇异（真实分布质量落在合成支撑之外）情形，构造反例说明表达能力足够的假设类下合成风险不能一致控制真实误差（即使Wasserstein距离任意小）；最后对假设与条件标签概率对数几率均Lipschitz的函数类给出以覆盖缺失概率为界的迁移保证；并用低维受控实验和32,200张由四种先进生成器生成、与真实ImageNet图像对应的图像进行验证。

Result: 对抗式分类器在合成域上误差仅0.5%，但在真实域上误差达99.25%；常规训练的架构则表现出合成与真实测试误差的正相关，表明迁移可靠性与覆盖缺口密切相关。

Conclusion: 合成数据应在高样本量下配置预算，且模型必须在真实数据上验证；仅凭合成风险无法保证真实世界性能，覆盖缺口是关键风险来源。

Abstract: High-quality training data are a major bottleneck in machine learning, motivating increasing use of artificially generated data. We study when a binary classifier trained on synthetic data transfers successfully to a real distribution, under a shared conditional label distribution. First, we derive finite-sample excess-error bounds when the real covariate distribution is absolutely continuous with respect to the synthetic distribution under a controlled density ratio. Second, we study the partially and fully singular case, where the real distribution has mass outside the synthetic one. If the hypothesis class is sufficiently expressive, synthetic risk alone cannot uniformly control real-world error: a classifier can be nearly optimal on synthetic data while approaching worst-case error on the uncovered real region, even when the distributions are arbitrarily close in Wasserstein distance. For function classes where hypotheses and the log-odds of the conditional label probability are Lipschitz, we recover a bound in terms of the probability that a real sample lies outside the reach of the synthetic data, restoring a transfer guarantee even in the fully singular case. Experiments confirm this in controlled low-dimensional settings and on 32,200 images generated as counterparts to real ImageNet images by four state-of-the-art generators: an adversarial classifier reaches 0.5% error on the synthetic domain but 99.25% error on the real domain, while ordinarily trained architectures show positively correlated synthetic and real test errors. Synthetic data should be budgeted at high sample counts and models validated on real data.

</details>


### [89] [Poisoning-Based Backdoors Are Not Cryptographic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28045)
*Masahiro Kaneko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：基于投毒的后门无法获得密码学意义上的不对称性——任何能被投毒安装的验证器（决定输入是否为触发器的函数）都可被拥有相当数量带标签触发器观测的白盒防御者重构，因此不存在“对攻击者有效且对防御者不可恢复”的后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御后门攻击的关键不是逐个屏蔽触发器，而是识别决定“输入是否为触发器”的验证器函数；因为攻击者可源源不断地生成满足同一验证器的新触发器。若该验证器能像密码认证那样具备不对称性（只有攻击者能构造触发输入，防御者无法从观测中逆推），则基于学习的防御在原理上就不可能。作者因此要回答：这种不对称性是否可能通过投毒安装获得。

Method: 核心是一个归约论证：把“从投毒数据安装验证器”的训练过程本身视为该验证器的学习器——若训练后的模型能对未见触发器触发且保持良性行为，则该训练过程即构成对验证器的有效学习算法。由此推出：(1) 计算上难以学习的验证器无法通过投毒安装；(2) 凡投毒可安装的验证器，持有相当数量带标签触发器观测的白盒防御者均可重构。另给出互补论证：在推理阶段提供给模型的秘密密钥同样可被同一防御者观测到。结果以代表性触发器观测为给定前提，关注“后门何时触发”而非“后门做什么”。

Result: 理论上一旦确立上述归约与密钥可观测性论证，即得到严格的二分结论：有效性与不可恢复性不可兼得。在指令微调的大语言模型上的实验验证了这一二分性，没有任何配置能产生既对攻击者有效、又对防御者不可恢复的后门。

Conclusion: 投毒式后门安装与验证器的可学习性在计算上等价，密码学式不对称在纯投毒范式下不可能存在，基于学习的防御原则上可行；该结论限定于“后门何时触发”这一问题层面，并为后续仅凭投毒数据重构后门验证器的防御方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: To defend against backdoor attacks on neural networks, the defender must identify the verifier function that determines whether an input is a trigger, rather than rejecting individual triggers in isolation, since an attacker can generate new triggers satisfying the same verifier faster than they can be blocked. If a backdoor verifier could be installed with the asymmetry of a cryptographic authentication scheme, in which only the attacker can produce trigger inputs while the defender cannot reverse-engineer the verifier from observations, learning-based defense would be impossible in principle. We show that no such asymmetry can arise from poisoning-based installation, in which the only verifier-dependent input to training is a poisoned dataset. The argument is a reduction. A training procedure that installs a verifier from poisoned data, so that the backdoor fires on unseen triggers while benign behavior is preserved, is itself a learner for that verifier. Any verifier that is computationally hard to learn is therefore impossible to install through poisoning, and any verifier that poisoning can install is reconstructible by a white-box defender holding comparably many labeled trigger observations. A complementary argument shows that a secret key supplied to the model at inference is observable to the same defender. The result takes representative trigger observations as given and concerns when the backdoor fires rather than what it does. Experiments on instruction-tuned large language models confirm the dichotomy, with no configuration yielding a backdoor that is both effective for the attacker and irrecoverable for the defender.

</details>


### [90] [Universal Online Convex Optimization With Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28058)
*Hao Qiu,Andrew Jacobsen,Emmanuel Esposito,Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带延迟反馈的通用在线凸优化，提出单一算法可同时适应一般凸、强凸与指数凹损失类型，无需事先知道曲率类别或参数，遗憾界在双对数因子内达到已知最优的 T 与延迟依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线优化算法通常需预先知道损失函数的曲率类别与参数，且延迟反馈下的曲率自适应研究不足；作者希望用统一算法在未知曲率情形下同时获得最优的 T 与延迟依赖。

Method: 核心是带二阶超额损失保证的延迟元学习器（delayed meta-learner），实现延迟基础学习器的曲率自适应聚合；并设计新的指数凹基础学习器。

Result: 遗憾界约为 ∂(√(T+d_tot))（凸损失）与 σ_max log T（曲率损失），其中 d_tot 为总累积延迟、σ_max 为最大同时缺失观测数；指数凹基础学习器将延迟依赖中的最大延迟替换为可能更小的 σ_max，同时保留互补的 √d_tot 依赖，改进 SOTA。

Conclusion: 单一算法在未知曲率类别下同时实现凸、强凸、指数凹损失的最优延迟遗憾界，其二阶延迟元学习器与新型指数凹基础学习器是关键技术贡献。

Abstract: We study universal online convex optimization with delayed feedback. We develop a single algorithm that simultaneously adapts to general convex, strongly convex, and exp-concave losses without prior knowledge of the curvature class or parameters. Our regret bounds match the best known dependence on the time horizon $T$ and delays up to doubly logarithmic factors: roughly $\sqrt{T + d_{\mathrm{tot}}}$ for convex losses and $σ_{\max}\log T$ rates with curved losses, where $d_{\mathrm{tot}}$ is the total cumulative delay and $σ_{\max}$ is the maximum number of simultaneously missing observations. Our main technical contribution is a delayed meta-learner with a second-order excess-loss guarantee, which enables curvature-adaptive aggregation of delayed base learners. In addition, our new exp-concave base learner yields a sharper regret bound which improves the state-of-the-art delay dependence by replacing the maximum delay with the potentially much smaller $σ_{\max}$, while retaining the complementary $\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{tot}}}$ dependence.

</details>


### [91] [MuPACE: Learning to Pace Muon for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28072)
*Jan Kobiolka,Christian Frey,Josif Grabocka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuPACE将学习率调度选择在LoRA微调问题间进行摊销：固定Muon优化器方向，仅用一个小型循环策略元学习全局步长，条件于训练轨迹对可解析调度施加有界调整。在64个任务上64–78%优于强默认固定调度，与逐任务调优峰值持平，并能一次性迁移到最深64层的未见模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调时合适的学习率尺度随模型、任务与适配器配置显著变化，实践中需对每个新问题重新调参；且训练前固定的调度无法对训练动力学作出反应。

Method: 不改动基础优化器Muon（其归一化更新固定更新方向），仅元学习全局步长；MuPACE是一个小型循环策略，在一组微调运行分布上元训练，以观测到的轨迹为条件，对可解释的解析调度输出有界调整量。

Result: 在64个微调问题上，相对强共享默认值的标准固定调度在64–78%的问题上占优，并与逐任务调优峰值的同类调度相当；仅在≤16层模型上元训练，即可一次性迁移到最深64层的未见任务与模型（如Qwen3-32B等），单次运行即可与扫描式调度竞争。

Conclusion: 学习率调度可通过元学习在LoRA微调问题分布上摊销，仅调节全局步长即可获得优于固定默认、比肩逐任务调参且具备深度泛化能力的调度策略。

Abstract: Learning rate schedules are typically fixed before training begins, yet when fine-tuning large language models with low-rank adapters (LoRA), the appropriate scale varies substantially with the model, task, and adapter configuration. Practitioners re-tune a schedule for every new problem, yet a tuned schedule still cannot react to the training dynamics it encounters. We instead amortize learning rate selection across LoRA fine-tuning problems. We leave the base optimizer, Muon, untouched, so that its normalized update fixes the direction, and meta-learn only the global step size. Our method MuPACE is a small recurrent policy, meta-trained across a distribution of fine-tuning runs, that conditions on the observed trajectory and emits bounded adjustments to an interpretable analytic schedule. On 64 fine-tuning problems, MuPACE outperforms standard fixed schedules at a strong shared default on 64-78% of problems and is on par with the same schedules with per-task tuned peaks. Meta-trained only on models with up to 16 layers, MuPACE transfers one-shot to unseen tasks on models as deep as 64 layers, such as Qwen3-32B and Qwen3.8-27B, where a single run remains competitive with swept schedules.

</details>


### [92] [PULSE: Personalized Opportunistic Learning for Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28081)
*Mattia Giovanni Campana,Dariush Salami,Hüseyin Yiğitler,Stephan Sigg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PULSE：面向可穿戴设备的个性化机会式学习框架，将每次设备相遇视为在线协作决策，仅在同伴梯度与本地学习方向兼容时进行模型融合，以80–94%的通信削减实现同等或更优的个性化性能，并在真实硬件上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备部署后的数据分布与训练数据存在显著差异，单纯本地适应受限于单设备数据量；传统联邦与去中心化学习依赖频繁通信与稳定连接，而现有机会式方法虽利用偶遇机会，却未充分评估对等节点的效用、兼容性与通信开销。

Method: 提出事件驱动框架PULSE，将每次相遇建模为在线协作决策：选择不交互或选取有前景的邻近对等节点；仅当同伴梯度与本地学习方向兼容时才纳入聚合，并利用历史交互经验指导未来的对等节点选择。

Result: 在人体活动识别、跌倒检测与压力识别任务上，基于合成与真实接触轨迹的实验中，PULSE保持或提升了个性化分类性能，同时相比机会式模型平均减少80–94%的对等辅助交换；端侧实验显示对等辅助操作能耗有限，完整实现占用149.8 KiB RAM与561.8 KiB flash。

Conclusion: 选择性机会式协作可在受限可穿戴设备上兼顾有效个性化与低通信、低资源开销。

Abstract: Wearable devices increasingly support personalized health and well-being applications, but post-deployment data can differ substantially from those used for training, while purely local adaptation may be limited by the data available on a single device during real-world operation. Conventional federated and decentralized learning rely on recurrent communication and stable connectivity, whereas existing opportunistic approaches often exploit available encounters without adequately considering their utility, compatibility, or communication cost.
  We propose PULSE, an event-driven framework for personalized opportunistic learning that treats each encounter as an online collaboration decision, selecting either no interaction or a promising nearby peer. Peer gradients are incorporated only when compatible with the local learning direction, while past interactions guide future peer selection. Across human activity recognition, fall detection, and stress recognition under synthetic and real contact traces, PULSE preserves or improves personalized classification performance, while reducing peer-assisted exchanges by 80-94% compared with opportunistic model averaging. On-device experiments further demonstrate its feasibility on wearable hardware: peer-assisted operations require limited energy, while the complete implementation occupies 149.8 KiB of RAM and 561.8 KiB of flash. The obtained results show that selective opportunistic collaboration can provide effective personalization while limiting communication and resource demands on constrained wearable devices.

</details>


### [93] [From Complex Histories to Simple Predictors: Predictive Dimension in Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28090)
*Hantong Feng,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出应以"足够的低维预测状态"而非"重构完整历史"为目标开展连续时间图学习，并定义可独立估计的内在预测维度以度量该视角，据此设计轻量模型 PreDyG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间图学习方法依赖复杂神经架构编码不断扩张的交互历史，但复杂历史诱导的未来条件分布往往可由低维预测状态流形刻画，即未来交互只需少量预测自由度。作者因此质疑重构全历史的必要性，主张识别对未来预测充分的低维状态。

Method: 为使该视角可度量，定义并独立估计"内在预测维度"：从历史条件未来分布中去除与历史无关的边缘分布，对所得预测残差算子进行分解，并估计保持全历史预测能力所需的最小有效秩。基于该发现提出 PreDyG，使模型容量与内在预测维度相匹配。

Result: 跨数据集实验表明，模型性能饱和主要由内在预测维度决定；PreDyG 以代表性复杂基线约 0.07% 的参数量取得更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: 连续时间图学习的有效复杂度受内在预测维度约束，容量与之一致即可；过度依赖复杂架构重构历史并无必要，轻量化设计可在极低参数预算下实现更强的预测表现。

Abstract: Existing continuous-time graph learning methods rely heavily on complex neural architectures to encode ever-expanding interaction histories. However, the future conditional distribution induced by complex histories can often be characterized by a low-dimensional predictive state manifold, implying that only a few predictive degrees of freedom are essential for future interactions. We therefore argue that continuous-time graph learning should identify low-dimensional states sufficient for future prediction rather than reconstruct the full history. To make this perspective measurable, we define and independently estimate the intrinsic predictive dimension, which quantifies the effective predictive degrees of freedom required by a given task. Specifically, we remove the history-independent marginal from the history-conditioned future distribution, decompose the resulting predictive residual operator, and estimate the minimum effective rank needed to preserve the predictive power of the full history. Experiments across datasets show that model performance saturation is largely determined by the intrinsic predictive dimension. Based on this finding, we propose PreDyG, a lightweight model whose capacity is matched to the intrinsic predictive dimension. PreDyG achieves superior predictive performance with only 0.07\% of the parameters of a representative complex baseline.

</details>


### [94] [Causal Discovery for Linear Models with Latent Confounding via Multi-domain Identifiable Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28134)
*Danru Xu,Aapo Hyvärinen,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LinADMG利用多域设定下的可识别表示学习来识别噪声变量，从而学习带隐混杂的线性因果模型（ADMG），不依赖非高斯性或无弓形约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带隐混杂的因果发现是现实数据的常见场景，但现有能完全识别ADMG的方法通常依赖非高斯性假设或图结构约束（如bow-freeness），限制较强。作者希望摆脱这些假设。

Method: 提出Linear ADMG Learning（LinADMG）框架：借助多域（multi-domain）数据设定，通过可识别的表示学习识别噪声变量；理论上证明噪声变量的可识别性足以恢复全部直接因果关系，并在额外的隐混杂"无抵消"（no-cancellation）条件下识别完整的ADMG；实现上采用文献中四种可识别表示学习方法。

Result: 理论上给出可识别性证明（噪声变量可识别⇒直接因果边可恢复；加无抵消条件⇒完整ADMG可识别）；实验上在模拟与半合成数据集上用四种表示学习实现进行验证。

Conclusion: LinADMG提供了一条不依赖非高斯性与无弓形假设、基于多域表示学习的线性ADMG识别路径，拓展了隐混杂因果发现的可识别条件。

Abstract: Causal discovery with latent confounding is particularly challenging, but a common setting in real-world data. In this work, we focus on linear causal models with latent confounding, represented by acyclic directed mixed graphs (ADMGs). Existing methods that fully identify ADMGs typically assume non-Gaussianity or graphical constraints, such as bow-freeness. In contrast, we propose Linear ADMG Learning (LinADMG), a framework for learning ADMGs for linear causal models through identifiable representation learning. Rather than relying on non-Gaussianity or bow-freeness, LinADMG leverages multi-domain settings to identify the noise variables. We show that the identifiability of the noise variables is sufficient for recovering all direct causal relations, and that, under an additional latent-confounding no-cancellation condition, LinADMG can identify the whole ADMG. We implement LinADMG with four identifiable representation learning approaches from the literature and validate the methods on simulated and semisynthetic datasets.

</details>


### [95] [Curriculum Learning for Generative Flexible Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28145)
*Wenyin Zhou,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将课程学习引入基于扩散/流的柔性对接任务，通过线性pacing函数渐进纳入大构象变化样本，得到GCLDock，在PDBBind ESMFold apo基准上top-1成功率达46.52%（含PoseBusters有效性为40.26%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性对接需从apo结构和配体SMILES预测复合物构象，扩散/流生成模型已可建模后验分布，但核心难点是apo→holo结构偏移的长尾问题：少数蛋白发生大构象变化。作者观察到对大幅偏移样本降权可提升泛化，且长尾由真正困难样本与噪声样本混合构成，故适合用课程学习处理。

Method: 在现有扩散/流匹配方法的训练中引入课程学习，采用线性pacing函数，随训练进程逐步提高大构象变化样本的权重/纳入程度，从易到难渐进学习；推理阶段使用relaxation与基于置信度的pose selection。

Result: 在PDBBind ESMFold apo基准上，GCLDock的top-1成功率为46.52%（配体RMSD<2Å），额外要求PoseBusters有效性时为40.26%，优于现有基线；表明线性pacing课程的简单改动即可显著增强已有方法。

Conclusion: 课程学习是缓解柔性对接中apo-holo长尾偏移的有效且低成本策略，可即插即用地增强扩散/流生成式对接模型，并在严格有效性约束下仍保持领先性能。

Abstract: Flexible docking is a highly practical task, aiming to predict the joint configuration of a protein-ligand complex from a protein apo structure and a ligand SMILES string. Diffusion and flow-based generative models have made this task tractable by modeling the posterior distribution over the ligand pose and the protein conformation conditioned on the apo input. The central challenge, however, remains the long-tailed apo-to-holo structure shift, where a small fraction of protein undergo large conformational changes. In this work, motivated by the practical observation that downweighting large shift examples improves generalization, and by the mixed nature of the long tail consisting of genuinely difficult examples and noisy ones, we propose to use curriculum learning for this task, gradually incorporating large conformational change examples during training. With a linear pacing function, we show that an existing method can be strongly enhanced. On the PDBBind ESMFold apo benchmark, with relaxation and confidence-based pose selection, the resulting model GCLDock achieves a 46.52% top-1 success rate (ligand RMSD less than 2A) and 40.26% when additionally requiring PoseBusters validity, outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [96] [Coincidence-Maximizing Customer Clustering Using Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28178)
*Ramiro H. Gálvez,Gabriel Martos Venturini,Iván Mondrzak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以组内重合因子加权平均为目标的客户分组优化问题，证明其强NP难，并给出多项式时间的固定峰值精化算法，在四个公开智能电表面板数据上稳定提升加权重合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电规划依赖客户群体内的峰值需求重合性，而基于相似度的智能电表聚类并不直接优化该目标，因此需要把客户分组问题直接形式化为重合因子优化问题。

Method: 将客户分组建模为最大化各组重合因子（coincidence factor）的加权平均，权重为该组日峰值需求占总个体峰值和的比例；证明联合优化成员归属与组峰值时刻即使对两组情形也是强NP难。据此设计固定峰值精化流程：在识别组峰值时刻与精确重分配客户之间交替，每次重分配在最小群组规模约束下以多项式时间求解并对当前划分紧的下界最大化，使接受的更新严格提升加权重合度；初始化采用个体峰值时刻分布的聚类。在四个公开智能电表面板（27,654户）上以时序留出与200次配对重复、完整重拟合进行评估。

Result: 精化过程在拟合阶段持续提升加权重合度并降低对初始化的敏感性；在留出日上优于随机分组及五个独立基准，相对最强基准的平均配对增益为1.08–3.48个百分点；要求近似等规模组仅小幅降低测试集加权重合度；所得群体随时间保留可辨识的峰值时刻模式。

Conclusion: 直接优化重合因子的精化框架在真实智能电表数据上有效且稳健，可用于峰值感知的客户聚合，且近似等规模约束代价有限。

Abstract: Electricity distribution planning depends on peak-demand coincidence within customer groups, yet similarity-based clustering of smart-meter data does not optimize it directly. We formulate customer grouping to maximize the average of group coincidence factors, weighted by each group's daily share of summed individual peak demand. We prove that jointly optimizing memberships and group peak times is strongly NP-hard even for two groups. We therefore develop a fixed-peak refinement procedure applicable to any feasible partition. It alternates between identifying group peak times and exactly reassigning customers under minimum group-size constraints. Each polynomial-time reassignment maximizes a lower bound tight at the current partition, so accepted updates strictly improve weighted coincidence. We initialize refinement by clustering individual peak-time distributions and evaluate the method on four public smart-meter panels (27,654 customers) using chronological holdouts and 200 paired replicates with complete refitting. Refinement consistently improves weighted coincidence during fitting and reduces sensitivity to initialization. On held-out days, the method outperforms random grouping and five standalone benchmarks, with mean paired gains of 1.08-3.48 percentage points over the strongest benchmark. Requiring nearly equal group sizes lowers test weighted coincidence only modestly. The groups retain recognizable peak-time patterns over time, suggesting applications in peak-aware customer aggregation.

</details>


### [97] [Adaptive Pullback: Reweighting Supervision at Penalty Jumps in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28194)
*Guangyu Liu,Siyuan Gan,Shilong Zhou,Linjian Meng,Boyan Wang,Jing Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文诊断了on-policy distillation中惩罚值的局部变化，发现"惩罚跳变"伴随教师置信度下降（惩罚-置信度张力），据此提出AP-OPD，用前一块作为参考自适应缩放后续块的监督系数，在数学推理与代码生成上均获提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD仅用采样token的学生-教师对数比监督，正值惩罚只看当前值，无法区分"持续高惩罚"与"沿响应突然升高"；作者认为局部变化包含当前惩罚值之外的信息，值得挖掘。

Method: 将同一响应切分为连续token块并比较块间平均惩罚，诊断出在留出token上持续存在的"惩罚跳变"；在每次跳变处，以紧邻的前一块为局部参考，统一缩放后一块的所有监督系数，使其加权平均惩罚等于前一块原始均值，缩放因子由参考自然确定，无需额外强度超参。

Result: 跳变处惩罚上升而教师置信度下降（惩罚-置信度张力）；而进入高惩罚块的边界并无类似置信度变化，说明局部变化提供独立于当前惩罚值的信息；AP-OPD在数学推理与代码生成任务上性能提升。

Conclusion: 惩罚的局部动态是有用的诊断与调控信号；以相邻块为参考的自适应缩放是一种无需额外超参的简洁改进，可推广到OPD类训练流程。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises student-generated responses using sampled-token student-teacher log-ratios. A positive log-ratio penalizes a token that the student assigns higher probability to than the teacher, but its current value does not distinguish sustained high penalties from abrupt increases along a response. We examine these local changes by comparing consecutive blocks of tokens within the same response. Our diagnostic identifies penalty jumps, abrupt increases in average penalty that persist on held-out tokens. Across these jumps, penalties rise while teacher confidence decreases. We call this pattern penalty-confidence tension. Boundaries into high-penalty blocks do not exhibit comparable confidence changes, showing that local changes provide information beyond the current penalty value. Motivated by this observation, we propose Adaptive Pullback OPD (AP-OPD). At each detected jump, AP-OPD uses the preceding block as a local reference and uniformly scales all supervision coefficients in the following block so that its weighted average penalty matches the preceding block's original mean. This reference determines the scaling factor without an additional strength constant. AP-OPD improves performance on both mathematical reasoning and code generation.

</details>


### [98] [Reframing Object-Centric Reinforcement Learning: From Absolute to Relative Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28197)
*Cedric Derstroff,Jannis Blüml,Janosch Moos,Michael Girstl,Zihan Ye,Debora Clever,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以锚点为参照的对象相对坐标（ORCA）及基于Transformer的ORBiT架构，用相对表示替代绝对坐标以提升强化学习智能体的泛化与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象中心的强化学习智能体通常以固定全局坐标系下的位置接收每个对象，但其决策实际依赖对象间的相对位置。绝对表示难以捕捉与行为最相关的局部上下文，导致智能体需在每个可能出现的配置位置重复学习同一响应，场景一旦平移性能即崩溃。

Method: 将实体描述于固定在锚点上的相对参考系中（Object-Relative Coordinates fixed to Anchor, ORCA），并引入Object-Relative inductive-Bias Transformers（ORBiT）——一种处理锚点相对输入的Transformer架构，用于强化学习，同时与MLP智能体对比验证相对表示的通用性。

Result: 相对表示同时提升MLP与Transformer智能体性能；ORBiT在13个Atari游戏上聚合HNS IQM大于1.0，优于基于像素的PPO基线；ORBiT从零学习Foosball并全面超越所有基线。在受控Tag环境中，ORCA可扩展到旋转情形，ORBiT能泛化到未见过的干扰物数量，且这些收益依赖决策相关的锚点。

Conclusion: 以决策相关锚点为核心的相对坐标表示能有效改善强化学习的样本效率与泛化能力，且与Transformer架构结合效果显著；该归纳偏置对旋转与干扰物数量变化具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Object-centric reinforcement learning agents typically receive each object's position in a fixed global frame, even though their decisions usually depend on where objects are relative to one another. These absolute views often fail to capture the local context that is most relevant to the agent's behavior. As a result, an agent must relearn the same response at every location where a configuration can occur, and its performance collapses when the scene is merely shifted. In this work, we propose a shift toward Object-Relative Coordinates fixed to Anchor (ORCA), where entities are described in a relative frame of reference. To operationalize this idea, we introduce Object-Relative inductive-Bias Transformers (ORBiT), a transformer-based architecture for processing anchor-relative inputs in reinforcement learning. Our results suggest these relative representations improve performance for both MLP and transformer agents. Furthermore, we demonstrate that ORBiT outperforms the pixel-based PPO baseline, achieving an aggregate HNS IQM greater than 1.0 over 13 Atari games. ORBiT learns the game of Foosball from scratch, dominating all baselines. In our controlled Tag environment, we further show that ORCA extends to rotations, that ORBiT generalizes to unseen numbers of distractors, and that these gains require a decision-relevant anchor.

</details>


### [99] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Time Series Foundation Models for Electrical Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28200)
*Aliullah,Julian Oelhaf,Edouard Fouché,Jessica Deuschel,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了预训练推理、全量微调与LoRA三种范式在四个电力负荷聚合层级上的预测精度与适配效率，发现rank-16 LoRA平均降低NMAE 7.5%且仅更新约1%参数，在强预训练检查点仍显著受益时提供最优的精度—效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力负荷预测需在国、区域、城市、楼宇多层级分别建模，重复开发目标专用模型成本高。时序基础模型（TSFM）提供可复用预训练骨干，但多域预训练未必充分捕获负荷特性，因此需明确"预训练直接推理 vs 全量微调 vs 低秩适配"在不同聚合层级上的适用性。

Method: 以MOIRAI 1.0 Large为主干、Chronos-2用于跨模型族验证；在四个负荷聚合层级上对比预训练零样本推理、全量微调和rank-16 LoRA，覆盖分布内（ID）与分布外（OOD）设定，并与季节朴素法与XGBoost基线对照，以NMAE衡量精度、以可训练参数量衡量适配效率。

Result: 预训练基础模型在各聚合层级均优于季节朴素与XGBoost基线；MOIRAI Large上rank-16 LoRA平均降低NMAE 7.5%，仅更新3.146M参数（约占模型1%），保留了全量微调的大部分增益；Chronos-2预训练NMAE更低但对适配的收益较小；OOD下适配增益因目标而异，局部区域序列与部分楼宇提升最明显。

Conclusion: LoRA在适配相对强预训练检查点能带来实质增益时，提供了有利的精度—效率权衡；适配收益并非普遍成立，需按目标/层级判断是否值得微调，预训练检查点强度与适配收益之间存在互补而非替代关系。

Abstract: Accurate electrical load forecasting supports power-system operation across countries, regions, cities, and buildings, but repeated development of target-specific models is costly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) provide reusable pretrained backbones, although multi-domain pretraining may not fully capture load-specific characteristics. This paper compares pretrained inference, full fine-tuning, and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) in terms of forecasting accuracy and adaptation efficiency across four electrical load aggregation levels. MOIRAI 1.0 Large serves as the primary backbone, while Chronos-2 assesses whether the findings transfer across model families. The evaluation covers both in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) settings and examines whether compact adaptation provides consistent gains over strong pretrained checkpoints. Pretrained foundation models outperform the implemented seasonal naive and XGBoost baselines across the evaluated aggregation levels. On MOIRAI Large, rank-16 LoRA reduces normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) by 7.5% on average while updating 3.146 million parameters, about 1% of the model, and retains most of the improvement from full fine-tuning. Chronos-2 achieves lower pretrained NMAE than MOIRAI but benefits less from adaptation. OOD results show that adaptation benefits vary by target, with the clearest gains for the local-area series and several buildings. Overall, LoRA provides a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off when adaptation yields a meaningful gain over a strong pretrained checkpoint.

</details>


### [100] [JAXbounce: A Continuous-Control Pong-Like Benchmark for Active Inference Beyond Low Dimensional Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28203)
*Chaitanya Kaul,Markus Klar,Sebastian Stein,Fraser C. Paterson,John H. Williamson,Roderick Murray-Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 JAXbounce：一个连续状态、随机动态、完全可微且可并行的 pong 类连续控制基准，用于在离散低维环境之外评估主动推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动推断评测多局限于离散、低维环境，缺乏连续状态与随机动态下、且能灵活切换观测模态（状态向量/像素）与噪声条件的标准化基准。

Method: 构建 JAX 实现的环境：连续状态、离散时间随机动态、可组合观测模型（状态向量或像素网格）、丰富噪声模型，支持求导与并行；比较三类智能体：确定性基线、基于无迹卡尔曼滤波感知与期望自由能规划的状态型主动推断智能体、以预训练神经网络编码器为测量模型的像素型智能体。

Result: 累计运行超 10,000 次仿真，覆盖三种递增难度场景与八种噪声条件；刻画了连续状态智能体性能随规划、规划时域、观测模态及噪声过程先验知识的变化规律，并在不确定性上升时仍表现出偏好导向行为。

Conclusion: JAXbounce 作为可复现基准公开，为连续控制主动推断研究提供标准化评测平台；结果表明连续主动推断智能体的性能高度依赖规划时域、观测模态与噪声知识。

Abstract: We introduce JAXbounce, a continuous control pong-like benchmark designed to evaluate active inference beyond discrete, low dimensional environments. JAXbounce combines continuous states and stochastic dynamics in discrete time with flexible and composable observation models that allow representing the environment as a state vector or a pixel grid, corruptible through the rich family of noise models we provide. It is fully differentiable and parallelisable in JAX. We run over 10,000 agent simulations on our environment comparing a baseline deterministic solution, to a state-based active inference agent with Unscented Kalman Filter perception and expected free energy planning, and a pixel based agent whose measurement model is a pretrained neural network encoder. All agents are evaluated across three scenarios of increasing difficulty with eight different noise conditions. Our experiments characterise how performance of continuous state agents scales with planning, planning horizons, observation modalities and knowledge of the noise process, exhibiting preference-oriented behaviour even in the presence of increasing uncertainty. We release JAXbounce as a reproducible benchmark for continuous control active inference research.

</details>


### [101] [TPO: Tri-level Distributionally Robust Learning for OOD Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28229)
*Chengtao Jian,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TPO，一个面向分布外偏好对齐的三层分布鲁棒框架，用分层矩形模糊集同时刻画群组级、提示级、偏好级三层偏好分布偏移，并给出可解且可解释的目标函数与有限样本收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO在观测偏好比较上最小化经验风险，隐含假设训练偏好分布与部署偏好分布一致。实际中该i.i.d.假设常被打破，偏好分布偏移呈现群组级、提示级、偏好级三层自然层级结构。现有鲁棒DPO方法要么对联合偏好分布建模不确定性，要么只关注单一偏移来源，未充分挖掘OOD偏好偏移的层级结构，给LLM安全部署带来挑战。

Method: 提出TPO三级分布鲁棒框架：构建分层结构的矩形模糊集，联合建模三层分布不确定性；以Wasserstein距离实例化，导出可处理且可解释的目标，包含闭式偏好级间隔惩罚、提示级对抗嵌入扰动与最坏情况群组重加权，三者各由独立半径控制。

Result: 建立了有限样本收敛性保证；实验验证TPO优于现有鲁棒DPO方法。

Conclusion: 通过分层矩形模糊集刻画偏好偏移的层级结构，可将分布鲁棒偏好对齐转化为由独立半径控制的组合式实用目标，兼顾理论保证与实证有效性，为OOD偏好对齐提供新范式。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) minimizes empirical risk over observed preference comparisons, implicitly assuming that the training preference distribution matches the deployment preference distribution. In practice, this i.i.d. assumption often fails, as preference distribution shift exhibits a natural hierarchical structure across three levels: group-level shift, prompt-level shift, and preference-level shift. Such multi-level distributional mismatch poses a critical challenge for safe LLM deployment, yet existing robust DPO methods typically either model uncertainty over the joint preference distribution or focus on individual sources of shift, leaving the hierarchical structure of OOD preference shifts underexplored. We propose TPO, a tri-level distributionally robust framework for OOD preference alignment, which jointly models all three levels of distributional uncertainty through a hierarchically structured rectangular ambiguity set. By instantiating it with Wasserstein distance, we derive a tractable and interpretable objective that combines a closed-form preference-level margin penalty, prompt-level adversarial embedding perturbation, and worst-case group reweighting, each governed by an independent radius. We establish finite-sample convergence guarantees and empirically validate the effectiveness of TPO over existing robust DPO methods.

</details>


### [102] [Learning Normal Coordinates on Riemannian Manifolds with Flow Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28302)
*Edmond Cunningham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"flow chart"——条件可逆神经网络，近似黎曼流形上点周围的法坐标，使指数/对数映射可用快速欧氏运算代替；核心是设计非负、在法坐标条件下恰为零的高效损失函数。16种几何上性能媲美专用神经测地插值器，预训练后对数映射估计提速60–70倍（中位数），并提升数值测地求解器精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何机器学习中学习的黎曼度量虽灵活，但许多几何算法需反复计算指数与对数映射，计算成本高，限制了学习型黎曼流形的实际可用性。

Method: 训练条件可逆神经网络（flow chart）逼近给定黎曼流形上点邻域的法坐标，从而在学习坐标空间中用快速欧氏运算近似指/对数映射；主要贡献是一个非负、且（在适当域假设下）仅在映射为法坐标时取零的高效训练损失。

Result: 在16种合成与数据导出的几何上，flow chart在对数映射估计上与专用神经测地插值器相当，同时还能估计指数映射；预训练后相对数值基线中位加速60–70倍且对数映射误差相当；其学习坐标可提升数值测地求解器精度。

Conclusion: 以可逆网络学习法坐标是降低学习型黎曼流形几何运算开销的有效途径，兼顾精度与速度。

Abstract: Riemannian manifolds are powerful models for problems with non-Euclidean structure. Recent work in geometric machine learning has focused on training deep neural networks to learn Riemannian metrics from high-dimensional data. However, many geometric algorithms require repeated evaluations of the exponential and logarithmic maps, whose high computational cost restricts the utility of learned Riemannian manifolds in practice. We propose to address this cost by training a conditional invertible neural network, called a flow chart, to approximate normal coordinates around points on a given Riemannian manifold. With this approach, the exponential and logarithmic maps can be approximated by fast Euclidean operations in the model's learned coordinate space. Our main contribution is an efficient loss function for training flow charts that is nonnegative and, under suitable domain assumptions, is zero precisely when the chart is normal. Across sixteen synthetic and data-derived geometries, flow charts are competitive with a specialized neural geodesic interpolator for logarithmic map estimation, while also being able to estimate the exponential map. When pretrained, flow charts provide a median speedup of 60-70x over numerical baselines at comparable logarithmic map estimation error, and their learned coordinates improve the accuracy of a numerical geodesic solver.

</details>


### [103] [Recommending Bacterial Cultivation Conditions from 16S rRNA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28310)
*Juntang Wang,Hao Wu,Yihan Wang,Runkun Guo,Shixin Xu,Dongmian Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DSNI基准（65,023株细菌，16S rRNA序列+4项培养条件，系统发育感知划分）与Variability-Gated Encoder，利用16S rRNA的超变区/保守区镶嵌结构引入位置加权先验，仅1280参数即可使联合准确率提升4.7%，互补RNABERT达44.2%精确匹配，top-25可覆盖90.2%真实培养方案，搜索空间缩小约60倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从序列数据推荐细菌培养条件属于结构化预测难题，但缺乏标准化基准，且现有方法未充分利用序列结构信息。

Method: 构建DSNI基准：65,023株菌，采用系统发育感知（phylogeny-aware）划分，配对16S rRNA序列与培养基、温度、pH、氧气四类条件；提出Variability-Gated Encoder，基于生物学特征（区域类型、位置、局部复杂度）学习位置相关的重要性权重门控，为Transformer引入显式归纳偏置，门控开销仅1,280参数（<1%）。

Result: 可学习门控使平坦Transformer的联合准确率提升4.7%；与RNABERT互补，达到44.2%精确匹配联合准确率；top-25推荐覆盖90.2%的真实培养方案，搜索空间压缩约60倍。

Conclusion: 显式建模16S rRNA镶嵌结构的位置门控是低数据场景下有效且轻量的归纳偏置，DSNI为培养条件推荐提供了标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Recommending bacterial cultivation conditions from sequence data is a challenging structured problem characterized by missing standardized benchmarks and limited use of sequence structure. To address this, we introduce DSNI, a benchmark of 65,023 bacterial strains with phylogeny-aware splits, pairing 16S rRNA sequences with four cultivation conditions (media, temperature, pH, oxygen). Exploiting the mosaic structure of 16S rRNA, where hypervariable regions support discrimination and conserved regions support primer design, we propose a Variability-Gated Encoder that learns position-dependent importance weights conditioned on biological features (region type, position, and local complexity). The gating module adds minimal overhead (1,280 parameters, <1%) while providing an explicit inductive bias valuable in low-data regimes. Experiments demonstrate that the learnable gate improves flat Transformers by 4.7% in joint accuracy and complements RNABERT, yielding 44.2% exact-match joint accuracy. The model also recovers 90.2% of ground-truth cultivation protocols within its top-25 recommendations, narrowing the exhaustive search space by approximately 60 times.

</details>


### [104] [Manifold-Informed Neural Networks for Low-Label ECG Electrolyte Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28322)
*Juntang Wang,Hao Wu,Yihan Wang,Dongmian Zou,Shixin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流形信息神经网络（MINN），用学习潜在空间k近邻图上的离散泊松方程残差替代PINN的物理算子残差，在MIMIC-IV-ECG上实现K⁺/Ca²⁺/Mg²⁺三分类，K⁺ Macro-F1达0.61（最佳基线0.55）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN依赖已知微分算子残差进行监督，但临床信号、行为测量、材料属性等高风险推断问题缺乏显式算子。经典流形正则化（拉普拉斯惩罚）虽无需算子，却受限于齐次光滑先验。作者希望在无物理方程可用的情形下，构造与PINN结构平行的监督范式，同时突破齐次光滑性限制。

Method: 在学习到的隐空间构建k近邻图，以离散泊松方程 L_G H = s_φ(x, m) 的平方残差作为监督项，源项 s_φ 为可学习、任务感知的函数，直接输入特征与辅助元数据；总损失为算子残差平方加标签数据（对应PINN的算子残差加边界数据）。s=0时退化为齐次拉普拉斯方程 L_G H=0（与经典流形正则化同方程但惩罚形式不同：后者最小化Dirichlet能量 tr(HᵀL_G H)）。另用门控Feature-wise Linear Modulation将元数据独立融合进预测分支。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ECG 12导联心电图上做K⁺/Ca²⁺/Mg²⁺三分类，对比12个基线（监督与表格方法、ECG基础模型、流形/图方法）。MINN取得最高K⁺ Macro-F1 0.61（最佳基线0.55），Ca²⁺与Mg²⁺上一致提升，1%标注数据下相对提升31%。

Conclusion: 六组针对性消融表明，增益来自非齐次泊松公式本身，而非单纯的图平滑；MINN可作为PINN在无已知算子问题上的平行范式。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) supervise on the residual of a known differential operator. Many high-stakes inference problems admit no such operator: clinical signals, behavioral measurements, materials properties. We introduce the Manifold-Informed Neural Network (MINN), a PINN-parallel paradigm that replaces the physics operator residual with a manifold operator residual: a discrete Poisson equation $\mathbf{L}_G \mathbf{H} = s_φ(\mathbf{x}, \mathbf{m})$ on a $k$-nearest-neighbor graph in the learned latent space, with a learned, task-aware source.
