The construction is a structural parallel to PINNs (squared operator residual plus label data, in place of operator residual plus boundary data); the $s{=}0$ limit recovers the homogeneous Laplace equation $\mathbf{L}_G\mathbf{H}=\mathbf{0}$ (classical manifold regularization solves the same equation but minimizes the Dirichlet energy $\operatorname{tr}(\mathbf{H}^\top\mathbf{L}_G\mathbf{H})$, a different penalty form). The source $s_φ$ takes input features and auxiliary metadata directly, breaking the homogeneous smoothness prior that limits classical Laplacian penalties; gated Feature-wise Linear Modulation separately fuses metadata into the prediction branch. We evaluate MINN on three-class classification of potassium, calcium, and magnesium from 12-lead ECGs in MIMIC-IV-ECG, against twelve baselines spanning supervised and tabular methods, ECG foundation models, and manifold/graph approaches. MINN attains the highest K$^+$ Macro-F1 of 0.61 (best baseline 0.55), with consistent gains on Ca$^{2+}$ and Mg$^{2+}$ and a 31% relative improvement at 1% labeled data. Six targeted ablations show that the inhomogeneous Poisson formulation, not graph smoothing alone, drives the gain.

</details>


### [105] [Observation Assimilation Transformer: Probabilistic Weather Analysis from Sparse Station Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28327)
*Christian Donner,Michele Cattaneo,Maxim Samarin,Francesco Zanetta,Icíar Lloréns Jover,Garbiela Aznar Siguan,Michele Volpi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出观测同化Transformer（OAT），一种几何感知神经过程，通过同化稀疏站点观测将NWP先验场订正为高分辨率后验场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将稀疏、含缺测的站点观测与NWP先验场融合，实现任意连续位置的集合/概率化高分辨率订正与降尺度，是资料同化与降尺度交叉的关键需求。

Method: 编码器-解码器Transformer：以观测站点位置及其NWP先验为条件，在任意未观测查询位置上做预测；稀疏注意力使其可扩展至数千站点，且仅依赖相对位置而非绝对坐标；支持部分缺测、无需网格投影、输出参数化分布；配合站点随机化训练算法与CRPS损失。

Result: 在区域尺度（数百站点）与大陆尺度（数千站点）两个数据集上优于多个基线，预测校准良好；案例中将ERA5（~31 km）降尺度至COSMO（1 km），在测试站上温度与降水的MAE和CRPS均优于COSMO。

Conclusion: OAT以几何感知、可扩展、概率化的方式统一了同化与降尺度，验证了神经过程在稀疏观测融合中的有效性，并可超越高分辨率模式本身的表现。

Abstract: We present the Observation Assimilation Transformer (OAT), a geometry-aware neural process that corrects and refines a prior numerical weather prediction (NWP) field into a high-resolution posterior field by assimilating sparse observations. OAT is an encoder-decoder transformer that considers a set of observed locations and their NWP prior to condition predictions at a set of arbitrary unobserved query locations. Its sparse attention mechanism makes it scalable to thousands of station observations and depends on relative positions between locations, never on the absolute coordinates. The model allows for partially missing observations, predictions at arbitrary continuous locations without grid projection, and outputs are parametric distributions. A station-randomized training algorithm is developed with continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. We test OAT on two datasets - one at regional scale with hundreds of stations, and one at continental scale with thousands of stations. On these datasets OAT outperforms several baselines and shows well calibrated predictions. In an illustrative case study, OAT's downscaling capabilities are demonstrated by downscaling ERA5 (~31km) to COSMO (1km), where OAT achieves lower mean absolute error (MAE) and CRPS for temperature and precipitation on test stations compared to COSMO.

</details>


### [106] [Identifying Dependent Components from Multi-domain Linear Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28330)
*Danru Xu,Lauri Parkkonen,Sara Magliacane,Aapo Hyvärinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究多数据域下含相依隐成分的线性混合模型，提出仅在二阶统计量下即可识别隐变量与混合函数的方法，并给出MuDo-CoM算法（联合对角化的推广），在仿真与真实fMRI数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型多假设成分独立或不相关（ICA范式）；允许相依成分的工作又依赖参数化依赖形式、多视角设定或干预，或不恢复单成分。作者希望在更弱、更实际的假设下识别相依隐成分。

Method: 设定多域场景：各域仅通过成分的域特定缩放不同，而隐成分分布跨域共享（可建模不同传感器、实验室、实验条件或个体差异）。理论证明在足够域变异性下，隐变量与混合函数可由二阶统计量识别；据此提出Multi-Domain Covariance Matching (MuDo-CoM)，推广既往联合对角化方法。

Result: 理论上给出基于二阶统计量的可识别性结论；MuDo-CoM在模拟数据与真实fMRI数据集上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过域特定缩放的共享隐分布假设，可在不依赖成分独立性的情况下识别相依隐成分与混合函数，MuDo-CoM为联合对角化提供了统一推广且具实证有效性。

Abstract: We study a linear mixing model with dependent latent components, assuming multiple data domains. Most existing models assume that the components are independent or at least uncorrelated, in line with independent component analysis (ICA). Some recent work allows for dependent components, but then makes specific assumptions such as parametric forms of dependencies, multi-view settings, or interventions, or does not recover the individual components. In contrast, we consider a multi-domain setting in which domains differ through domain-specific scalings of the components, while the distribution of the underlying latent components is the same across domains. This approach can model data collected, for example, from different sensors measuring the same process, different laboratories conducting the same experiment, different experimental conditions, or different subjects that might differ in biological or physiological factors. We show that, under sufficient domain variability, latent variables and mixing functions can be identified from second-order statistics alone. We propose the Multi-Domain Covariance Matching (MuDo-CoM) algorithm that generalizes previous methods of joint diagonalization. MuDo-CoM is validated on simulated data and a real-world fMRI dataset.

</details>


### [107] [Characterizing Cross-Relay Generalization Failure in Machine Learning-Based Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28348)
*Nithin Pradeep Nadayil Kizhakkethil,Julian Oelhaf,Christian Bergler,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于局部测量的机器学习故障检测器在不同继电器间（跨站点）的泛化性，发现总体F1几乎不变，但误报率从0.088升至0.341、漏报率从0.399降至0.318，误差呈明显的方向性（位置结构），说明聚合指标会掩盖误报行为的分布性偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML故障检测器多在训练所用继电器位置评估，但电网各处的电压电流测量存在差异，缺乏对跨继电器泛化能力的受控评估；且聚合指标可能掩盖误报/漏报等分类型误差的结构性变化。

Method: 在仿真110 kV输电网中，采用50 ms窗口的局部测量，做全网范围的二值故障检测（非选线跳闸）。使用多层感知机，穷举8个继电器的64种源-目标组合×5个随机种子；采用仿真级（simulation-level）划分与仅源端阈值选择，实现无目标校准的受控留出评估。辅以线性探针与类条件潜在分布分析。

Result: 平均F1几乎不变（站内0.730 vs 跨站0.734），但平均FPR由0.088升至0.341，平均FNR由0.399降至0.318；变化具强方向性：Bus 2训练的模型在Bus 1、Bus 3继电器上平均FPR达0.670，而同母线组内跨站迁移仅0.096。线性探针表明表示保留粗粒度母线组信息，但类条件潜变量分布与迁移误差无一致的单调关联。

Conclusion: 聚合指标会掩盖位置结构化的误报行为变化；评估跨继电器泛化时应分别报告误报与漏报等误差类型，并重视迁移的站点方向性。

Abstract: Machine learning-based fault detectors are often evaluated at the relay locations used for training, although local voltage and current measurements vary across power networks. This work investigates cross-relay generalization for binary detection of faults anywhere in the network using local measurements, rather than line-selective tripping. Using 50 ms windows from a simulated 110 kV transmission network, we evaluate a multilayer perceptron across all 64 source target combinations of eight relays and five random seeds. A shared simulation-level split and source-only threshold selection provide a controlled held-out evaluation without target-specific calibration. Despite nearly unchanged mean F1-scores (0.730 within-relay versus 0.734 cross-relay), the mean false-positive rate (FPR) increases from 0.088 to 0.341, while the mean false-negative rate decreases from 0.399 to 0.318. The change is strongly directional: models trained at Bus 2 relays yield a mean FPR of 0.670 at Bus 1 and Bus 3 relays, compared with 0.096 for cross-relay transfers within the same bus group. Linear probing indicates that learned representations retain coarse bus-group information, while exploratory class-conditional latent distribution analysis shows no consistent monotonic association with transfer errors. These findings demonstrate that aggregate metrics can conceal location-structured changes in false-alarm behavior, highlighting the importance of evaluating error types separately when assessing cross-relay generalization.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond the Point Mass: Acceptance-Derived Weights and Distributional Losses for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28355)
*Tianyu Wu,Zhuohan Wang,Jonathan Geuter,Yu Yao,Weifan Jiang,Chengxi Zhang,Ju Li,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从投机解码的精确期望接受长度出发，推导出D-PAL损失，用草稿模型与目标分布的重叠（1−TV）作为接受信号，得到基于重叠的位置权重与对数接受（LA）损失；并发现Rényi-1/2散度可替代LA并具有上下界性质，在Qwen3-4B上提升接受长度与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D-PACE以草稿模型对记录目标token的概率对位置加权，交叉熵也仅用该点概率，把目标分布当作点质量；但拒绝采样下草稿token的接受概率实际等于草稿与目标分布的重叠（1−总变差TV），故D-PACE的损失与真实接受长度不匹配。

Method: 从精确期望接受长度推导D-PAL：以重叠作为接受信号，得到重叠式位置权重，并以对数接受（LA）作为逐位置损失；目标为点质量时退化为D-PACE。因权重被detach，任意逐位置损失可替换LA，作者比较前向KL与1/2阶Rényi散度，并证明Rényi-1/2可从上下界真实接受率。

Result: 前向KL与Rényi-1/2匹配或超过LA；将CE替换为Rényi-1/2在D-PACE权重与本文权重下均提升接受长度。六个基准、Qwen3-4B目标模型上，D-PAL将平均接受长度从4.50提升至4.57（T=0），从4.20提升至4.28（T=1），并在两温度下均获得更高吞吐。

Conclusion: 以分布重叠而非点概率刻画接受概率更为准确，D-PAL框架更具理论一致性；Rényi-1/2是实用的逐位置损失，可无条件替换CE并稳定提升投机解码效率。

Abstract: In speculative decoding, a lightweight draft model proposes tokens that the target model verifies in parallel, speeding up generation without changing the target distribution. D-PACE (Wu et al., 2026) trains such drafters with a loss that weights each position by its contribution to the expected accepted length. Both its weights and its cross-entropy (CE) loss use only the draft's probability of the recorded target token, treating the target as a point mass. Under rejection sampling, however, a sampled draft token is accepted with probability equal to the overlap of the draft and target distributions, one minus their total variation (TV). We derive D-PAL (Dynamic Position-wise Acceptance Loss) from the exact expected accepted length, using this overlap as the acceptance signal. This yields overlap-based position weights with log acceptance (LA) as the per-position loss, and the objective reduces to D-PACE for a point-mass target. Since the weights are detached, any per-position loss can replace LA in practice. We show that forward KL divergence and Rényi divergence of order 1/2 match or exceed LA, and that Rényi-1/2 provably bounds the true acceptance rate from above and below. Replacing CE with Rényi-1/2 improves acceptance length under both D-PACE's weights and ours. On six benchmarks with a Qwen3-4B target, D-PAL raises mean acceptance length over D-PACE from 4.50 to 4.57 at T=0 and from 4.20 to 4.28 at T=1, and consistently achieves higher throughput at both temperatures.

</details>


### [109] [Relative Representations Enable Portable White-Box Monitoring Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28366)
*Tim Bakker,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"全局锚定探针"（global anchored construction）：用每个样本对一组共享锚点的角度距离重新表达激活，汇总所有Transformer层信息，从而让一个模型上训练的白盒探针可跨模型迁移，平均保留至少85%的同模型AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的自回归语言模型常习得相似能力，但其内部表征是否以兼容方式组织行为相关信息尚不清楚。若能证明兼容性，即可实现可移植的白盒监控：探针在一个模型上训练验证后可复用于另一模型，无需目标模型的标注数据。难点在于不同模型的激活坐标不兼容。

Method: 1) 用行为探针检验跨模型表征兼容性；2) 将每个样本的激活表示为相对一组共享锚点的角度距离，以规避原生激活坐标不兼容；3) 提出全局锚定构造，汇聚所有Transformer层信息，去除模型特定的层选择，使同一冻结探针可直接应用于另一模型；4) 在常识问答数据集、安全监控和数学推理任务上评估跨模型探针迁移。

Result: 迁移后的全局锚定探针在扣除随机水平后，平均保留至少85%的同模型探针AUROC；锚定探针还支持向相关数据集迁移，对模型规模和提示变化保持稳健，且在模型更新后性能保持优于标准探针。但迁移依赖任务与目标模型，即使被监控属性在目标模型中仍高度可解码，迁移也可能失败。

Conclusion: 全局锚定揭示了独立训练模型间存在显著的任务相关表征兼容性，为可移植白盒监控提供了实用基础；同时表明这种兼容性是任务与目标依赖的，并非普遍成立。

Abstract: Independently trained autoregressive language models often acquire similar capabilities, but it remains unclear whether their internal representations organize behaviorally relevant information in compatible ways. We show that models do in fact organize information similarly by using specific behavioral probes that operate on compatible representations. This unlocks portable white-box monitoring: a probe developed and validated on one model can be reused when the model changes, without requiring labeled target data. To overcome incompatible native activation coordinates, we express each example through its angular distances to a shared set of anchors. We introduce a global anchored construction that summarizes information from all transformer layers and removes model-specific layer selection, so the same frozen probe can be straightforwardly applied to another model. We evaluate cross-model probe transfer across monitoring tasks on trivia datasets, safety monitoring, and mathematical reasoning. On average, transferred global anchored probes preserve at least 85% of same-model probe AUROC over chance. Anchored probes also support transfer to related datasets, remain robust across model scale and prompt changes, and retain performance better than standard probes after model updates. Transfer is nevertheless task- and target-dependent and can fail even when the monitored property remains strongly decodable in the target model. Overall, global anchoring reveals substantial task-relevant compatibility across independently trained models and provides a practical basis for portable white-box monitoring.

</details>


### [110] [Don't Skip the Baseline: A Controlled Comparison of Dynamic Expert Skipping in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28369)
*Jakob Steimle,Thomas Elsken,Daniel Ehrhardt,Lukas Rinder,Daniel Cremers,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一梳理并公平对比了四种MoE动态专家跳过方法,发现它们在与"静态减少专家数k"这一简单基线在匹配专家计算预算下对比时难以稳定胜出,并揭示校准有效k与测试有效k的偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态专家跳过方法(校准重要性分数、专家间相似度、可学习per-token分配器)因校准方式、专家缩减幅度、评测集各不相同而难以比较;同时缺乏与最简单基线(全局静态降低k)的公平对照,因此需要统一分类与匹配算力的受控基准。

Method: 将四种代表性动态跳过方法归入统一分类体系,在匹配专家计算预算下与静态k缩减基线比较;覆盖两个现代MoE(Gemma4-26B-A4B、Qwen3-30B-A3B)的纯文本场景,以及Qwen3-VL的多模态场景,并考察不同校准设置;再分析校准期有效k与测试期有效k的偏差。

Result: 简单静态基线在跨模型、跨模态、跨基准与跨校准设置下难以被稳定超越;动态方法常低于基线,即使有增益也较小;校准中实现或优化的有效k可能与测试有效k不一致,导致推理算力被高估或低估。

Conclusion: 推荐采用免校准、按模态的静态k缩减,并在留出模型与模态上验证;主张任何动态跳过方法都应以匹配实测算力的静态控制作为必须超越的基线。

Abstract: A growing body of work proposes dynamic expert-skipping methods for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models that adjust the number of experts activated per token during inference, aiming to preserve model quality while further reducing the number of active parameters. These methods have added calibrated importance scores, inter-expert similarity metrics, or learned per-token allocators in order to identify experts to skip. However, different calibration regimes, different reductions in the effective number of experts, and evaluation suites make comparisons difficult. We classify four representative prior methods into a unified taxonomy and benchmark them against a natural, simple baseline: statically reducing the number of experts $k$ (i.e., same reduced $k$ across all layers and tokens). We provide a fair comparison of these methods at matched expert-compute budget, on two modern MoEs (Gemma4-26B-A4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B) in a text-only setting and a contemporary vision-language model (Qwen3-VL) in a multimodal setting. Surprisingly, we find that the simple baseline is hard to outperform consistently across models, modalities, benchmarks, and calibration settings. The methods we benchmark often underperform the baseline and achieved gains, when present, are minor. We further find that the effective $k$ achieved (or optimized for) in calibration can diverge from the effective $k$ in testing, resulting in over- or under-estimating the compute at inference time. Based on these findings, we recommend a calibration-free per-modality static $k$ reduction and validate this on a held-out model and modality. We further argue that a static control at matched measured compute is the baseline any dynamic skipping method must beat.

</details>


### [111] [Self-Credit: Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Agentic RL via Intra-group Rescaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28388)
*Fangcong Yin,Ming Yin,Tanvi Bhandarkar,Aashu Singh,Eryk Helenowski,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Saghar Hosseini,Karthik Abinav Sankararaman,Greg Durrett,Dinkar Juyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Self-Credit：一种端到端智能体强化学习方法，利用模型自生成的引用式摘要结合组内重缩放，将稀疏结果奖励转化为稠密信用信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体通常用RLVR训练，但典型RLVR奖励稀疏，导致长时序任务的信用分配困难。

Method: 基于分组式RL，含三部分：(1) rollout时策略模型生成带引用的摘要，指向含关键证据的先前轮次，作为自生成信用；(2) 组内重缩放，比较同一prompt下成功与失败rollout中被引用的动作，用组级一致/不一致去除不应得的信用并强调关键动作信用；(3) 优势重加权，用重缩放后信用权重更新被引用轮次的优势。

Result: BrowseComp-Plus上，Qwen3 8B与32B分别较基座提升37.0和36.2点，较最强智能体RL基线超出8.5和5.6点；GAIA提升4.9点，xBench-DeepSearch提升4.7点。

Conclusion: 自生成信用可为选取关键动作提供有效信号，组内重缩放（尤其在失败rollout上）在各模型规模下均重要。

Abstract: LLM agents are commonly trained with RLVR, but the sparse nature of typical RLVR rewards makes credit assignment challenging, especially for long-horizon tasks. We propose Self-Credit, an end-to-end agentic RL method that creates dense supervision signals from sparse outcome rewards using self-generated credit assignment via intra-group rescaling. Self-Credit builds on group-based RL with three components. First, during its rollouts, the policy model generates summaries with citations to earlier turns containing critical evidence, which serve as self-generated credit. Second, we refine this model-generated credit with intra-group rescaling, which compares cited actions across successful and failed rollouts for the same prompt. This approach uses group-level agreement and disagreement to remove undeserved credit and further emphasize credit assigned to critical actions. Third, advantage reweighting uses the rescaled credit weights to update the advantages of the cited turns. On BrowseComp-Plus, Self-Credit improves over the base models by 37.0 and 36.2 points on Qwen3 8B and 32B respectively, and outperforms the strongest agentic RL baselines by 8.5 and 5.6 points, respectively. Self-Credit also improves over the strongest baselines by 4.9 points on GAIA and 4.7 points on xBench-DeepSearch. Analyses show that self-generated credit provides useful signals for selecting critical actions, and that intra-group rescaling, especially on failed rollouts, is important across model scales.

</details>


### [112] [Rewards for Budget-Aware Multi-Turn Agents That Adapt to Arbitrary Test-Time Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28409)
*Tom A. Lamb,Adam Golinski,Eleonora Gualdoni,Sinead Williamson,Michael Kirchhof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨多轮LLM智能体能否在动态设定的预算下自主学习资源分配，并比较十一种奖励目标；结果表明，对超支施加惩罚的奖励可显著提升预算内成功率与"自然合规性"，且能泛化到未见预算、分布偏移任务与新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体需在多次环境交互中分配推理预算，外部框架虽可强制约束，但智能体本身能否学会"何时花、花多少"尚不清楚；同时何种奖励最能教会这一行为也缺乏系统比较。

Method: 将现有的长度惩罚推广到涵盖多种资源类型的预算约束，提出新的奖励公式，并在统一训练流程中比较十一种目标。在 AppWorld 上以 token、API 调用、环境步数为预算训练 Qwen3.5 4B/9B，在 BabyAI 网格世界上以回合预算训练；每个环境与资源类型的策略在多个预算值上联合训练。

Result: 惩罚超支的奖励相对未训练模型和仅优化任务成功率的训练，显著提升预算内成功率与自然合规性；智能体学会根据给定预算与任务难度调整资源使用，收益可泛化到未见预算，并零样本迁移到偏移任务分布和新基准。sigmoid 超支惩罚在单个预算下通常取得最高预算内成功率，固定负超支奖励自然合规性最强，组合超支频率与幅度惩罚在各预算下与最优方法的平均差距最小。

Conclusion: 单一智能体可借预算反馈学习跨回合资源分配并适配用户指定预算，无需外部预算分配控制器。

Abstract: Large language model agents are often given an inference budget and asked to maximise success within it. A multi-turn agent has to allocate this budget across many environment interactions. An external harness can enforce the budget, but it remains unclear whether the agent itself can learn when and how much to spend. We ask whether agents can learn this allocation across dynamically specified budgets and which rewards best teach this behaviour. We adapt existing length penalties to budget constraints spanning multiple resource types, introduce new reward formulations, and compare eleven objectives in a shared training pipeline. We train Qwen3.5 models at 4B and 9B on AppWorld with budgets over tokens, API calls, and environment steps, and on the BabyAI gridworld with turn budgets. We train a separate policy for each environment and resource type, with each policy trained across budget values. Training with rewards that penalise overspending substantially improves within-budget success and natural compliance over untrained models and over training on task success alone, with agents learning to adapt their resource use to stated budgets and task difficulty. These gains extend to unseen budgets and, zero-shot, to a shifted task distribution and a new benchmark. Reward objectives trade off success within budget and natural compliance. A sigmoid penalty for overspending most often achieves the highest within-budget success at an individual budget, while a fixed negative reward for overspending generally yields the strongest natural compliance. Combining penalties for overspend frequency and magnitude gives the smallest average gap to the best method at each budget. Overall, we show that a single agent can learn to allocate resources across turns and adapt its behaviour to user-specified budgets using budget feedback without external budget-allocation controllers.

</details>


### [113] [Region-Specific EEG Classification of Hypnotic Susceptibility Using a Sliding-Window 2D CNN: A Subject-Independent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28427)
*Krzysztof Wrobel,Grzegorz M. Wojcik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验基于极简预处理的EEG信号训练的2D CNN能否按脑区分类催眠易感性，发现随机划分下的高准确率主要源于被试特异性指纹，仅额区与前额区在LOSO下保持高于随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探明2D CNN能否利用最小预处理的EEG信号在区分脑区的条件下判别催眠易感性，并厘清既往高分类准确率是否反映真实的组间差异。

Method: 62名男性被试（对照32、引导意象/催眠组30），256导EGI帽采集20分钟EEG；以滑动窗口方式对7个预定义脑区（前额、额、颞、中央、顶、枕、面部）内的全部10秒窗口建模，最多训练868个独立模型；先以片段级90/10随机划分筛选，再以留一被试交叉验证（LOSO）进行被试独立评估。

Result: 随机划分下5个脑区超过0.67准确率阈值，枕区最高（均值0.721，覆盖90.3%窗口）；LOSO下枕区降至近随机水平（ACC 0.453，AUC 0.429），而额区与前额区分别保持0.646与0.624的ACC，高于随机水平。

Conclusion: EEG分类研究必须采用被试独立验证；随机划分的高准确率反映被试特异性指纹而非组间差异，前部皮层区域是催眠易感性更可靠的神经标记。

Abstract: We investigate whether a 2D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained on minimally preprocessed EEG signals can classify hypnotic susceptibility across distinct brain regions. Sixty-two male participants (32 Control, 30 Guided Imagery / Hipno group) underwent a 20-minute EEG recording using a 256-channel EGI cap. A sliding-window approach evaluates all 10-second windows within the recording for each of seven predefined brain regions (prefrontal, frontal, temporal, central, parietal, occipital, and facial), yielding up to 868 independently trained models. Initial screening with a segment-level 90/10 random split showed that five regions exceeded the 0.67 accuracy threshold, with the occipital region achieving the highest mean accuracy (0.721) across 90.3% of windows. Subject-independent evaluation via Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) cross-validation, however, substantially revised this picture: occipital performance collapsed to near-chance (mean ACC 0.453, AUC 0.429), revealing that high random-split accuracy was driven by subject-specific EEG fingerprints rather than genuine group differences. In contrast, the frontal and prefrontal regions maintained above-chance LOSO accuracy (0.646 and 0.624, respectively), demonstrating genuine generalisability. These results underscore the necessity of subject-independent validation in EEG-based classification studies and highlight anterior cortical regions as more reliable neural markers of hypnotic susceptibility.

</details>


### [114] [Do Neural Models Prefer Their Submodels?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28439)
*Alvaro Ribot,Yulia Alexandr,Anna Seigal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究神经模型（给定网络架构可表达的函数集合）的几何性质，提出一个几何框架来判断损失函数的局部极小值是否偏好位于某些子模型（如将部分神经元置为不活跃所得的子网络）之中；结论是除少数边界例外，子模型偏好无法由神经模型几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经模型中存在自然的子模型（例如令部分神经元失活所得到的子网络）。既有研究常观察到训练后网络呈现“子网络化”或稀疏结构，作者希望厘清这一现象是否源自神经模型本身的几何结构，即局部极小值是否在几何上倾向于落在特定子模型内，还是另有来源（如训练动力学）。

Method: 构造一个几何分析框架，用于判定损失函数的局部极小值是否落在给定子模型内（即“子模型偏好”）。在该框架下分别分析：浅层多层感知机（MLP）、深层MLP（给出不偏好的充分条件）、以及lightning自注意力（分析低秩子模型）。核心是比较子模型所在流形的局部几何（切空间、边界、维度）与损失函数的局部极小条件。

Result: 对浅层MLP证明：含失活隐藏神经元的子网络永远不会被偏好。对深层MLP给出子网络（隐藏神经元失活）不被偏好的条件。对lightning自注意力：低秩子模型通常不被偏好，但识别出因位于模型边界上而被偏好的反例。

Conclusion: 除少数（边界）例外，对子模型的偏好无法用神经模型的几何来解释，而必须来自其他来源，例如训练动力学。

Abstract: A neural model is the set of functions that can be expressed by a given neural network architecture. Neural models contain natural submodels, such as those obtained by setting some neurons to be inactive. We study the geometry of neural models and ask whether they prefer their submodels. By this we mean whether local minimizers of the loss function lie in a given submodel. We introduce a geometric framework to determine if this occurs. For shallow multilayer perceptrons, we show that subnetworks with inactive hidden neurons are never preferred. For deep multilayer perceptrons, we give conditions under which subnetworks with inactive hidden neurons are not preferred. For lightning self-attention, lower-rank submodels are usually not preferred, but we identify examples where they are because they lie on a boundary of the model. Our results show that, aside from a few exceptions, preference for submodels cannot be explained by the geometry of the neural model, and must instead come from other sources such as training dynamics.

</details>


### [115] [CNN-based classifier for subject-independent MBTI Extraversion/Introversion preference classification from short-time EEG signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28442)
*Filip Postepski,Grzegorz M. Wojcik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验短时、最少预处理的EEG片段是否足以区分MBTI外向/内向个体。将50名被试EEG切分为4秒片段，采用被试独立的嵌套5折交叉验证，比较EEGNet、EEGProgress与自提出的紧凑卷积网络SSCNet。SSCNet取得最高平均准确率0.6481、AUC 0.7063、F1 0.6276，且折间波动最小、计算效率最高（最短中位训练轮时间与最高吞吐）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人格维度（MBTI内外向偏好）能否从原始时域EEG中解码，避免人工频谱特征工程；同时强调被试独立验证的必要性与模型计算效率的重要性。

Method: 50名被试EEG划分为4秒片段，端到端时域输入、最少预处理；采用被试独立的嵌套5折交叉验证防止数据泄漏；对比EEGNet、EEGProgress与提出的紧凑卷积架构SSCNet，指标含准确率、AUC、F1、折间变异及训练时间/吞吐。

Result: SSCNet平均准确率0.6481、AUC 0.7063、F1 0.6276，均为三者最高；折间方差最小；可训练参数量与EEGProgress相近但中位单轮训练时间最短、吞吐最高。

Conclusion: MBTI内外向偏好可从短时域EEG片段中获得中等程度的可预测性，无需手工频谱特征；被试独立验证与轻量化高效模型设计对该类人格解码研究至关重要。

Abstract: This study examines whether short, minimally preprocessed EEG segments contain sufficient information to distinguish between individuals classified as extraverted or introverted according to the Myers--Briggs Type Indicator (MBTI). EEG recordings from 50 participants were divided into 4-s segments and analyzed using a subject-independent nested 5-fold cross-validation procedure to compare EEGNet, EEGProgress, and a newly proposed compact convolutional architecture, SSCNet. SSCNet achieved the highest mean accuracy (0.6481), AUC (0.7063), and F1-score (0.6276). The proposed architecture also showed the lowest variability across folds. SSCNet demonstrated the best computational efficiency, with the shortest median training epoch time and the highest training throughput despite having a similar number of trainable parameters to EEGProgress. The results suggest that MBTI Extraversion--Introversion preferences may be moderately predictable from short time-domain EEG segments without manual spectral feature extraction, while highlighting the importance of subject-independent validation and computationally efficient model design.

</details>


### [116] [LocaLeJEPA: Maximum Entropy Representations under Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28458)
*Kartik Tandon,Alessio Borgi,Pavel Snopov,Inés García-Redondo,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano,Fabrizio Silvestri,Pietro Liò,Francesca Dominici,Randall Balestriero,Claudio Battiloro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LocaLeJEPA：基于流形视角重构 LeJEPA 的熵最大化目标，提出局部欧氏分布匹配正则化器 LocaLeReg，在多种（含双曲与等变）骨干上稳定训练并超越 LeJEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 类自监督方法易发生表示坍缩；LeJEPA 通过将嵌入推向各向同性高斯来缓解，但其目标建立在欧氏假设之上，且全局目标无法体现领域特定的几何先验（如等变 CNN、双曲 ViT 等非欧嵌入几何）。

Method: 从流形理论角度重新表述 LeJEPA 的熵最大化性质，提出 LocaLeJEPA 框架；并设计 LocaLeReg——一种局部欧氏分布匹配正则化器，实现该框架的稳定、可扩展训练。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 训练，于双曲 ViT-S/8 与等变 ResNet-50 骨干上显著优于 LeJEPA；在普通欧氏 ViT-S/8 与 ViT-L/14 骨干上同样取得提升；在 ImageNet 线性与 k-NN 分类及多项下游任务上均一致优于 LeJEPA。

Conclusion: LocaLeJEPA 是一种更尊重数据内在几何的 JEPA 范式原则性方法，并可自然推广到非欧等异域嵌入几何。代码已开源。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer an appealing approach to self-supervised learning, but are typically prone to representation collapse. LeJEPA resolves this by introducing a theoretically grounded distribution-matching objective that drives the embedding towards an isotropic Gaussian. However, this approach is fundamentally tied to Euclidean assumptions, and its global target does not reflect domain-specific geometric priors, such as the use of non-Euclidean embedding geometries, e.g., equivariant CNNs or hyperbolic ViTs. We thus introduce LocaLeJEPA, a framework motivated by a manifold-theoretic reformulation of LeJEPA's entropy-maximization property. The accompanying LocaLeReg, a novel locally Euclidean distribution-matching regularizer, enables a stable and scalable implementation of the LocaLeJEPA framework. We then perform extensive experiments in the vision setting on models trained on ImageNet-1K. LocaLeJEPA significantly outperforms LeJEPA when paired with hyperbolic ViT-S/8 and equivariant ResNet-50 backbones, and also with ordinary Euclidean ViT-S/8 and ViT-L/14 backbones. This holds both in terms of linear and k-NN classification performance on ImageNet and on a series of downstream tasks tested. We view the LocaLeJEPA formalism as a principled approach to the JEPA paradigm that better respects intrinsic data geometry while also accommodating generalizations to exotic embedding geometries. Code available at https://github.com/alessioborgi/LocaLeJEPA.

</details>


### [117] [Geometry-Agnostic Data-Driven Discovery of Extreme Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28487)
*Angeline Hu,Ava K. Tse,Kaitlyn S. Yasumura,Luciana Sarabia,Malcolm Yeung,Kavin K. Prasanna,Kai Wong,William Hammond,Ioannis G. Keverkidis,Shengkai Li,Trung V. Phan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段数据驱动神经框架：先从观测数据推断有效动力学约束，再用物理信息神经搜索发现罕见高影响的极端/相干结构，并实现跨几何、跨维度的动力学迁移与识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上发现极端或相干结构（如同宿脉冲、孤子、瞬子）依赖已知控制方程与先验假设。作者希望在不预设控制方程的前提下，仅从数据推断动力学并搜索未见过的极端情形。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段从数据中学习有效动力学约束；当系统具备对称性时，采用与几何无关的神经表示，使学习到的动力学不依赖固定网格、坐标系、维数和内禀曲率。第二阶段用物理信息神经搜索，基于所学动力学搜索罕见、高影响、观测中未出现的相干结构。以Duffing动力学、非线性薛定谔（驻波孤子）、Townes孤子及Liouville系统（从(1+1)维Lorentzian准流形迁移到(2+0)维Euclidean流形识别瞬子）为例验证。

Result: 从Duffing动力学中找到同宿脉冲作为局部极端情景；经由非线性薛定谔等价性将其解释为驻波孤子；用几何无关表示将动力学提升到高维得到Townes孤子；Liouville系统上实现从(1+1)维Lorentzian到(2+0)维Euclidean的动力学迁移并识别瞬子。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广到kink、畸形波、compacton、peakon乃至奇点等相干时空结构，应用于风险分析、非线性预测与异常检测。

Abstract: We introduce a two-stage data-driven neural framework for discovering dynamically admissible extreme scenarios. Rather than assuming prior knowledge of the governing equations, the first stage infers the effective dynamical constraints directly from observational data. When the underlying physical system possesses suitable symmetries, the learned dynamics can be represented in a geometry-agnostic form, independent of any fixed grid, coordinate system, dimensionality, and intrinsic curvature. In the second stage, a physics-informed neural-search uses this learned dynamics to uncover rare, high-impact coherent structures that may not have appeared in the original observations. We demonstrate this framework through representative examples of increasing dimensionality and geometric complexity. Starting from a traditionally learned Duffing dynamics, we find a homoclinic pulse as a plausible localized extreme scenario. Next, since the same equation also appears in the stationary nonlinear Schrodinger system, with the coordinate interpreted as space instead of time, this pulse becomes a standing soliton. We then retrain the first stage using a geometry-agnostic neural representation, enabling the learned dynamics to be lifted to higher-dimensional space to obtain higher-dimensional solitary profile, known as Townes soliton. Finally, with a Liouville system, we demonstrate that dynamics learned from data on a (1+1)-dimensional Lorentzian quasi-manifold can be transferred to a (2+0)-dimensional Euclidean manifold to identify an instanton. Our approach can be applied to other coherent spatiotemporal structures, including kinks, rogue waves, compactons, peakons, and potentially even singularities, with applications in risk analysis, nonlinear forecasting, and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [118] [Model- and Layer-Level Closed-Loop Forecasting with SSM-Based Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28495)
*Erik Stolt,Sanja Karilanova,Ayca Ozcelikkale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究脉冲神经网络（SNN，作为一类深度状态空间模型）如何做时间序列预测，提出模型级与层级的两种闭环（自回归反馈）预测策略，在ETT-small基准上验证其效果可与S4零掩码及SpaceTime相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep SSM在含复杂时序依赖的时间序列上表现良好且计算高效，SNN以离散脉冲事件通信是其生物启发变体；但预测任务需用网络自身输出替代不可得的未来输入，标准自回归在脉冲框架下如何实现仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出两种闭环预测策略：(i) 模型级闭环——将网络整体输出的预测值反馈为下一时刻输入；(ii) 层级闭环——在各层内部将本层生成值反馈为输入。在ETT-small数据集上，分别以脉冲激活与连续激活评估，并与SpaceTime及S4的zero-masking基线对比。

Result: 两种闭环方法均与S4 zero-masking相当或更优，多数情况下可与SpaceTime匹配；结果揭示了连续激活与低比特量化激活之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提Deep SSM架构为时间序列预测提供了有竞争力的框架，闭环反馈可有效解决SNN自回归预测中输入缺失问题。

Abstract: Deep State-Space Models (Deep SSMs) have shown promising performance and computational benefits in processing of time-series data with complex temporal dependencies. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) constitute a biologically inspired class of deep SSMs, where computing blocks communicate through spikes, discrete single-bit events rather than continuous activation functions. A particularly important task in time-series processing is forecasting, i.e. predicting future values of a sequence from past observations alone. However, how to best perform forecasting with SNNs is an open question, where the standard autoregressive nature of the data processing with SNNs requires values that are not available during the forecasting phase. In this article, we address this challenge. We explore two closed-loop forecasting strategies, in which the network's own generated values are fed back into the network as if they were observed past values: (i) model-level and (ii) layer-level closed-loop forecasting. We evaluate these approaches on the ETT-small datasets, a standard benchmark for time-series forecasting, under both spike-based and continuous activation functions. We compare the proposed closed-loop approaches against state-of-the-art methods such as SpaceTime, as well as the zero-masking baseline in S4. Both of the proposed approaches perform comparably to or better than zero-masking with S4, and in most cases match SpaceTime. These findings highlight the trade-off between continuous and low-bit quantized activations, and demonstrate that the proposed Deep SSM architectures provide a competitive framework for forecasting.

</details>


### [119] [Present but Hard to Read: Probing Latent Reasoning with Minimal Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28498)
*Aur Shalev Merin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对Ouro-1.4B与Qwen2.5-1.5B的探查实验表明：输入中复制的数字在最后提示词位置以跨题目共享的编码存在（可读性约0.9），但逐条提示的常规探查几乎读不出（0.36/0.19）；因此"探查失败"不等于"值缺失"，且成功探查也存在歧义；两步问题中未写出的中间量a+b仅有微弱信号且大部分符号可被混合效应解释，将其激活替换（patch）到另一题并不能改变输出，说明"可读但未被使用"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜推理（latent reasoning）的探查研究常只读"答案起始处"的模型状态，并把探查失败当作某数值不存在的证据。作者质疑这一推断逻辑的可靠性，需要对"探查失败/成功"的解释给出更严格的检验。

Method: 在循环模型Ouro-1.4B与标准Transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B上，以仅某一位数字不同的提示对作对照，检验该数字是否编码于最后提示词位置；比较逐条提示探查与跨题目共享编码的可读性；用随机噪声方向与随机替换词作对照，区分"变化小"与"被挤占"；并提出一套协议（含用于泛化混合的合成零假设）以区分"计算得到的值"与"输入副本及输入混合"；在两步问题上进行预注册研究，并对中间量a+b做激活替换实验。

Result: 该数字在最后提示词位置存在且编码跨题目共享，readout约0.9；而逐提示探查仅0.36（Ouro）与0.19（Qwen），即使训练5,249道题后仍如此。数字变化幅度小而并非被挤占：随机噪声落在同样"繁忙"的方向上，而原位替换为随机词使状态移动更大且可读。预注册两步问题研究在Ouro写出答案末位数字前发现a+b的微弱信号，其符号随运算类型变化，但其中相当部分可由纯混合产生。把该值自第一轮循环起替换到另一题，并不使写出的数字偏向另一题；而对答案位置的替换则有效。

Conclusion: "探查失败"不能作为数值缺失的证据，因为常规的逐提示探查设定会系统性低估共享编码；同时成功探查也不具决定性，因为任意变动token都会留下痕迹。应使用包含合成零假设的协议，将计算值与输入副本/混合区分开。实验中中间量a+b"可读但未被使用"，提示可读性不等于因果作用，需以替换实验判定功能使用。

Abstract: Probing studies of latent reasoning often read a model's state where the answer begins and take a failed probe as evidence that a value is absent. We test this with a digit copied from the question, in the looped model Ouro-1.4B and the standard transformer Qwen2.5-1.5B. Comparing prompts that differ only in that digit shows it is present at the last prompt token, in a code shared across questions (readout about 0.9). A probe that sees one prompt at a time, the usual setting, barely reads it: 0.36 in Ouro and 0.19 in Qwen even after 5,249 training questions. The digit's change is small rather than crowded: random noise lands on equally busy directions, while a random word in its place moves the state far more and is readable. Since every varied token leaves a trace, a successful probe is ambiguous too. We give a protocol, including a synthetic null for generic mixing, for testing computed values against copies and mixtures of the inputs. In pre-registered studies of two-step problems it finds a small signal for the unwritten intermediate a+b just before Ouro writes the answer's last digit, and its sign follows the operation, though mixing alone produces much of that sign-following part. Holding that value at another problem's from the first loop on does not move the written digit toward the other problem's, while the same patch of the answer does: the value is readable but not used.

</details>


### [120] [ECG for laboratory values:A comprehensive physiological and technical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28507)
*Walentin Palanchuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究从ECG记录与患者元数据预测NT-proBNP，采用多模态深度学习框架，比较1导联（II导联）与12导联配置，并在附录中扩展到血糖、血红蛋白和钾。12导联一致更优；精确回归仍困难，但较宽浓度区间分类更可行，ECG预测可提供互补信息，但不能替代实验室检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室检测对临床决策至关重要，但通常需要侵入性采血。近期研究表明ECG可能蕴含超越心律的信息，与实验室指标相关，因此探索无创的ECG-based生物标志物预测具有临床价值，同时需考察其生理学合理性。

Method: 基于MIMIC-IV数据，构建融合ECG信号与患者元数据的多模态深度学习框架，用于NT-proBNP预测；开展回归、二分类及基于临床既定阈值的三分类实验；对比1导联（II导联）与12导联配置；附录中补充血糖、血红蛋白和钾的实验。

Result: 12导联配置一致优于1导联：回归MAE为514.27 vs 534.98 pg/mL；二分类AUROC为0.876 vs 0.849；三分类宏平均AUROC为0.752 vs 0.738。结果显示精确NT-proBNP回归仍具挑战，而较宽浓度区间的分类效果更好。

Conclusion: 结果与“ECG特征与NT-proBNP浓度反映共同心血管过程”的生理学假设一致；ECG-based NT-proBNP预测可提供互补信息，尤其适用于较宽浓度区间分类；但实验室检测仍不可替代，且需在独立数据集上验证。

Abstract: Laboratory measurements are essential for clinical decision making but typically require invasive blood sampling. Recent research suggests that ECG recordings may contain information beyond cardiac rhythm and provide information related to laboratory values. This thesis investigates NT-proBNP prediction from ECG recordings and patient metadata using a multimodal deep learning framework, while considering the physiological plausibility of ECG-based prediction. It also compares a 1-lead configuration using lead II with the corresponding 12-lead configuration. Additional experiments on glucose, hemoglobin, and potassium are presented in the appendix.
  Regression, binary classification, and three-class classification experiments were conducted using MIMIC-IV, with the three-class task based on clinically established thresholds. The 12-lead configuration consistently achieved better performance. Regression reached a mean absolute error of 534.98 pg/mL for 1-lead and 514.27 pg/mL for 12-lead. Binary classification achieved AUROCs of 0.849 and 0.876, respectively, while three-class classification achieved macro-averaged AUROCs of 0.738 and 0.752.
  The results indicate that precise NT-proBNP regression remains challenging, whereas broader concentration ranges can be classified more effectively. The findings are consistent with the physiological hypothesis that ECG characteristics and NT-proBNP concentrations reflect shared cardiovascular processes. ECG-based NT-proBNP prediction may therefore provide complementary information, particularly for classifying broader concentration ranges. However, laboratory measurements remain necessary, and validation on independent datasets is required.

</details>


### [121] [GraphCast-Mamba: Learning Non-Markovian Corrections for Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28512)
*Ilya Vilkoviskiy,Liang-Hong Mo,Suozhi Huang,Dmitry A. Abanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphCast–Mamba 在冻结的 1° GraphCast 主干上附加可训练的 Mamba 修正分支，通过跨六个小时预报步保留时间记忆状态，仅用少量训练即显著降低十天预报误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多天气预报模型只使用很短的输入窗口，未解析的大气过程会带来"历史依赖性"，即预报需要超出两帧输入的时间上下文。作者希望在不重训主干的前提下引入更长时序记忆。

Method: 以冻结的预训练 1° GraphCast 为主干，新增可训练的修正分支，该分支结合图消息传递与 Mamba 层；分支在连续六个小时预报步之间维持时间记忆状态，学习由 GraphCast 两帧输入之外的时间上下文引导的修正量。训练仅限修正分支，使用 2015–2021 年 ERA5 数据，含预热共 2,500 次优化器更新。

Result: 相对未修正主干，GraphCast 损失（归一化加权 MSE）在十天总体聚合上降低 14.42%，第 10 天（240 h）降低 21.66%；在第 10 天，全部 11 个评估变量组（温度、风、气压、位势、比湿、降水）的 RMSE 下降 7.09%–14.60%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过短周期的修正训练并保留预训练主干，即可在十天天气预报上获得广泛提升，说明引入时间记忆的轻量修正分支是低成本且有效的后处理式改进途径。

Abstract: Unresolved atmospheric processes can introduce history dependence beyond the short input windows used by many weather forecasting models. We introduce GraphCast--Mamba, which augments a frozen, pretrained $1^\circ$ GraphCast backbone with a trainable Mamba correction branch combining graph message passing and Mamba layers. The branch retains a temporal memory state across successive six-hour forecast steps, learning corrections informed by temporal context beyond GraphCast's two-frame input. Training is confined to the correction branch, using ERA5 data from 2015--2021. After 2,500 optimizer updates, including warm-up, the selected model achieves substantial improvements relative to the uncorrected GraphCast backbone. It reduces the GraphCast loss (normalized weighted mean-squared error; MSE) by 14.42% when aggregated over the full ten-day forecast and by 21.66% at day 10 (240 h after forecast initialization). At this final forecast lead time, root-mean-square error (RMSE) decreases by 7.09--14.60% across all 11 evaluated variable groups, spanning temperature, wind, pressure, geopotential, specific humidity, and precipitation. These results demonstrate broad improvements in ten-day weather forecasts through a short correction-training schedule while retaining the pretrained backbone.

</details>


### [122] [Binary Discrete Diffusion: Candidate-Set Diffusion for Logic and Planning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28543)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Dimitri von Rütte,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Binary Discrete Diffusion：以二进制成员向量表示每个位置的候选token集合，通过增删操作实现逐步损失与恢复的连续时间离散扩散过程，在推理/规划类任务上优于掩码与均匀扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散与均匀扩散在单次加噪事件中即抹去token身份，其噪声状态无法显式表达"多个备选仍合理"的部分不确定性，限制了迭代推理能力。

Method: 构建一类连续时间离散扩散过程，噪声状态为每位置候选token集合（二进制成员向量）；加噪核通过候选的加入/移除改变集合，使信息可逐步丢失与恢复，扩散状态本身即编码部分不确定性；采用对称（symmetric）形式化。

Result: 在Sudoku-Extreme、Boxoban与图着色任务上，对称二元扩散在架构匹配及受控的训练/采样/调参预算下稳定优于调优良好的掩码与均匀基线，最难实例增益最大；在Text8、TinyStories、OpenWebText语言建模上保持竞争力。JS散度（JSD）低于基线。

Conclusion: 候选集合可作为迭代推理的有效中间表示；加噪核（corruption kernel）会实质性地改变逆模型所学到的计算。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models iteratively denoise a noisy sequence of categorical data such as text, enabling any-order generation with self-correction and in-filling capabilities. This makes them promising candidates for reasoning, planning, and constrained-generation tasks like coding and math. Yet masked and uniform diffusion erase a token's identity in a single corruption event, so their noisy states cannot explicitly represent partially resolved uncertainty in which several alternatives remain plausible. We close this gap with Binary Discrete Diffusion, a family of continuous-time discrete-diffusion processes whose noisy state is a set of candidate tokens per position, represented as a binary membership vector. Binary diffusion changes candidate sets through additions and removals, allowing information to be lost and recovered gradually while the diffusion state itself represents partial uncertainty. On Sudoku-Extreme, Boxoban, and graph coloring, the symmetric formulation of binary diffusion consistently outperforms strong, well-tuned masked and uniform baselines under matched architectures and controlled training, sampling, and tuning budgets, with its largest gains on the hardest instances, while remaining competitive on language modeling (Text8, TinyStories, OpenWebText). These results support candidate sets as an effective intermediate representation for iterative reasoning and show that the corruption kernel can materially change the computation learned by the reverse model.

</details>


### [123] [Discount Yourself: Self-inclusive Freshness-weighted Gossip for Decentralised Learning under Client Outages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28545)
*Qiyuan Wang,Ke Xiao,Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对去中心化联邦学习中客户端长时间离线后仅能聚合邻居模型的问题，论文形式化"离线后聚合"为全可用网络状态估计，提出三条原则（接收方须同样按陈旧度折扣、陈旧度应以继承滞后而非年龄度量、折扣须陡峭），据此提出SFG聚合规则族，在图像基准与真实手机可用性轨迹上优于DFL与AoI规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实设备常离线数小时至数天，回归时只能依靠邻居持有的模型恢复；其中最陈旧的往往是客户端自身模型，而已有的陈旧感知规则仅折扣接收到的模型，未处理该情形。

Method: 将离线后聚合形式化为估计完全可用网络的状态，导出三条原则：一是接收方须像邻居一样被折扣（否则误差随离线时长无界增长，按相对陈旧度折扣则误差有界）；二是以"继承滞后"而非"年龄"度量陈旧度，提出有效滞后（每消息一个标量开销）；三是折扣须陡峭。由此构造SFG（自包含新鲜度加权Gossip）规则族，含SFG-L与SFG-Cut等成员，通信与计算同DFL平均。

Result: 在三个图像基准、八种离线过程（含真实智能手机可用性轨迹）上，基于滞后的SFG-L与SFG-Cut一致优于去中心化联邦平均与近期AoI规则；消融实验证明三条原则均必要，并解释了gossip learning中受限合并（limited merging）为何有效。

Conclusion: 离线恢复的关键在于把接收方自身纳入陈旧度折扣，并以继承滞后作为度量；SFG在保持DFL级开销的同时提供了含陈旧接收方在内的理论保证和实证收益。

Abstract: Decentralised federated learning (DFL) trains personalised models on devices that average with their neighbours rather than with a central server. Real devices, however, are often offline for hours or days, and a client returning from an outage can recover only by aggregating the models its neighbourhood holds. We observe that the stalest of these models is usually the client's own, a case that existing staleness-aware rules, which discount only the models a client receives, do not address. We formalise aggregation after an outage as estimating the state of a fully available network and derive three principles. First, the receiver must be discounted like any neighbour. Every rule that weights it at least as much as each neighbour has an error that grows without bound with the outage, whereas discounting all candidates by relative staleness keeps the error bounded. Second, staleness must be measured as inherited lag rather than age, since local training resets a model's age but not the lag it absorbed from stale neighbours. We introduce the effective lag, which tracks this quantity at the cost of one scalar per message. Third, the discount must be steep, because absorbed lag persists in every later round. These principles define Self-inclusive Freshness-weighted Gossip (SFG), a family of drop-in aggregation rules with the communication and computation of decentralised federated averaging, whose guarantees hold for the stale receiver itself. On three image benchmarks and eight outage processes, including real smartphone availability traces, the lag-based members SFG-L and SFG-Cut consistently outperform decentralised federated averaging and recent age-of-information rules, and ablations show that each principle is necessary. The analysis also explains why tuned limited merging from gossip learning is effective.

</details>


### [124] [Learning Computational Structure for Sample-Efficient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28555)
*Hangyu Zhou,Constantine Dovrolis,Stephen Mussmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Learned Wiring (LeWi)：为每个神经元学习低维嵌入，仅邻近神经元相连，嵌入与权重联合训练，从而在高维组合稀疏目标上提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维统计学习受维度灾难困扰；许多实用函数具有组合稀疏性（由少数变量组成的局部函数复合而成），样本复杂度可依赖局部元数而非环境维度。但实现该收益需利用隐藏的组合结构，而既有理论无法保证基于梯度的实际训练能做到这一点。

Method: 构建CSFRBench受控基准：训练时隐藏目标的组合计算图、诊断时保留。受生物神经回路空间组织启发，提出LeWi——每个神经元具有可学习的低维嵌入，只有空间邻近的神经元才连接，嵌入与权重联合优化。

Result: 稠密网络与基于稀疏性的基线未能兑现该收益。LeWi在具有层级结构、中间复用或独立模块的目标上提升样本效率；在额外32个由独立采样计算图生成的目标上，LeWi是未获得目标图的最高效方法，学习曲线面积均值比稠密网络低27%；稠密网络仅在单一高元数函数目标上更优。LeWi的表示与连接性与目标计算对齐。在控制、导航、操作的17个世界模型预测任务中，LeWi在16个上优于稠密基线，119次对比中胜99次。

Conclusion: 联合学习连接性与权重可利用计算结构提升样本效率。

Abstract: Statistical learning in high dimensions suffers from the curse of dimensionality. Fortunately, many functions of practical interest are compositionally sparse, meaning they admit a composition of local functions, each depending on only a few variables, allowing sample complexity to depend on local arity rather than ambient dimension. Yet realizing this benefit requires exploiting hidden compositional structure, which theory does not establish for practical gradient-based training. We study this gap with CSFRBench, a controlled benchmark that hides the target's compositional computation during training but retains it for diagnosis, and find that dense networks and sparsity-based baselines leave much of that benefit unrealized. Inspired by the spatial organization of biological neural circuits, we introduce Learned Wiring (LeWi): each neuron has a learned low-dimensional embedding, only nearby neurons connect, and embeddings and weights are trained jointly. LeWi improves sample efficiency on targets with hierarchical structure, intermediate reuse, or independent modules. Across 32 additional targets generated by independently sampled computation graphs, LeWi is the most sample-efficient method not given the target graph, with 27% lower mean learning-curve area than dense networks. Dense networks perform better on targets with a single high-arity function. LeWi's representations and connectivity align with the target computation. On 17 world-model prediction tasks spanning control, navigation, and manipulation, LeWi beats the dense baseline on 16 and wins 99/119 baseline comparisons. These results show that jointly learning connectivity and weights can exploit computational structure to improve sample efficiency.

</details>


### [125] [When Do LLMs Help with Feature Selection? A Systematic Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28556)
*Fateme Memar,Dongjie Wang,Arvin Agah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在18个数据集的统一框架下（10个GRPO训练、2个checkpoint验证、6个留存评估）比较经典统计特征选择器、冻结的Qwen2.5-7B/Llama-3.3-70B选择器和两个GRPO适配的Qwen2.5-7B模型，发现经典选择器在预测性能上通常优于所测LLM/RL选择器，而语义特征名对零样本Qwen-7B的增益在回归上明显、在分类上较小且依赖数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以结合语义特征名与汇总统计进行表格特征选择，但相对于成熟统计方法，语义信息在何种条件下真正改善子集选择仍不明确，且缺乏系统性、可比的实证证据。

Method: 构建18数据集框架（10个用于GRPO训练、2个用于checkpoint验证、6个留存评估）；对比5个经典基线、冻结的Qwen2.5-7B与Llama-3.3-70B选择器以及两个GRPO适配的Qwen2.5-7B模型；分类用ROC-AUC、回归用R²分别报告，并用数据集层面排名进行跨任务比较；采用5个带种子的评估划分，设置约33%特征预算且经典方法严格仅在训练集上拟合；另做基数匹配的语义敏感性实验（信息性特征名 vs 匿名化 vs 打乱）。

Result: 经典选择器在5个带种子划分上通常取得更高预测性能，包括在约33%特征预算、严格train-only拟合条件下，而7B选择器保持更紧凑子集；语义敏感性实验显示，零样本Qwen-7B在回归上使用信息性特征名较匿名化与打乱分别提升平均R² +0.1964与+0.1606，分类上差异较小（ROC-AUC +0.0159与+0.0084）且依赖数据集。

Conclusion: 语义信息对LLM特征选择的价值具有任务与数据集依赖性，主要体现在回归任务；经典统计选择器仍是预测性表格特征选择的强基线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can leverage semantic feature names alongside summary statistics for tabular feature selection, yet it remains unclear when such information improves subset selection relative to established statistical methods. We investigate this question within an 18-dataset framework comprising 10 datasets for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training, 2 for checkpoint validation, and 6 held-out evaluation datasets. We compare five classical baselines, frozen Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.3-70B selectors, and two GRPO-adapted Qwen2.5-7B models, reporting classification ROC-AUC and regression R^2 separately and using dataset-wise ranks for cross-task comparison. Across five seeded evaluation partitions, classical selectors generally achieve higher predictive performance than the tested 7B LLM/RL selectors, including under a pre-specified ~33% feature budget with strict train-only classical fitting, while the 7B selectors retain compact subsets. A cardinality-matched semantic sensitivity experiment shows that, for Zero-shot Qwen-7B, informative feature names improve regression performance over anonymized and shuffled alternatives (+0.1964 and +0.1606 mean R^2), whereas aggregate classification differences are smaller (+0.0159 and +0.0084 ROC-AUC) and dataset-dependent. These results characterize when semantic information benefits LLM-based feature selection and show that classical selectors remain strong baselines for predictive tabular feature selection.

</details>


### [126] [Convergence of stochastic gradient descent for learning linear neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28561)
*Gabin Maxime Nguegnang,Bubacarr Bah,Holger Rauhut,Ulrich Terstiege*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度线性网络中随机梯度下降（SGD）的收敛性，在正则化平方损失下证明了SGD迭代的几乎必然有界性，并借助随机逼近理论的渐近伪轨迹框架，对确定性与自适应步长均给出SGD几乎必然收敛到临界点集合的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD是现代深度学习的基石，但其在非凸情形下的理论收敛性质仍理解不足；已有工作多集中于梯度下降（GD），缺乏对SGD的收敛保证，且常依赖Lipschitz条件或白化数据假设。深度线性网络既保留关键非凸特性又具解析可处理性，因此作为研究载体。

Method: 以深度线性神经网络为模型，在正则化平方损失下分析SGD动力学；先证明SGD迭代的几乎必然有界性，再采用随机逼近理论中的渐近伪轨迹（asymptotic pseudotrajectories）框架，对确定性步长与自适应步长两类选择分别建立收敛性分析。

Result: 在温和条件下（不依赖Lipschitz性质、不要求白化数据），证明SGD几乎必然收敛到临界点集合，且在确定性步长与自适应步长下均成立；同时得到迭代序列的几乎必然有界性。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个给出深度线性网络中SGD几乎必然收敛保证的工作，弥合了理论优化与实际随机训练之间的差距。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is the cornerstone of modern deep learning, yet its theoretical convergence properties remain poorly understood in non-convex settings. To address this gap, we study SGD dynamics in deep linear neural networks - simplified models that retain key non-convex characteristics while remaining analytically tractable. Building on recent results for gradient descent, we extend convergence guarantees to SGD under regularized squared loss. We establish almost sure boundedness of SGD iterates. Using the framework of asymptotic pseudotrajectories from stochastic approximation theory, our analysis establishes then, for both deterministic and adaptive step-size choices, convergence of SGD to the set of critical points under mild conditions (without relying on Lipschitz properties or whitened data). To our knowledge, this is the first work providing almost sure convergence guarantees for SGD in deep linear networks, bridging the gap between theoretical optimization and practical stochastic training.

</details>


### [127] [Asymmetric Bidirectional Predictive Dynamics in Google Search and COVID-19 Time Series: A Multi-Horizon Forecast-Error Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28563)
*Andrei Velichko,Imen Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用匹配的LightGBM滚动预测（400天窗口、1–14天预测期）双向检验8组Google Trends–COVID-19配对，将log-RMSE增益与预测误差分布熵（FEDE）结合，提出方向性预测签名与相对持久性加权方向预测响应强度（DPRS，0–100），发现预测效应具有方向依赖与指标依赖，且预测期本身是关系的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线搜索数据虽常被视为流行病期间信息寻求的数字痕迹，但其预测价值往往被简化为相关性、某个选定滞后或单一预测误差分数，缺乏对方向性、误差分布动态与预测期结构的系统刻画。

Method: 预设定8组Google Trends与COVID-19指标配对并作双向分析；在滚动400天窗口、1–14天预测期上用匹配的LightGBM进行预测；将log-RMSE增益与预测误差分布熵（FEDE，刻画有符号误差分布随预测期的变化）结合，构建方向性预测签名（响应类别、RMSE效应、FEDE端点与早期斜率、跨滚动区制的持久性、预测期剖面）；并引入相对持久性加权方向预测响应强度DPRS（研究内0–100相对刻度，100为对比集中最强响应，非绝对耦合上限）。

Result: 各配对呈现一致型、仅RMSE型、仅FEDE型与混合型响应。"loss of smell"→报告病例为精度主导（平均log-RMSE增益0.104，约合9.9% RMSE降低；DPRS=100），反向则为FEDE主导（D_FEDE=0.048；DPRS=42）；"covid test"→阳性率为FEDE主导（D_FEDE=0.039；72%窗口为正；DPRS=98），但RMSE增益为负；"lockdown"→严格度为最清晰的一致型（DPRS=78）；世界级症状/病例配对的持久性较低。

Conclusion: 预测精度与误差分布动态互补；预测效应可依赖方向与指标；预测期是关系结构的一部分；聚合的区制层面模式不应外推到单个时间点，也不应被解读为因果证据。

Abstract: Online search activity is widely used as a digital trace of information seeking during epidemics, but its predictive value is often reduced to correlation, a selected lag, or a single forecast-error score. We examine eight pre-specified Google Trends-COVID-19 pairs in both directions using matched LightGBM forecasts over rolling 400-day windows and horizons of 1-14 days. Log-RMSE gain is combined with forecast-error distribution entropy (FEDE), which captures horizon-dependent changes in the signed error distribution. The resulting directional predictive signature comprises response class, RMSE effect, FEDE endpoint and early-slope responses, persistence across rolling regimes, and horizon profile. For screening candidate directions, we also introduce a relative persistence-weighted directional predictive response strength (DPRS) on a within-study 0-100 scale; 100 denotes the strongest observed response in the comparison set, not an absolute coupling maximum. The pairs show concordant, RMSE-only, FEDE-only, and mixed responses. "loss of smell" -> reported cases is accuracy-dominant, with mean log-RMSE gain 0.104 (about 9.9% equivalent RMSE reduction) and DPRS 100, whereas the reverse direction is FEDE-dominant (D_FEDE=0.048; DPRS=42). "covid test" -> positive rate is FEDE-dominant (D_FEDE=0.039; positive in 72% of windows; DPRS=98) despite negative RMSE gain, while "lockdown" -> stringency is the clearest concordant case (DPRS=78). World-level symptom/case pairs are less persistent. Thus, forecast accuracy and error-distribution dynamics are complementary, predictive effects can be direction- and metric-dependent, and forecast horizon is part of the relationship. Aggregated regime-level patterns should not be extrapolated to individual time points or interpreted as evidence of causality.

</details>


### [128] [The Dynamics of Collapse and Recovery in Clustered Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28565)
*Priyanka Sinha,Snigdhansu Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将聚类联邦学习（CFL）中的"聚类坍塌"问题建模为未知动力系统的控制问题，通过理论分析揭示贪心路由导致坍塌自增强的初始化区域，并提出基于blocked EXP3的客户端自主控制器选择机制以恢复可用专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFL在不利初始化下会出现聚类坍塌：早期客户端分配将异构群体集中于同一聚类模型并训练为混杂折衷，而另一模型得不到任何更新。由于模型决定路由、路由又决定后续模型更新，这种耦合使坍塌自我强化，标准CFL基线陷入不利起点后无法恢复，需要针对性干预机制。

Method: 将CFL形式化为未知动力系统控制问题：聚类模型为演化状态，客户端路由模式为控制输入，路由干预为状态依赖控制器。在两组线性模型中刻画使贪心路由坍塌自增强的初始化区域，并推导有限时域误差动态，表明通过反复用相容组更新"饥饿"模型可逆转坍塌。基于该条件，每个客户端用blocked EXP3算法在贪心路由与重定向干预（将更新导向未使用模型）之间选择，实现自主控制器选择。实验含同步入场（synchronized-entry）配置与持续承诺（persistent commitment）机制。

Result: 自主控制器选择配合更简单预测器和更快的专家形成，可从标准CFL基线被困的不利起点中恢复可用专家。在变换后的FEMNIST上，带持续承诺的同步入场配置可在自主控制器配置常失效的情形下恢复可用专家。

Conclusion: 将CFL坍塌问题转化为可控动力学问题，并从理论与实验证明路由干预可逆转坍塌；blocked EXP3的分布式控制器选择及持续承诺机制为缓解聚类坍塌提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clustered federated learning (CFL) can suffer from cluster collapse. In particular, under unfavorable initialization, early client assignments concentrate heterogeneous groups on one cluster model, which is then trained toward a pooled compromise, while another model receives no updates. Since the current models determine client routing and routing determines subsequent model updates, we formulate clustered federated learning as control of an unknown dynamical system, with cluster models as the evolving state, client routing patterns as the control input, and routing interventions as state dependent controllers. In a two group linear model, we identify an initialization region in which greedy routing makes collapse self reinforcing. We then derive finite horizon error dynamics showing how routing interventions can reverse collapse by repeatedly training the starved model, primarily with updates from its compatible group. Motivated by this condition, each client uses blocked EXP3 to choose between greedy routing and interventions that redirect updates toward unused models. Experiments show that with simpler predictors and faster specialist formation, autonomous controller selection recovers usable specialists from unfavorable starts that trap standard CFL baselines. In transformed FEMNIST, the synchronized-entry configuration with persistent commitment recovers usable specialists in settings where the autonomous controller configuration often does not. Code and scripts for reproducing the experiments and figures are available at https://github.com/prsinha1/FedRecover.

</details>


### [129] [The Token-Energy Law of LLM Decode: Active Bytes and Realized Time Account for Joules per Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28576)
*Chenxu Niu,Batuhan Sencer,Wei Zhang,Yong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"Token-Energy Law"：以"活跃字节"与"实际解码时间"两个维度构建仿射模型，预测稠密、全历史解码下GPU板级每输出token能耗；在Hopper/Blackwell上三模型BF16验证，留出条件平均误差≤3.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的高能效部署需要理解模型架构、硬件与服务配置如何共同决定"每生成token能耗"，但缺乏简洁、可解释且可跨配置标定的定量模型。

Method: 定义每输出token的GPU板级能耗模型，用两个核心量：活跃字节（区分每解码迭代共享的稠密权重与每请求读写的私有KV历史）= 逻辑字节需求；实际解码时间（解码总时长/输出token数）。二者以共同仿射形式组合，系数按模型与硬件/运行配置分别标定。

Result: 实验覆盖三个模型、BF16解码、NVIDIA Hopper与Blackwell GPU（含Hopper双卡张量并行）。固定系数下，结合实测解码时间的留出条件平均能耗估计与实测值偏差最大3.73%。

Conclusion: 该仿射两坐标公式可解释批处理对共享权重工作的摊薄、更长注意力历史抬高私有KV需求、吞吐决定时间相关每token成本；为能耗感知的部署配置提供简明可解释的预测工具。

Abstract: Energy-efficient deployment of large language models requires understanding how model architecture, hardware, and serving settings shape energy per generated token. We introduce the Token-Energy Law, a compact, interpretable model of GPU-board energy per output token for dense, full-history decoding. It relates token energy to two core dimensions: active bytes and realized decode time. Active bytes describe logical byte demand per output, separating dense weights shared within a decode iteration from private key--value (KV) histories read and updated for each request. Realized decode time is the elapsed decode duration divided by the number of output tokens produced. We combine the two byte coordinates and time in a common affine form, with coefficients calibrated for each model and hardware/runtime configuration. This formulation explains how batching amortizes shared-weight work, longer attended histories increase private KV demand, and throughput determines the time-associated cost per output. Our experiments cover three models under BF16 decoding on NVIDIA Hopper and Blackwell GPUs, including two-GPU tensor parallelism on Hopper. Using fixed coefficients and observed decode time, held-out condition-mean energy estimates differ from measurements by at most 3.73%.

</details>


### [130] [Continuous-Time Policy Gradient from Discrete Data: Convergence Analysis and Model-Free Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28580)
*Martin Hernandez,Xinyan Qing,Mo Zhou,Yuhua Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个结合严格收敛分析与离散数据学习的连续时间强化学习策略梯度框架，并与PhiBE生成器结合形成无模型actor-critic算法，在线性二次调节器与轮式机器人上验证性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间强化学习需在由随机微分方程驱动、动力学未知的条件下，仅利用离散时间观测数据寻找最优策略；现有方法缺乏将严谨收敛分析与离散数据学习相统一的框架。

Method: 先建立性能差引理，推导随机化策略与参数化策略的策略梯度公式；证明熵正则化策略流以指数速率收敛到正则化最优解；对离散参数化更新导出显式区分critic误差、策略回归误差与学习步长贡献的收敛界；并将该框架与PhiBE（由离散转移数据构造连续时间代理生成器）结合，得到保持连续时间控制结构的on-policy与off-policy无模型actor-critic算法。

Result: 理论上给出指数收敛性及含误差分解的收敛界；数值实验在熵正则化线性二次调节器和非线性轮式机器人上，于已知动力学与数据驱动动力学两种设定下均表现出良好性能与稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将连续时间策略梯度的收敛理论与离散数据驱动的无模型学习有机统一，误差分解揭示了关键误差来源，为未知动力学下的连续时间强化学习提供了可证明且实用的算法框架。

Abstract: Continuous-time reinforcement learning seeks an optimal policy under unknown dynamics driven by stochastic differential equations using observations collected at discrete times. We develop a policy gradient framework that combines rigorous convergence analysis with learning from discrete data. We first establish a performance difference lemma and derive policy gradient formulas for randomized and parametrized policies. We then prove exponential convergence of an entropy-regularized policy flow to the regularized optimum. For discrete parametrized updates, we derive a convergence bound that explicitly separates the contributions of critic error, policy-regression error, and the learning step size. To enable learning under unknown dynamics, we integrate this framework with PhiBE, which constructs a continuous-time surrogate generator from discrete transition data. The resulting on-policy and off-policy model-free actor-critic algorithms preserve the continuous-time structure of the control problem. Numerical experiments on an entropy-regularized linear-quadratic regulator and a nonlinear wheeled robot demonstrate the performance and stability of the proposed methods with both known and data-driven dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [PaPi: Parallel Picard-Duhamel Integration for Fast Neuro-Spectral Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28591)
*Arthur Bizzi,Márcio Marques,Kleyton Da Costa,Vitor Balestro,Tiago Novello,João M. Pereira,Daniel Yukimura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PaPi：针对神经谱架构(NeuSA)中时序演化需要昂贵序贯数值积分的问题，提出基于弱非线性流动的指数积分+Duhamel-Picard时间并行推理方案，并给出指数收敛性与误差界，训练/推理加速最高两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeuSA类物理信息神经网络在频域求解时间相关PDE有效，但其训练与推理依赖对学到向量场的昂贵数值积分（通常为序贯Runge-Kutta时间步进），无法进行时间并行，成为效率瓶颈。

Method: 受弱非线性流动启发，构建PaPi积分格式：利用指数积分处理线性主导部分，并结合Duhamel-Picard迭代实现时间并行推理；理论上证明该迭代对所有流动指数收敛，并给出误差界，其界依赖于流动与线性流动的接近程度。

Result: 实验表明PaPi将训练与推理加速最高达两个数量级；在低维PDE学习问题上训练时间低于1秒，同时保持有竞争力的预测精度。

Conclusion: 用指数积分与Duhamel-Picard迭代替代序贯时间步进，可在保持精度的同时显著提升NeuSA的PDE求解效率，为时间并行化训练/推理提供理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Neuro-spectral architectures (NeuSA) are a class of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) that have proven effective for solving time-dependent PDEs in the frequency domain. However, training and inference often rely on costly numerical integration of the learned vector field, typically using sequential Runge-Kutta time stepping, which limits temporal parallelism. We introduce PaPi, an integration scheme inspired by weakly nonlinear flows that enables time-Parallel inference through exponential integration and a Duhamel-Picard scheme. We show that the Duhamel-Picard iteration converges exponentially for all flows, and provide explicit error bounds that depend on how close the flow is to linear. Experiments show that PaPi accelerates training and inference by up to two orders of magnitude, achieving training times of under one second for low-dimensional PDE learning problems, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [132] [Turning TS2Vec into a Foundation Model for Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28602)
*Vasilii Feofanov,Zhenghan Tai,Andrei Ermakov,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TS2Vec-FM将TS2Vec的紧凑膨胀卷积编码器转为一次性预训练的基础模型：在合成语料上预训练，以原始幅度视图与实例归一化形状视图双stem输入，并将两视图专家编码器蒸馏为单一编码器；冻结后线性探测在UCR-128与UEA-27上分别达83.3%/75.3%，距SOTA MantisV2仅0.3pp，且优于逐数据集训练的TS2Vec。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型倾向跨数据集复用单一编码器，但普遍依赖注意力、patch tokenizer与中间层特征提取；而TS2Vec具备直接作用于时序样本、支持变长输入无需重采样的紧凑卷积架构优势，却须逐数据集重新预训练。作者欲验证这些属性在"仅预训练一次"的前提下能否保留。

Method: 三项改动：(1) 在合成语料上完成一次性预训练；(2) 通过两个小型stem向编码器分别输入原始幅度视图与实例归一化形状视图；(3) 将每个视图的专家编码器蒸馏进单一编码器。最终模型为1.6M参数的膨胀卷积网络，冻结后仅做线性探测。

Result: 冻结编码器+线性探测下，UCR-128达83.3%、UEA-27达75.3%，与当前SOTA MantisV2相差0.3pp以内，并超过在每个目标数据集上单独训练的TS2Vec。

Conclusion: TS2Vec可实质上转变为基础模型，且其"老派"卷积编码器加两层MLP stem的设计，在不使用注意力、patch tokenizer与中间层提取的情况下即可逼近SOTA，表明基础模型性能并非必然依赖上述现代架构组件。

Abstract: TS2Vec is one of the strongest self-supervised methods for time series classification, introduced in 2022. It is designed to be trained anew on every dataset: self-supervised pre-training followed by linear probing. Since then, the field has shifted towards foundation models that reuse one encoder across datasets. Yet TS2Vec offers a compact convolutional architecture that operates directly on temporal samples and accepts variable-length inputs without resampling. We ask whether these properties can be retained when the encoder is pre-trained only once, and show that they can: TS2Vec can essentially be turned into a foundation model. Three changes suffice: pre-train on a synthetic corpus, feed the encoder a raw amplitude view and an instance-normalized shape view of the input through two small stems, and distill one specialist encoder per view into a single encoder. The result, TS2Vec-FM, is a 1.6M-parameter dilated convolutional network that, kept frozen, reaches 83.3% on UCR-128 and 75.3% on UEA-27 under linear probing, within 0.3 pp of MantisV2, the current state of the art, and above TS2Vec trained separately on each target dataset. What we find most interesting is how different TS2Vec-FM is from other foundation models in design: near-parity is reached without attention, without a patch tokenizer, and without intermediate-layer extraction, using an "old-school" convolutional encoder and a two-layer MLP stem.

</details>


### [133] [Efficient Edge-aware Attention Network for Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28618)
*Vincent Jung,Alba Carballo-Castro,Yiming Qin,Lonneke van der Plas,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向图生成的边感知注意力层，通过头级分数调制注入结构信息、以轻量残差机制更新边表示，并基于PyTorch FlexAttention实现，在保持生成质量的同时将峰值GPU显存降低约65%，显著提升训练与采样吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成模型依赖图专用Transformer架构联合更新节点与边特征，无法直接复用硬件感知的高效注意力实现（如FlexAttention），导致可扩展性受限。直接迁移并非易事：需将边级与图级特征注入成对注意力分数、遵守图掩码，且动态边更新是二次方显存占用的主要来源。

Method: 设计面向图生成的边感知注意力层：1）通过head-wise score modulation将结构信息注入注意力分数；2）采用轻量残差机制更新边表示，同时保留依赖注意力分数的边更新；3）该层可高效地用PyTorch FlexAttention实现。

Result: 生成质量与基线相当；峰值GPU显存降低约65%；训练与采样吞吐大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该架构在兼容高效注意力内核的前提下保持图生成质量并显著降低显存开销，是迈向可扩展图基础模型的务实一步。

Abstract: Graph generative models commonly use graph-specific Transformer architectures to jointly update node and edge features. However, these architectures do not directly benefit from recent hardware-aware attention implementations, limiting their scalability. The adaptation is, however, non-trivial: edge and graph-level features must be injected into pairwise attention scores, graph masks must be respected, and dynamic edge updates remain a major source of quadratic memory usage. We propose an edge-aware attention layer for graph generation that incorporates structural information through head-wise score modulation, while updating edge representations with a lightweight residual mechanism that retains attention-score dependent edge updates. This layer can be effectively implemented using PyTorch's FlexAttention. We achieve similar generation quality to our baseline while reducing peak GPU memory by around 65% and substantially improving training and sampling throughput. These results suggest that the proposed architecture is a practical step toward scalable graph foundation models.

</details>


### [134] [Planning under Noise with Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28623)
*Michael Chen,Hong Chul Nam,Xi Deng,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PUN——一种学习噪声感知价值场的神经算子，用于在未见几何中做不确定性下的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定控制执行噪声下的规划需要跨环境可靠的反馈策略。现有随机最优控制的screened-Poisson表述存在两个难题：边界条件难以适配不同几何（零Dirichlet障碍边界会终止采样路径、削弱长路径上的目标信号），以及无网格高效计算带可靠梯度的场困难。

Method: 1) 将障碍边界改为反射Neumann条件、目标处设Dirichlet条件，使采样路径可持续探索自由空间；2) 将蒙特卡洛求解器Walk on Stars (WoSt)扩展到高维以无网格求解PDE；3) 用部分收敛的含噪MC标签学习几何到场映射（摊销计算），避免每条样本昂贵的收敛计算；4) 推理时以预测全局场引导的有界局部搜索做test-time scaling。

Result: 在多个维度的规划任务、四个噪声水平下，PUN匹配或超越所有对比规划器；4D重规划基准达95–100%成功率且运行时间最低；4D最高噪声水平下成功率相对提升10.7–60.6%，速度提升3.29–32.15倍。

Conclusion: 反射Neumann+目标Dirichlet的边界设计结合高维WoSt与神经算子摊销，能有效生成噪声感知价值场，在高维不确定规划中兼顾成功率与效率。

Abstract: Planning under uncertain control execution noise requires reliable feedback policies across diverse environments. We introduce Planning Under Noise (PUN), a neural operator that learns noise-aware value fields for planning in unseen geometries. Building on an established screened-Poisson formulation of stochastic optimal control, we address two challenges: specifying boundary conditions that support navigation across varying geometries and efficiently computing fields with reliable gradients. Zero-Dirichlet obstacle boundaries terminate sampled paths at walls, weakening the goal signal along long routes and making it harder to estimate reliably. We instead impose reflecting Neumann conditions on obstacles and a Dirichlet condition at the goal, allowing sampled paths to continue exploring free space until they reach the target. To solve the resulting PDE without a configuration-space mesh, we extend a Monte Carlo (MC) solver, Walk on Stars (WoSt), to higher dimensions. However, local MC fluctuations can reverse estimated gradient directions and cause planning failures, making direct planning require costly sampling. PUN amortizes this computation by learning geometry-to-field mappings from noisy, partially converged Monte Carlo labels, avoiding expensive convergence for every training example. At inference, test-time scaling uses bounded local search guided by the predicted global field. We evaluate PUN on planning tasks across dimensions. Across four noise levels, PUN matches or outperforms all compared planners, achieving 95-100% success on the 4D replanning benchmark while maintaining the lowest runtime. At the highest tested noise level in 4D, PUN achieves 10.7-60.6% relative gains in success rate over other methods while running 3.29-32.15 times faster.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Automatic Differentiation with Constraints in Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27888)
*Nikolay Yavich,Alexander Ryabov,Viacheslav Naumov,Sayan Ranu,N. M. Anoop Krishnan,Evgeny Burnaev,Vladimir Vanovskiy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种完全可微的图基隐式求解器，将PDE数值求解中的大规模方程组作为约束处理，在四类任务上验证其稳定性和可微性，可显著降低正演建模复杂度并高效求解反问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构网格上PDE的近似解可在图上表示；求解刚性演化与静态PDE涉及大规模方程组，需将其视为约束进行数值优化。现有方法在兼顾稳定性、可微性与复杂度方面存在不足。

Method: 构建完全可微的图基隐式求解器，将大规模线性方程组的求解作为约束纳入优化过程，实现端到端可微的隐式求解框架。

Result: 在四个任务上完成评估：a) 演化与静态问题的线性/非线性正演；b) 估计方程系数的反问题；c) 估计右端项的反问题；d) 用于加速正演的可学习图粗化。求解器具备稳定性与可微性，显著降低正演建模复杂度，且能高效求解相关反问题。

Conclusion: 该求解器为多种物理与工程应用提供有力工具，尤其适用于需通过反问题降低模型不确定性的地球物理领域。

Abstract: Approximate solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) on unstructured grids can be represented on a graph. Numerical optimization involving stiff evolutionary and static PDEs often requires solving a large system of equations, which can be treated as a constraint. In this work, we present a fully differentiable graph-based implicit solver that incorporates this constraint. We evaluate its performance across four key tasks: a) linear and nonlinear forward modeling of evolutionary and static problems, b) solving inverse problems to estimate equation coefficients, c) solving inverse problems to estimate the right-hand side, and d) learnable graph coarsening to accelerate forward modeling. The stability and differentiability of our solver enable significant reductions in the complexity of forward modeling while efficiently addressing related inverse problems. These capabilities make it a powerful tool for different physics and engineering applications, in particular for geophysics, where inverse problems are commonly solved in order to reduce the model uncertainty.

</details>


### [136] [Navalia: Advancing Cowork Agents through Verifiable Task Synthesis and Long-Horizon Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26887)
*Hengqi Liu,Ziyuan Liu,Yang Qin,Wanting Zhou,Zichuan Wang,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Zhaokai Luo,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Navalia，一个面向 Cowork Agents 的框架，将可验证任务合成与长时程后训练结合，用于金融分析任务，并在五个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正从对话助手转向可自主规划、使用工具、执行复杂任务的 Cowork Agents；金融分析要求跨文档证据整合与多步数值推理，但专家人工编写任务成本高，而直接用 LLM 合成难以产出难度足够且答案可靠的任务。

Method: 以企业财报为数据源，从完整事实集合中语义筛选支撑证据，构建可执行计算链，生成具有可追溯证据与步骤间真实数据依赖的分析任务；再用独立 LLM 审核与程序化检查验证证据一致性与答案正确性；将验证后的任务用于轨迹采集、冷启动监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL），提升长时程任务执行能力。模型从 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 初始化。

Result: 在 OfficeQA Pro、FinSearchComp、Finance Agent Benchmark、APEX-Agents-AA 与 Workspace-Bench-Lite 五个基准上相较基座模型均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: Navalia 提升了证据整合、工具使用与长时程任务执行能力，并可泛化到更广泛的 Cowork 场景（包括通用办公任务）。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from conversational assistants into Cowork Agents capable of autonomous planning, tool use, and complex task execution. Financial analysis requires these agents to integrate cross-document evidence and perform multistep numerical reasoning, but expert task authoring is costly, while direct LLM synthesis struggles to produce challenging tasks with reliable answers. We introduce Navalia, a framework for Cowork Agents that combines verifiable task synthesis with long-horizon post-training. Using corporate financial reports as its data source, Navalia semantically selects supporting evidence from complete fact sets and constructs executable computation chains to generate analytical tasks with traceable evidence and actual data dependencies between steps. We further use independent LLM review and programmatic checks to verify evidence consistency and answer correctness. The verified tasks are then used for trajectory collection, cold-start supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) to improve long-horizon task execution. Navalia, initialized from Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, achieves consistent gains over the base model across all five benchmarks: OfficeQA Pro, FinSearchComp, Finance Agent Benchmark, APEX-Agents-AA, and Workspace-Bench-Lite. These results demonstrate that Navalia improves evidence integration, tool use, and long-horizon task execution capabilities and generalizes to broader Cowork settings, including general office tasks.

</details>


### [137] [Large language model and reinforcement learning based autonomous construction and progress updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26899)
*Yifei Xiao,Huasheng Lin,T. Y. Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合大语言模型与强化学习的自主施工方法：用改进的soft actor-critic算法为移动起重机规划施工动作，以机器人运动学与控制算法保证动作可执行，并用定制嵌入模型与ChatGPT 5完成进度更新、安全信息传递与进度报告生成，最后通过足尺挡土墙施工实验验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口快速增长使传统施工方式难以满足住房需求；机器学习算法的进步推动施工行业智能化探索，而近年大语言模型的兴起为传统施工向智能施工转型提供了更多可能。

Method: 1) 提出基于大语言模型与强化学习的自主施工方法；2) 开发增强型soft actor-critic强化学习算法，依据施工任务为移动起重机规划动作；3) 采用机器人运动学与控制算法确保强化学习生成的动作可由起重机实际执行；4) 开发定制嵌入模型，按用户兴趣提取施工环境信息；5) 使用大语言模型ChatGPT 5更新施工进度、向控制系统提供安全信息并生成施工进度报告。

Result: 通过若干足尺（full-scale）挡土墙施工实验验证了所提方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的“大语言模型+强化学习”自主施工框架在真实尺度的挡土墙施工任务中具备可行性，表明大语言模型可有效支撑施工过程的进度管理、安全控制与报告生成，为智能施工转型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Due to the rapid growth of the global population, traditional construction methods are gradually unable to meet the current housing demand. The advancement of machine learning algorithms has encouraged researchers to explore their applications in the construction industry, leading to more intelligent and efficient building processes. In particular, the rise of large language models in recent years has provided more possibilities for the transformation from traditional construction to smart construction. In this study, a large language model and reinforcement learning based autonomous construction method is proposed. An enhanced soft actor-critic reinforcement learning algorithm is developed to plan actions for a mobile crane according to construction tasks. Robot kinematics and control algorithms are used to ensure that the actions generated by the reinforcement learning algorithm can be executed by the crane. In addition, a customized embedding model is developed to extract information from the construction environment according to user interests. Then, a large language model, ChatGPT 5, is used to update construction progress, provide safety information to control system, and generate construction progress reports. The feasibility of proposed method is verified through several full-scall retaining wall construction experiments.

</details>


### [138] [Pareto-Optimal RTL Code Generation via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26904)
*Koki Takeshita,Kenji Uebayashi,Hikaru Tomonari,Masaya Tsunokake,Koichiro Yawata,Tatsuya Sasaki,Yusuke Ohtsubo,Kota Dohi,Yasuhiro Sogawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个面向LLM电路生成的方法，优化多次生成所得整个电路集合在PPA目标空间中的质量与多样性，而非单一电路质量；通过生成式训练流程与基于Hypervolume Contribution的奖励实现Pareto前沿覆盖提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法将Power、Performance、Area多目标聚合成单一标量奖励，难以同时产出覆盖低功耗、低时延、小面积等不同权衡的多样化Pareto最优解；而实际设计中各目标相对重要性随产品需求与约束变化，事后由设计者挑选所需权衡更有价值，因此需要高质量、多样化的候选电路集合。

Method: (1) 基于生成的训练流程：将整个Pareto解集覆盖度的改进重构为序列化电路生成问题；(2) 基于Hypervolume Contribution (HVC)的奖励函数：度量生成电路对Pareto前沿的贡献量。整体以集合级质量与多样性为优化目标。

Result: 在RTLLM v2基准上，相对无PPA优化基线实现+13.3个百分点的相对Hypervolume提升，相对单目标强化学习方法提升+14.4个百分点，并在电路多样性上表现更优。

Conclusion: 将LLM电路生成的优化目标从单电路质量转向集合级Pareto覆盖质量与多样性是有效的，HVC奖励与序列化生成流程能显著提升Hypervolume并改善多样性，为设计者提供可事后选择的高质量候选集。

Abstract: Recent studies have proposed reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) methods that enable LLMs to generate high-quality RTL circuits from natural-language specifications.
