Existing approaches optimize circuit PPA (Power, Performance, Area) by aggregating multiple objectives into a single scalar reward, making it difficult to simultaneously generate diverse Pareto-optimal solutions across different trade-offs such as low power, low latency, and small area. However, in practical design, the relative importance of each objective varies depending on product requirements and design constraints; thus, obtaining a diverse and high-quality candidate set is beneficial in that designers can choose a preferred trade-off a posteriori. In this work, we are the first to propose a learning method for LLM-based circuit generation that optimizes, not the quality of a single circuit, but the quality and diversity of the entire circuit set obtained through multiple generations in the PPA objective space. The proposed method consists of (1) a generation-based training pipeline that reformulates coverage improvement of the entire Pareto solution set into a sequential circuit generation problem, and (2) a reward function based on Hypervolume Contribution (HVC) that measures how much a generated circuit contributes to the Pareto front. Evaluation on the RTLLM v2 benchmark shows that the proposed method achieves +13.3 percentage points in relative Hypervolume over a baseline without PPA optimization and +14.4 percentage points over a single-objective reinforcement learning method, while also demonstrating superiority in circuit diversity.

</details>


### [139] [MedPAO-Assist: Edge-Deployable, Modality-Aware Agentic AI for Radiology Report Structuring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26913)
*Shrish Shrinath Vaidya,Minmini Selvam,Ganapathy Krishnamurthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedPAO-Assist：可在普通笔记本CPU上离线运行的边缘智能体框架，用0.6B级小模型从放射科医生口述直接生成结构化影像报告，峰值内存约3.3GB、端到端约31秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射报告结构化方案多依赖专有云端大模型或需高端GPU的大开源模型，难以满足临床对数据隐私与离线运行的要求，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 基于Extract-Ground-Generate范式的多智能体架构：概念抽取、SNOMED-CT本体对齐、概念归类与结构化报告生成，随后加入语音驱动的人在环纠正阶段；采用异步多线程流水线，全流程由0.6B参数量级SLM驱动，无键盘输入、无外部API、无网络。支持胸部X线、胸部CT、腹部CT、脑部MRI四种模态。

Result: 峰值内存约3.3GB，标准笔记本CPU上平均端到端延迟约31秒；对概念抽取、本体对齐、概念归类各阶段分别评估，并给出硬件性能剖析，证明实时CPU-only临床部署可行。

Conclusion: 全流程SLM的智能体系统可在边缘设备上实现"无接触"放射报告工作流，为隐私敏感、需离线运行的临床场景提供可行路径；据作者所知是最早在0.6B参数量级运行完整流水线的智能体系统之一。

Abstract: The adoption of Large Language Models for radiology report structuring has been largely dominated by either proprietary cloud-based models or large open models requiring high-end GPUs for deployment, which is a critical bottleneck for clinical adoption in radiology workflows that demand data privacy and offline operation. To address this, we present MedPAO-Assist, an edge-deployable agentic framework that generates structured radiology reports directly from radiologist dictation and voice commands, requiring only $\approx3.3$ GB peak RAM and achieving an average end-to-end latency of $\approx31$ seconds on a standard laptop CPU through an asynchronous, multi-threaded execution pipeline. The system currently supports four major imaging modalities -- Chest X-ray, Chest CT, Abdomen CT, and Brain MRI. To our knowledge, it is among the earliest agentic systems to run its entire pipeline with a Small Language Model (SLM) in the 0.6B-parameter range. This multi-agent architecture, built around an Extract-Ground-Generate paradigm, performs concept extraction, SNOMED-CT grounding, and structured report generation, followed by a speech-driven human-in-the-loop correction phase, together forming a fully "no-touch" reporting workflow that requires no keyboard input, external API calls, or internet connectivity. We evaluate each pipeline stage individually -- concept extraction, ontology grounding, and concept categorization and report hardware profiling results demonstrating the system's feasibility for real-time, CPU-only clinical deployment.

</details>


### [140] [DataPraxis: Scaling Analytics Tasks via Verified Workspace Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26924)
*Nan Jia,Wenxuan Shi,Jingqing Ruan,Zhizhao Zeng,Tianyi Zhai,Zhongda Su,Feng Hong,Zhengyu Chen,Wenhao Yan,Haojin Yang,Siqi Dai,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于"验证式工作区演进"的通用分析任务合成框架 DataPraxis，构建 5,000 条已验证轨迹数据集 DataPraxis-5K，并训练 DataPraxis-27B / 35B-A3B，在 DABStep 与 JobBench 上取得与前沿模型相当或更优的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体需具备持续推理、迭代调查与可验证证据的专业分析能力，而此类能力训练依赖多样且具挑战性的任务。直接从原始数据生成任务存在两点不足：一是忽略了"通过分析才浮现"的深层问题；二是缺乏可用于验证的证据支撑。

Method: 提出 DataPraxis 任务合成框架：以工作区演进为核心，逐步向工作区注入经校验的分析发现与工件，通过工作区的继承与组合生成更深层问题；任务由累积发现及其依赖关系构造，并在演进过程中与任务组装完成后进行双重验证。

Result: 基于该框架构建 DataPraxis-5K，涵盖多领域与多种输出格式的 5,000 条已验证轨迹，用于训练 DataPraxis-27B 与 DataPraxis-35B-A3B。DataPraxis-27B 在 DABStep 上达 55.11、JobBench 上达 51.9，与 GLM-5.2 等前沿模型持平或超越。

Conclusion: 验证式工作区演进可作为高质量分析任务合成的有效范式，兼顾问题深度与证据可验证性；所得数据可训练出在专业分析基准上具竞争力的中小规模智能体模型，数据集已开源。

Abstract: General-purpose agents are increasingly expected to perform professional analytics work, which requires sustained reasoning, iterative investigation, and reliable evidence. Training these capabilities requires diverse and challenging tasks. However, generating tasks directly from raw data often overlooks deeper questions that emerge through analysis and provides limited evidence for verification. To address these limitations, we introduce DataPraxis, an analytics task synthesis framework based on verified workspace evolution. The framework progressively enriches workspaces with validated findings and artifacts, then inherits and combines these workspaces to uncover deeper questions. It constructs tasks from accumulated findings and their dependencies, verifying analytical results both during evolution and after task assembly. Using this framework, we construct DataPraxis-5K, a dataset of 5,000 verified trajectories spanning diverse domains and output formats, to train DataPraxis-27B and DataPraxis-35B-A3B. DataPraxis-27B achieves 55.11 on DABStep and 51.9 on JobBench, matching or surpassing frontier models such as GLM-5.2. DataPraxis-5K is available at huggingface.co/datasets/stargazerhust/DataPraxis-5k

</details>


### [141] [Industrial Agentic AI: LLM-Driven New-Generation Agents for Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26941)
*Lei Ren,Haiteng Wang,Shixiang Li,Jing Zhang,Yuqing Wang,Jingwei Guo,Yunfei Zhu,Yuanjun Laili,Yang Tang,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述LLM驱动的工业智能体，提出五大挑战分析、统一分类体系（应用场景/具身形态/五级能力）、四层系统架构及闭环执行机制，并梳理工业全生命周期代表性应用与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM智能体具备自主规划与执行能力，为智能制造带来新机遇；但动态环境、复杂物理约束与严格可靠性要求限制其在智能制造中的直接部署，亟需对工业智能体进行系统化梳理与框架化指导。

Method: 综述性研究：先分析五项关键挑战并界定工业智能体的概念与核心特征；构建涵盖应用场景、具身形态与五级能力层次的统一分类法；提出四层系统架构并详述核心模块与闭环任务执行机制；最后按工业全生命周期回顾代表性应用。

Result: 形成结构化的知识框架：五大挑战、智能体概念与核心特征、三维统一分类体系、四层架构与闭环执行机制，以及全生命周期代表性应用图谱与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该文为工业智能体的设计、开发与应用提供结构化参考，弥合通用LLM智能体与智能制造可靠性需求之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are enabling agents to move beyond predefined workflows toward autonomous planning and execution, creating new opportunities for smart manufacturing. However, dynamic environments, complex physical constraints, and stringent reliability requirements hinder the direct deployment of general-purpose agents in smart manufacturing. This article presents a systematic overview of LLM-driven industrial agents. First, we analyze five key challenges and discuss the concept and core characteristics of industrial agents. We then establish a unified taxonomy covering application scenarios, embodiment forms, and five capability levels. Next, we propose a four-layer system architecture and detail its core modules and operational mechanisms for closed-loop task execution. Finally, we review representative applications across the industrial lifecycle and outline future research directions. This article provides a structured reference for the design, development, and application of industrial agents.

</details>


### [142] [Fix, Not Just Resample: Bounded Compiler-Guided Repair in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26942)
*Haotian Deng,Chris Farber,Sonal Narasimha,Samskrithi Sivakumar,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出有界在线步骤级修复模块：利用 Lean 编译器错误信息驱动预训练修复模型就地修正失败的 tactic，在 LeanDojo 与 Lean Workbook 上提升求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定理证明器依赖语言模型采样，但 Lean 等证明助手对每个失败步骤都会返回精确错误信息，而纯采样策略直接丢弃这些反馈，造成信息浪费。

Method: 在每步采样 K 个候选 tactic，对失败 tactic 最多修复 R 次；将编译器错误信息输入预训练修复模型，就地修复被拒绝的步骤，而非丢弃。在 LeanDojo 的 1,990 定理子集上针对 K∈{1,3,5,8} 与纯采样基线对比，并在 Lean Workbook 上复现。

Result: 在所有候选预算下均优于采样基线，相对提升最高 32%，额外解出 82 个定理；收益前置，首次修复尝试即贡献总增益的 84–96%；增益主要来自浅层 tactic 执行失败，而非类型不匹配等结构性错误；在 Lean Workbook 上复现，求解率近乎翻倍且零退化。

Conclusion: 修复以额外运行时换取可达证明，且代价可预测：采样受限时收益最大、回报前置、且集中于浅层错误；作为一种针对性、有界的机制，可稳定复现并显著提升求解率。

Abstract: Automated theorem provers often rely on sampling language models, but proof assistants like Lean reject each failed step with a precise error message --- information that pure sampling discards. To overcome this, we introduce a bounded, online step-level repair module. Our search samples $K$ candidate tactics at each step and repairs a failed tactic at most $R$ times. Instead of discarding failed tactics, we feed the compiler's error message to a pretrained repair model to fix individual rejected steps in place. This approach improves the solve rate over a sampling-only baseline across all tested candidate budgets ($K \in \{1,3,5,8\}$), achieving up to a $32\%$ relative gain and solving $82$ additional theorems on a $1{,}990$-theorem subset of the LeanDojo benchmark. Repair is not free: it trades extra runtime for reachable proofs. But the trade is predictable: repair pays most when sampling is constrained; its return is front-loaded, with the first repair attempt capturing $84\text{--}96\%$ of the total gain; and the gain comes almost entirely from shallow tactic-execution failures rather than structural errors such as type mismatches. This targeted repair successfully replicates on a second benchmark (Lean Workbook), nearly doubling the solve rate with zero regressions.

</details>


### [143] [MAMath-Bench: Process-Aware and Robust Evaluation of Multimodal Multi-Agent Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26943)
*Xinping Wang,Jie Ma,Rui Xing,Wensong Guo,Xiayi Zhang,Jianhao Deng,Zihao Zhou,Boqian Yang,Feijuan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多模态、过程感知的评测基准 MAMath-Bench（1003题、11类、含文本/图像/音频与细粒度里程碑标注），从答案正确性、推理过程可靠性和对抗扰动鲁棒性三维评估多智能体数学推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多智能体系统（MAS）数学推理的评测多聚焦纯文本场景下的最终答案正确性，缺乏对多模态推理能力、中间推理步骤可靠性以及面对推理错误时鲁棒性的洞察。

Method: 构建过程感知基准 MAMath-Bench：涵盖11个数学类别、多难度层级与文本/图像/音频多模态，并引入细粒度里程碑（milestone）标注，支持从解题正确性、推理过程可靠性、对对抗性推理链扰动的鲁棒性三个维度进行评测。

Result: 三项关键发现：(1) 多智能体协作相较强单模型并不能稳定提升最终答案准确率；(2) 协作可显著提高中间推理覆盖度，但可能带来推理冗余增加；(3) 当前系统对对抗性推理错误（尤其是细微结构性扰动）仍然脆弱。

Conclusion: 仅以最终答案正确性评测 MAS 数学能力不足；需采用过程感知、多模态与鲁棒性导向的评测范式，MAMath-Bench 为后续改进协作机制与验证策略提供了基准与诊断工具。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning is a fundamental capability for educational applications of Large Language Models (LLMs), where reliable problem solving requires not only correct answers but also coherent and verifiable reasoning processes. Multi-agent systems (MASs) have shown growing potential in this domain by leveraging collaboration and verification among multiple LLM agents. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on the correctness of final answers in text-only settings, offering limited insight into multimodal reasoning capabilities, the reliability of intermediate reasoning steps, and robustness against reasoning errors. To address these limitations, we introduce MAMath-Bench, a process-aware benchmark designed to evaluate the multimodal mathematical reasoning capabilities of MASs. MAMath-Bench comprises 1,003 problems spanning 11 mathematical categories, multiple difficulty levels, and several modalities, including text, images, and audio. It features fine-grained milestone annotations to evaluate systems across three dimensions: problem-solving correctness, reasoning-process reliability, and robustness to adversarial reasoning-chain perturbations. Our evaluation yields three key findings. First, multi-agent collaboration does not consistently improve final-answer accuracy over strong single LLMs. Second, collaboration can substantially enhance the coverage of intermediate reasoning, but such gains may come at the cost of increased reasoning redundancy. Third, current systems remain vulnerable to adversarial reasoning errors, particularly those involving subtle structural perturbations. The dataset is available at https://github.com/mira-ai-lab/AgentMath.

</details>


### [144] [LightCRS: Lightweight Multi-Domain Conversational Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26948)
*He Ma,Lan You,Qiyang Wu,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出轻量级、查询条件化的统一多域多模态对话推荐框架LightCRS，并以5.3M参数模块实现跨域共享，在新建25域基准MM-R2A-25上取得SOTA，HR@1较最强基线提升35.4%，单查询延迟降低266倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉-语言模型用于对话推荐面临多域扩展困境：每域单独适配一个VLM成本随域数线性增长且需对话路由；而将多域简单合并为单一VLM又会因品类间商品特征差异大而导致精度下降。

Method: LightCRS用冻结编码器离线预计算多模态商品表示；在线阶段由仅5.3M参数、跨域共享的紧凑模块通过查询条件化交叉注意力为每条对话融合证据，并用候选间Transformer对候选进行联合比较。同时构建25域基准MM-R2A-25，将Reddit推荐求助对话与Amazon商品listing配对。

Result: 在MM-R2A-25上取得最优的平均HR@1、NDCG@5与MRR，HR@1较最强基线提升35.4%，单查询延迟降低266倍；联合多域训练相比各域单独训练使HR@1提升15.2%。

Conclusion: 通过离线预计算+在线轻量查询条件化融合与跨域参数共享，可在避免线性扩展成本与路由的同时实现统一多域多模态对话推荐，且多域联合训练本身带来正向迁移收益。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems have recently adopted vision-language models to match user dialogue intents with multimodal product information. However, extending these methods to many product domains faces a dilemma: deploying a separately adapted VLM per domain scales cost linearly with the number of domains and requires routing each dialogue to the right model, while naively merging all domains into a single VLM degrades accuracy because product characteristics vary widely across categories. We propose LightCRS, a lightweight, query-conditioned framework for unified multi-domain multimodal conversational recommendation. LightCRS precomputes multimodal product representations once offline with frozen encoders. Online, a compact 5.3M-parameter module fuses this evidence for each dialogue via query-conditioned cross-attention, whose parameters are shared across all domains, and compares candidates jointly with an inter-candidate Transformer. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct MM-R2A-25, a 25-domain benchmark pairing natural Reddit recommendation-seeking conversations with Amazon product listings. LightCRS achieves state-of-the-art average HR@1, NDCG@5, and MRR, improving HR@1 by 35.4\% over the strongest baseline while reducing per-query latency by 266 times. Joint multi-domain training itself improves HR@1 by 15.2\% over separately trained single-domain models.

</details>


### [145] [When Planning Problems Become Hard for LLMs: Separating Structural Complexity from Semantic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26964)
*Mayukh Debnath,Adeyinka Adedigba,Rammohan Mallipeddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文区分"结构难度"与"语义难度"两个概念：零训练的delete-relaxation启发式$h_{\mathrm{add}}$能高精度预测结构难度（mean $|\rho|=0.931$），远超学习型方法；而谓词重命名实验表明LLM规划成功率随语义改名从67.5%降至40.0%（拒绝率恒为0%），说明LLM的困难本质来自谓词语义而非结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM经典规划benchmark无法区分"实例本身的结构难度"与"解读符号词汇的语义难度"，导致对LLM失败原因的归因模糊；同时，已有工作倾向于构建学习的结构难度预测器与混合路由，但缺乏对"哪种预测器更有效"以及"难点究竟在哪"的实证检验。

Method: 在7个STRIPS域（以BFS最优解长度作为难度真值）上：(1) 比较零训练的delete-relaxation启发式$h_{\mathrm{add}}$与学习型结构预测器（ridge回归、object count、episodic retrieval架构ARC）在out-of-domain上的Spearman相关；(2) 语义隔离实验——在保持状态、转移与最优计划不变的前提下令谓词逐步改名（0%→100%），测量LLM有效规划成功率；(3) 用ARC的conditioning信息做in-context难度预测；(4) LLM与符号规划器的hybrid routing中对结构分数的校准分析。

Result: $h_{\mathrm{add}}$ 在held-out域上的平均 $|\rho|=0.931$，优于ridge（0.707）、object count（0.657）和ARC（0.400）；ARC甚至不优于使用相同特征的线性模型。谓词0%→100%改名时，LLM有效规划成功率从67.5%降至40.0%，但拒绝率始终为0%。提供ARC conditioning信息的LLM in-context预测达 $|\rho|=0.826$，超过训练过的ARC。混合路由效果更依赖对目标LLM结果的校准，而非所选结构分数。

Conclusion: 经典符号启发式已能充分刻画结构难度，开发更强的结构预测器收益有限；LLM规划失败主要由谓词语义敏感度导致。提升混合规划的重点应是建模LLM对谓词语义的行为特性，而非继续改进结构难度指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) struggle with classical planning, but benchmarks rarely distinguish structural difficulty from the difficulty of interpreting symbolic vocabulary. We examine seven STRIPS domains with BFS-optimal plan lengths. For predicting structural difficulty without solving an instance, the zero-training delete-relaxation heuristic $h_{\mathrm{add}}$ outperforms learned alternatives on held-out domains (mean Spearman $|ρ|=0.931$), including ridge regression ($0.707$), object count ($0.657$), and the episodic retrieval architecture ARC ($0.400$). ARC also fails to outperform a linear model using the same features. To isolate semantic effects, we progressively rename predicates while preserving states, transitions, and optimal plans. Valid LLM planning success drops from $67.5\%$ to $40.0\%$ between $0\%$ and $100\%$ renaming, while refusals remain at $0\%$. An LLM supplied with ARC's conditioning information predicts difficulty in context better than trained ARC (mean $|ρ|=0.826$). Hybrid routing between an LLM and a symbolic planner depends more on calibration to the target LLM's outcomes than on the structural score selected. Thus, established symbolic heuristics already estimate structural difficulty well; improving hybrid planning requires modeling LLM behavior sensitive to predicate semantics rather than developing more structural predictors.

</details>


### [146] [SPEM: Structure-Aware Procedural Memory for Efficient Skill Use in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26995)
*Ran Zhou,Zehui Kong,Fanyu Lin,Jiaheng Liu,Zhen Zhang,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPEM——一种结构感知的轻量程序性记忆框架，通过原子技能记忆与Skill-DAG记忆以及Skill-GNN实现高效准确的技能复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖可复用技能，但技能库扩展带来效率与准确性问题：全量加载token与运行时开销高；扁平语义检索将技能视为独立文本条目，忽视依赖、执行顺序、状态转移与数据流约束。

Method: SPEM维护Atomic Skill Memory（单项技能）与Skill-DAG Memory（由成功执行提炼的可复用组合）。新任务优先复用高置信度Skill-DAG模板；否则检索原子技能并用Skill-GNN进行状态与结构感知的技能选择；成功执行回写为新Skill-DAG，实现记忆随时间累积。

Result: 在ALFWorld与SkillsBench上取得最佳整体效果-效率权衡：ALFWorld成功率87.1%，SkillsBench奖励38.38%，同时相对强基线降低token与运行时成本。

Conclusion: 结构感知的程序性记忆可同时提升LLM智能体技能使用的准确性与效率，并通过执行回写实现能力持续积累。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on reusable skills. However, scaling skill libraries makes efficient and accurate skill use difficult. Full skill loading incurs high token and runtime overhead. In contrast, flat semantic retrieval treats skills as independent text entries, ignoring dependencies, execution order, state transitions, and data flow constraints. We propose SPEM, a lightweight structure-aware procedural memory framework for efficient skill use in LLM-based agents. SPEM maintains Atomic Skill Memory for individual skills and Skill-DAG Memory for reusable compositions derived from successful executions. For a new task, SPEM reuses high-confidence Skill-DAG templates when available. Otherwise, it retrieves atomic skills and invokes Skill-GNN for state- and structure-aware skill selection. Successful executions are written back as new Skill-DAGs, allowing procedural memory to accumulate over time. Experiments on ALFWorld and SkillsBench show that SPEM achieves the best overall effectiveness--efficiency trade-off, reaching 87.1% ALFWorld success and 38.38% SkillsBench reward while reducing token and runtime costs against strong baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Does Multi-Agent Collaboration Reduce Hallucination in Clinical Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27027)
*Won Seok Jang,Harry Bhaskar Joshi,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在多智能体LLM临床系统评估中，协作能提升任务性能，但不普遍降低幻觉；幻觉水平及变化方向主要由模型本身决定，而非拓扑结构或智能体数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估普遍只对最终答案计分，而临床医生依据推理过程行事，故"性能提升=更可信"是错误推断；需在推理链内部衡量协作是否真正减少幻觉、以及何种因素决定其效果。

Method: 在事实性与忠实性分类体系下，对4个模型×4个系统（1个单LLM基线+3种多智能体拓扑）×3个临床任务的36组比较，在系统生成的rationale内部对幻觉计分，并与任务指标并列报告；另做自纠正实验（向模型展示其被标记的错误）。

Result: 协作带来任务性能收益但并非规律性降低幻觉，且无任何拓扑稳定优于其他；36组比较中12组效应可分辨（4组下降、8组上升），无模型同时出现在两侧。模型而非拓扑或智能体数量决定幻觉水平及变化方向。自纠正实验中除Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct外所有模型均降低幻觉；协作降幻觉最多的模型获益最大，而协作放大幻觉最多的Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct完全不降。

Conclusion: 协作只是重新分配模型已有能力，并不能提供其不具备的能力；评估多智能体临床系统必须对推理过程计分，不能以任务性能代替可信度。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems improve performance on clinical tasks, and that improvement is often read as evidence of a more trustworthy system. However, the two are not the same claim. A clinician acts on the stated reasoning and not only on the conclusion, so whether collaboration reduces hallucination, and what decides when it does, has to be measured in the reasoning too. Yet most evaluations score only the final answer. We evaluate four systems (a single-LLM baseline and three multi-agent topologies) across four models and three clinical tasks, scoring hallucination, under a factuality and faithfulness taxonomy, inside the rationale each system generates and reporting it alongside the task metric. We report that collaboration buys task performance but not lower hallucination as a rule, and no topology is reliably better than another. The effect differs by model: of the $36$ model--system--task comparisons, $12$ are large enough to resolve --- four decreases and eight increases, with no model on both sides. What sets the level, and the direction of any change, is the model rather than the topology or the number of agents. A self-correction experiment is consistent with this: shown its own flagged errors, every model except Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct reduces hallucination; the one model for which collaboration lowers hallucination gains the most, and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, the model whose hallucination it amplifies most, does not reduce its hallucination at all. We find that collaboration redistributes what its models can already do; it does not supply what they cannot.

</details>


### [148] [Teaching AI Agent to Play Poker Strategically Through Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27028)
*Phurinat Polasa,Thanapon Thanadunpremdet,Phatthanasak Kraiduang,Saksorn Ruangtanusak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究紧凑型AI智能体能否在隐藏信息下表现出策略行为，并提出SPOKE方法，通过将教师相对价值惩罚、策略锚定、风险约束与结构化理由监督相结合进行偏好优化。实验表明SPOKE在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上取得最低可利用度，并在六人桌top-1一致性上表现最佳，同时保持与SFT相当的教师KL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑克智能体可能走出合法但策略性差的招法。作者希望回答两个问题：紧凑型AI智能体能否在隐藏信息下展现策略行为，以及偏好优化能否超越静态与零样本策略。此外，冻结语言模型在精确博弈评估与六人桌评估之间性能差异显著，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 提出SPOKE（带KL锚定均衡的策略偏好优化），融合四项技术：教师相对价值惩罚、策略锚定、风险约束、结构化理由监督；并将其与SFT、DPO、GRPO、GSPO进行对比。基线包括静态策略及SmallThinker、Qwen3、Gemma-3三个冻结语言模型。

Result: 在SmallThinker-4B-A0.6B策略的三次运行中，SPOKE在Kuhn取得最低平均可利用度0.213，在Leduc取得最低1.081，六人桌top-1一致性最高达0.775；教师KL为0.522，与SFT的0.523相当。

Conclusion: 结果提供初步证据表明，策略偏好优化可在保持与教师一致行为的同时提升策略质量。但结论仍属初步，需进一步验证其泛化性。

Abstract: A poker agent can make a legal move and still make a strategically poor one. We ask whether compact AI agents can exhibit strategic behavior under hidden information and whether preference optimization can improve beyond static and zero-shot policies. We first evaluate a static baseline and three frozen language models: SmallThinker, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 whose performance varies substantially between exact-game and six-player evaluation. We therefore propose Strategic Preference Optimization with KL-anchored Equilibrium (SPOKE), combining teacher-relative value penalties, policy anchoring, risk constraints, and structured rationale supervision, and compare it with SFT, DPO, GRPO, and GSPO. Across three runs of a SmallThinker-4B-A0.6B policy, SPOKE achieves the lowest mean exploitability in Kuhn (0.213) and Leduc (1.081) and the highest six-max top-1 agreement (0.775), while maintaining teacher KL comparable to SFT (0.522 vs 0.523). These results provide preliminary evidence that strategic preference optimization can improve strategic quality while preserving teacher-consistent behavior.

</details>


### [149] [Evolving Semantic Indexes for Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28568)
*Jinming Nian,Jyun-Yu Jiang,Henry Tsai,Yi Fang,Zhirong Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用LLM驱动的进化式程序搜索来优化生成式检索（GR）中的离散语义标识符（SID）设计，借助轻量代理解码器加速评估，在多个数据集上显著提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GR将检索/推荐建模为离散SID的自回归生成，SID设计（构建、冲突处理、解码、训练）由多个离散且相互依赖的组件构成，无法用梯度端到端优化，目前大多依赖人工设计，限制了GR质量。

Method: 用本地部署的开源31B模型对可执行的GR组件进行变异，实现LLM驱动的进化式程序搜索（不调用专有API）；用1M参数的代理解码器替代反复训练1B目标生成器以提供快速反馈；在固定训练配方下于NQ320K、MSMARCO300K及多语言电商数据集上搜索与验证。

Result: 在21个SID程序的NQ320K实验中，代理与目标模型的Recall@100呈强秩相关（Spearman ρ=0.90）；联合进化SID构建与冲突处理后，NQ320K上Recall@1从55.8%升至59.3%，Recall@100从85.8%升至90.6%；组件研究表明代理可靠性取决于被进化对象——语义索引上的改进可可靠迁移，而生成器训练目标与完整流程则不能。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的进化搜索是GR设计的实用工具，但前提是需针对所选搜索空间验证代理评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval (GR) casts search and recommendation as autoregressive generation of discrete semantic identifiers (SIDs). SID design largely determines GR quality, but its discrete, interdependent components (SID construction, collision handling, decoding, and training) cannot be optimized end-to-end with gradients and remain largely hand-designed. We introduce a framework that improves these components through LLM-driven evolutionary program search, to our knowledge the first application of this approach to GR. A locally served, open-weight 31B model mutates executable GR components, so no proprietary LLM API is used, and a 1M-parameter proxy decoder provides fast feedback in place of repeatedly training the 1B target generator. Across 21 SID programs on NQ320K, proxy and target Recall@100 show strong rank correlation (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Guided by this proxy, evolution improves the seed GR on NQ320K, MSMARCO300K, and a multilingual e-commerce dataset under a fixed training recipe. On NQ320K, jointly evolving SID construction and collision handling improves Recall@1 from 55.8% to 59.3% and Recall@100 from 85.8% to 90.6%. A component study shows that proxy reliability depends on what is evolved: improvements transfer reliably for the semantic index but not for the generator's training objective or the full pipeline. LLM-driven evolutionary search is thus a practical tool for GR design, provided proxy reliability is established for the chosen search space.

</details>


### [150] [M-Plan: Comprehensive Planning For LLM Agents with Memory Arrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27032)
*Ziyang Liu,Jiakang Yuan,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出闭环多智能体框架M-Plan，融合长短期记忆与全局-局部、过去-当前引导，提升无先验训练的长时程多轮任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在单轮任务表现优异，但在长时程、无先验训练环境中仍受限：单模型难以处理长上下文，连续决策与推理能力弱。现有带长期记忆的MAS多依赖知识复用或摘要，直接检索缺乏对当前任务的适配，且检索记忆中混杂大量历史噪声；而以任务特定引导为特征的MAS又缺少专为该引导设计的长期记忆。

Method: M-Plan为闭环MAS框架，整合长期与短期记忆，采用全局-局部、过去-当前双重引导，并保持即插即用设计。任务执行中，智能体获得任务特定的里程碑与洞见，仅在出现困惑时提供局部提示，二者共同构成全局-局部引导；引导的生成与使用也构成短期记忆。任务完成后，将当前任务相关信息优化或重组后写入长期记忆库，供未来检索。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop、PDDL等多轮任务及多种模型上的大量实验表明，M-Plan在准确率与效率上均优于多个基线方法。

Conclusion: M-Plan通过长短期记忆协同与多层次引导的闭环设计，区别于单一长期或短期记忆方案，形成完整框架，有效提升多轮长时程任务性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents have recently demonstrated outstanding performance in single-turn tasks, yet they continue to struggle with long-horizon tasks, particularly in environments without prior training. A key limitation of single LLMs is their difficulty in processing long contexts, which stems from weaknesses in continuous decision-making and reasoning capabilities. Although existing multi-agent systems (MAS) equipped with long-term memory bases can partially address this gap, they mostly rely on knowledge reuse or summarization, an approach that is often constrained by directly retrieval without any adaptation for the current task, while the retrieved memory may contain much history noises waiting to be preprocessed. In contrast, MAS characterized by task-specific guidance often lack long-term memory specifically designed for the guidance. To address these challenges, we propose M-Plan, a closed-loop MAS framework that integrates long-term and short-term memory. By combining global-local and past-current guidance, M-Plan enhances agents' performance on multi-turn tasks while maintaining a plug-and-play design. During task execution, agents receive task-specific milestones and insights, and are provided with local hints only when confusion arises, which form global-local guidance together. These guidance generation and utilization construct the short-term memory as well. After task completion, relevant information about the current task is optimized or reorganized before being sent to the long-term memory storage for future retrieval. The designments mentioned above build a comprehensive framework different from other individual long-term or short-term memory works. Extensive experiments on multi-turn tasks including ALFWorld, Webshop, and PDDL across diverse models demonstrate that M-Plan outperforms multiple baseline methods in both accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [151] [Physical AI: Concepts, Techniques, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27045)
*Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen,Tianfan Xue,Bin Zhao,Bingqi Jiang,Zihan Guo,Zicai Cui,Yang Li,Ming Zhou,Xinyuan Chen,Lihe Ding,Xiao Chen,Yujin Wang,Shi Guo,Junting Dong,Tao Lu,Xing Shen,Haoyu Guo,Yao Mu,Haiyong Yu,Haoxiang Ma,Fuxian Huang,Qiaojun Yu,Yunsong Zhou,Kailin Li,Zirui Wang,Yufei Xue,Weishuai Zeng,Tao Huang,Zhigang Wang,Junli Liu,Pengyuan Wu,Pingrui Zhang,Haotian Wang,Dongzhan Zhou,Jiafei Cao,Feng Yuan,Xinyi Ye,Quanli Xuan,Mulin Yu,Shangding Gu,Kaiwen Song,Dong Wang,Jiarui Li,Kehui Liu,Zhongjie Jia,Dongjiang Li,Jiacheng Bao,Haoran Yang,Zhaoqing Chen,Yiming Sun,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于物理人工智能（physical AI）的专著性综述，旨在统一界定物理AI并系统梳理其技术版图与核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI近年发展迅速并受到学界与产业界广泛关注，但该领域在定义、技术版图与核心挑战上尚未形成共识，本文以填补这一空白为动机。

Method: 提出统一框架：将物理AI定义为在真实世界约束下通过连续反馈感知、建模并作用于物理世界的人工系统；并将其与传统数字AI、具身AI、信息物理系统及数字孪生加以区分；随后系统梳理其物理、控制、认知、学习与推理基础，以及多模态感知、物理数据与仿真、世界模型、具身基础模型、运动控制系统、多智能体协同、软硬件协同设计等方法。

Result: 覆盖机器人、制造、基础设施、科学实验与自主服务等应用，并重点讨论仿真到现实迁移、可靠性、实时执行、评测、安全、治理与长期部署。

Conclusion: 通过将感知、预测、决策、行动与适应闭环连接，本文识别了从窄域自动化走向更可泛化的物理AI乃至物理通用人工智能（Physical AGI）所需的技术挑战与研究方向。

Abstract: In recent years, physical artificial intelligence (physical AI) has experienced rapid development, capturing significant attention in academia and industry. However, the field has yet to reach consensus on its definition, technical landscape, and core challenges. To address this gap, this monograph seeks to define physical AI, present its technical landscape, and examine each key technical subfield in depth. In doing so, it establishes a unified framework for Physical AI: artificial systems that perceive, model, and act upon the physical world through continuous feedback under real-world constraints. Further, this monograph distinguishes physical AI from conventional digital AI, embodied AI, cyber-physical systems, and digital twins. It develops the foundations of physical AI across physics, control, cognition, learning, and reasoning. It reviews key methodologies, including multimodal perception, physical data and simulation, world models, embodied foundation models, motor-control systems, multi-agent coordination, and hardware-software co-design. Furthermore, it examines applications spanning robotics, manufacturing, infrastructure, scientific experimentation, and autonomous services. Particular attention is given to sim-to-real transfer, reliability, real-time execution, evaluation, safety, governance, and long-term deployment. By connecting perception, prediction, decision-making, action, and adaptation in a closed loop, this monograph identifies the technical challenges and research directions required to advance from narrow automation toward more generalizable physical AI and, ultimately, physical artificial general intelligence (Physical AGI).

</details>


### [152] [ReuF2F: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Formalized Undergraduate Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27046)
*Zeyu Zheng,Yiyang Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Congmin Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Jianian Chen,Kangning Zhang,Kayue Daniel Wong,Lingyu Yang,Rong Shan,Shihao Chen,Thomas Zhu,Wentao Zhang,Xiaopeng Li,Yan Hu,Yongxi Lin,Yuxiang Chen,Zhuoying Ou,Jianghao Lin,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Jingbang Chen,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReuF2F——一个包含 244 个来自本科生科研体验（REU）项目的 Lean 4 开放问题形式化基准，配合 Docker 化的编码智能体评测设定与 Leanimum-agent 统一测试框架，用于端到端开放问题求解的留出评估；最优模型仅解出约 40%，仍有很大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 系统开始解决开放研究问题，可靠验证愈发关键；形式数学提供了可信的机器可检查框架。但现有形式数学基准多聚焦已知结果或少数知名开放问题，一旦问题被解决并广泛传播，就难以再作为可靠评测；且既有评测协议与现代编码智能体的实际运行方式不完全一致。

Method: 从 REU 项目中收集并形式化 244 个开放问题，覆盖多个数学子领域与入门级开放研究问题；采用标准编码智能体评测设定，将每个问题及其逻辑否定分别置入 Docker 容器中的 Lean 项目；基于 mini-SWE-agent 构建 Leanimum-agent，提供统一评测 harness 以保证跨模型公平比较。

Result: 最强模型仅解决约 40% 的问题（约 40% 的解决率），说明形式化开放研究问题上仍有显著改进空间。

Conclusion: 相对冷门、关注度较低、可能长期未解的 REU 开放问题更适合作为端到端 Lean 开放问题求解的留出评测集；ReuF2F 与 Leanimum-agent 为这一方向提供了可复现、可比较的评测基础。

Abstract: As AI systems begin to resolve open research problems, reliable verification becomes increasingly important. Formal mathematics provides a trusted framework for mathematical reasoning, enabling agents to produce machine-checkable proofs while conducting mathematical exploration. Existing formal mathematics benchmarks often focus on known results or a small set of prominent open problems, which can become difficult to evaluate reliably once solutions are discovered and widely disseminated. Existing evaluation protocols are also not always aligned with how modern coding agents operate. We therefore introduce ReuF2F, a Lean 4 benchmark of 244 formalized open problems drawn from Research Experiences for Undergraduates (REU), spanning entry-level open research problems across a broad range of mathematical subfields. We expect these less prominent open problems, which tend to attract less attention and may remain unsolved for longer, to be better suited for held-out evaluation of end-to-end open problem resolution in Lean. We adopt a standard coding-agent evaluation setting, placing each problem and its logical negation into a Lean project in a Docker container. We also build Leanimum-agent on mini-SWE-agent to provide a unified harness for fair comparisons across models. Our evaluation finds that even the best-performing model resolves only ~40% of the problems, leaving substantial room for improvement on formalized open research problems.

</details>


### [153] [A general-purpose System One decision model for clinical records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27099)
*Andrew Zhang,Trinh T. L. Vuong,Sophia J. Wagner,Dongmin Bang,Tong Ding,Yuchang Su,Mary Cipperman,Christina S. Chen,Judith Lefkes,Ramin Kahidi,Ming Y. Lu,Caiwei Tian,Tianhao Luo,Jingnan Ma,Rowland Pettit,Long Phi Le,Faisal Mahmood*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出40亿参数临床决策模型CS1-4B及两种智能体框架，在留出与外部数据集上以远小规模超越大型开放决策模型，并与语言模型分工实现高效人群级试验筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从患者记录中提取可靠答案对临床照护、卫生系统运营与生物医学发现至关重要，但面向全人群应用多样化临床问题仍计算代价高昂；需要兼顾准确性、速度与可审查性的方案。

Method: 构建40亿参数决策模型CS1（CS1-4B），训练数据为多医院卫生系统3.6百万患者的12.4百万问答，融合结构化数据键、观测结局与语言模型监督（覆盖临床笔记、诊断报告、患者时间线）；单次前向传播即可回答多类型问题，无需任务特定重训，每次输出概率以路由不确定病例至人工复核；并设计两种与通用语言模型协作的智能体框架。

Result: 在963,408名留出患者上，CS1-4B在14项任务中的13项（独立于其训练教师评分）优于最高270亿参数的开源决策模型；21个外部数据集上总体性能接近最强测试的270亿模型，匹配负载下推理快6.4倍；与语言模型智能体结合后，可对多年完整病史给出带引用的答案，并按17项注册试验的入组标准筛查人群，前20名候选中被判为符合条件者数量为关键词检索的4倍，且消耗的语言模型token少690倍。

Conclusion: 结果支持临床AI中的分工范式：小型决策模型负责重复性记录读取，生成式模型负责提出问题与综合证据。

Abstract: Clinical care, health-system operations and biomedical discovery depend on extracting reliable answers from patient records, yet applying diverse clinical questions across entire populations remains computationally expensive. Here we present Clinical System One (CS1), a 4-billion-parameter decision model for fast, typed judgments from clinical records, along with two agentic harnesses in which CS1 works together with a generalist language model. CS1-4B is trained on 12.4 million questions and answers from 3.6 million patients across a multi-hospital health system, combining structured-data keys, observed outcomes and language-model supervision across clinical notes, diagnostic reports and patient timelines. It answers multiple typed questions in a single forward pass without task-specific retraining, and each answer carries a probability that can route uncertain cases to human review. On 963,408 held-out patients, CS1-4B outperformed open decision models up to 27 billion parameters on 13 of 14 tasks scored independently of its training teacher. Across 21 external data sets, its aggregate performance approached that of the strongest tested 27-billion-parameter model, with 6.4-fold faster inference in a matched workload. Integrating CS1-4B with a language model agent produced cited answers over complete multi-year patient histories and screened a patient population against the eligibility criteria of 17 registered clinical trials, placing four times as many judged-eligible patients among its top 20 candidates as keyword search while using 690-fold fewer language-model tokens than a language model reading every record. These findings support a division of labour in clinical AI, with small decision models performing repeated record reading and generative models formulating questions and synthesizing evidence.

</details>


### [154] [Omni-Woodpecker: Hallucination Correction via Evidence-Prior Rebalancing for Omni LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27103)
*Zijian Liu,Chaoyou Fu,Yan Yang,Caifeng Shan,Ran He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 Omni-Woodpecker (OWP)，将全模态大模型的跨模态幻觉缓解从输出层操纵转向冲突条件下的表征重平衡，通过正交分解分离证据与先验并抑制误导性先验，在多个基准上显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Omni LLM 在联合音视频推理上表现出色，但跨模态幻觉仍是主要挑战。已有缓解方法多作用于输出层，忽视了融合表征中"grounding 证据"与"学习先验"相互纠缠的问题，因此需要在表征层面进行干预。

Method: OWP 为免训练框架：首先从互补的模态专属视角识别跨模态冲突；随后用正交分解将隐藏状态中的 grounding 证据与竞争性先验分离；最后在解码前自适应重平衡融合表征，抑制误导性先验并恢复被忽略的证据。

Result: 以 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 为骨干，OWP 在 CMM 和 AVHBench 上分别提升 12.9 和 8.6 分；在四个通用 OLLM 推理基准上稳定获得 0.67–2.25 分增益；并且能泛化到 VideoLLaMA2-AV，在同两个基准上提升 4.6 和 3.9 分。

Conclusion: OWP 通过冲突条件下的表征重平衡有效缓解跨模态幻觉，具备免训练、效果显著和跨架构泛化强的特点，验证了表征层干预思路的可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Recent Omni LLMs (OLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in joint audio-visual reasoning, yet cross-modal hallucinations remain a major challenge. Existing mitigation methods mainly operate at the output level, overlooking the entanglement of grounding evidence and learned priors within fused representations. To address this issue, we propose Omni-Woodpecker (OWP), a training-free framework that shifts hallucination mitigation from output-level manipulation to conflict-conditioned representation rebalancing. Specifically, OWP first identifies cross-modal conflicts from complementary modality-specific views. It then employs orthogonal decomposition to separate grounding evidence from competing priors within the hidden states. Finally, OWP adaptively rebalances fused representations by suppressing misleading priors and restoring ignored evidence before decoding. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and strong cross-architecture generalization of OWP. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the backbone, OWP improves performance by 12.9 and 8.6 points on CMM and AVHBench, respectively, while consistently achieving gains of 0.67-2.25 points across four general OLLM reasoning benchmarks. Furthermore, OWP generalizes effectively to VideoLLaMA2-AV, improving performance by 4.6 and 3.9 points on the same benchmarks. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZiiJianLiu/Omni-Woodpecker

</details>


### [155] [PROVISO: Provenance-Informed Action Selection for Secure LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27115)
*Bo Yan,Qi Zhang,Ziwen Kan,Fnu Suya,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PROVISO：一种在动作执行前基于来源类型（provenance）追踪与备选动作比较的LLM智能体动作提交控制器，在安全性与实用性上同时优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过工具和第三方技能作用于外部世界，单次执行的动作可能造成不可逆伤害。现有防御存在两个缺陷：(1) 判断提议动作时不追踪其内容来自用户还是工具输出等外部来源；(2) 孤立地判断该动作，而非与可用备选动作对比，从而被迫在安全与效用之间取舍。

Method: PROVISO在智能体提出动作之后、执行之前决定提交哪个动作。它将可信观测、外部观测、提议动作与备选动作编码进带来源类型（provenance-typed）的记忆，通过角色特定的查询建立关联，并提交从结果中学习到的校准价值最高的动作。

Result: 在覆盖智能体生命周期的四项智能体安全任务上，没有基线在安全性与实用性两方面同时超过PROVISO。PROVISO在SkillsBench上F1比最强基线提升13.84点，在ToolSafe上提升7.07点，并在另外三个技能检测基准上达到88–99 F1。

Conclusion: 通过来源追踪与备选比较的联合建模，PROVISO缓解了安全—效用的权衡，可作为执行前的通用动作提交控制层，且在多类安全任务上表现稳健。

Abstract: LLM agents act on the external world through tools and third-party skills, so a single executed action can cause irreversible harm. Existing defenses judge a proposed action without tracking whether its content comes from the user or from external sources such as tool outputs, and they judge it in isolation rather than against an available alternative, which forces a trade-off between security and utility. We present PROVISO, a controller that decides which action to commit after the agent proposes an action and before it is executed. PROVISO encodes trusted observations, external observations, the proposed action, and an alternative into provenance-typed memory, relates them through role-specific queries, and commits the action with the highest calibrated value learned from outcomes. On four agent security tasks spanning the agent lifecycle, no baseline exceeds PROVISO in both security and utility. PROVISO improves F1 over the strongest baseline by 13.84 points on SkillsBench and by 7.07 points on ToolSafe, and it reaches 88-99 F1 on three additional skill detection benchmarks.

</details>


### [156] [PORA: Learning When More Reasoning Pays Off for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27119)
*Bo Yan,Qi Zhang,Zhen Tan,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Paired Outcome-guided Reasoning Allocation (PORA)，通过在同一交互状态下对比短/长推理的结果来学习"何时值得长推理"，在推理预算受限下按剩余预算动态分配思考长度，在应用、记忆、代码、零售、数学任务上均取得强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在多步任务中总推理预算有限且需支撑后续步骤，每步动作前须在"短推理"与"长推理"间权衡：长推理可能减少后续错误、提升最终任务质量，但会消耗未来决策所需预算。由此产生两个难点：估计长推理的下游价值，以及判断该价值是否值得动用剩余预算。

Method: PORA以同一交互状态为起点，分别用短推理额度与长推理额度各完成一次任务（成对采样），比较两者的最终质量与总处理开销，从而学习长推理的价值估计。推理阶段用控制器将该价值估计与学习得到的"剩余预算压力价格"加权，直接择优选择短或长推理，无需并行执行两种方案。

Result: 在应用、记忆、代码、零售、数学任务上均表现强劲；BFCL memory上比最强基线高17.4–32.9个百分点；在代码与数学任务中，与同一agent"始终长推理"相比效果持平或更优，同时分别减少50.6%的actor token与31.2%的模型请求。

Conclusion: 将推理长度分配建模为"下游价值估计+预算压力定价"的决策问题，并通过成对结果对比进行学习，是提升LLM agent预算效率的有效途径：可在不损失甚至提升任务质量的前提下显著降低token与请求开销。

Abstract: LLM agents perform tasks over multiple steps and revise their actions based on feedback. As the total reasoning budget is often limited and must support future steps, the agent must choose between short and long reasoning before each action. Longer reasoning may improve the final task outcome by preventing later errors, but it also uses budget capacity that may be needed for future decisions. This creates two challenges: estimating the downstream value of long reasoning and deciding when that value is worth using the remaining budget. We propose Paired Outcome-guided Reasoning Allocation (PORA), which learns this decision by comparing short and long reasoning from the same interaction state. During training, \method{} completes the task twice from each selected state, once with a short reasoning allowance and once with a long allowance, and compares their final quality and total processing to estimate when longer reasoning is valuable. During inference, a controller weighs this estimate against learned prices on remaining-budget pressure and chooses either short or long reasoning without running both alternatives. We evaluate PORA across application, memory, code, retail, and mathematics tasks, achieving strong results across all settings. On BFCL memory, it exceeds the strongest existing method by 17.4--32.9 percentage points. Within the same agent, it matches or exceeds always using long reasoning on code and mathematics with 50.6% fewer actor tokens and 31.2% fewer model requests, respectively.

</details>


### [157] [EDMA: Entropy-Driven Multimodal Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27120)
*Emanuele Mezzi,Gertjan Burghouts,Fabio Massacci,Mengyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EDMA，用逻辑熵把多模态问答形式化为迭代的熵缩减过程，量化每种模态对区分正确/错误候选答案的贡献，从而构建可量化的答题轨迹并选择熵最小的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型方法虽通过拆解中间步骤提升性能，但未量化每种模态对最终答案的贡献；作者认为量化模态贡献能提升过程可追溯性与最终性能。

Method: EDMA 包含三部分：(i) 模态条件划分，估计单模态熵缩减；(ii) 熵驱动的多模态融合，捕获跨模态互补信息；(iii) 基于熵的轨迹选择，选取逻辑熵最小的答题轨迹。

Result: 在标准 MMQA 基准上优于提示类基线：相对 Planning Prompting 平均提升 12.8 F1 与 4.6 LLM-J 点，相对 Chain-of-Thought 提升 18.6 F1 与 8.3 LLM-J 点；优于 self-consistency 基线，与多智能体系统基线相当。并验证高熵与更多假阳性相关，逻辑熵可作为正确推理路径的单调选择器。

Conclusion: 逻辑熵是有效的性能代理指标，可作为选择正确推理路径的可靠依据，熵驱动的模态贡献量化能提升 MMQA 的可追溯性与准确率。

Abstract: Multimodal question answering (MMQA) is based on integrating heterogeneous data sources, selectively leveraging relevant modalities, and ignoring distractors. Existing approaches based on Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) improve performance by splitting the task into intermediate steps, but do not quantify how each modality contributes to reaching the final answer. We argue that quantifying the effect that each modality has on the answering process improves process traceability and final performance. Thus, we propose Entropy-Driven Multimodal Answering (EDMA), which uses logical entropy to formalise multimodal QA as an iterative process of entropy reduction and to quantify the contribution of each modality in distinguishing correct from incorrect candidate answers. This enables the construction of quantifiable answering trajectories, where each step is associated with a measurable entropy reduction. EDMA introduces (i) modality-conditioned partitioning to estimate unimodal entropy reduction, (ii) entropy-driven multimodal fusion to capture complementary information across modalities, and (iii) entropy-based selection of the answering trajectory that minimises the most logical entropy. Evaluated across standard MMQA benchmarks, EDMA outperforms competitive prompting baselines, with average gains of 12.8 F1 and 4.6 LLM-J points over Planning Prompting and 18.6 F1 and 8.3 LLM-J points over Chain-of-Thought. Moreover, EDMA outperforms self-consistency baselines and is competitive with the multi-agent system baseline. Moreover, we show that higher entropy correlates with more false positives and, through controlled trajectory selection, demonstrate that logical entropy serves as an effective, monotonic selector for correct reasoning paths, validating entropy level as a reliable proxy for performance.

</details>


### [158] [MoSAIK: Motion Search with Artificial Intelligence in K-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27156)
*Oscar Dabrowski,Abijith Jagannath,Frédéric Grouiller,Sébastien Courvoisier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoSAIK直接从标准多线圈TSE原始k空间回顾性估计平面内刚体运动参数，并用于运动感知的模型化重建，实现无需参考或导航的回溯性运动校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维T2加权TSE脑MRI中的运动伪影限制诊断信心并常导致重复扫描，现有方法依赖无运动参考、外部导航或额外采集。

Method: 将选定复数线圈数据表示为对数幅度与相位通道，采用解耦的卷积嵌入与Transformer编码器估计按相位编码行索引的旋转与二维平移；训练使用无运动临床数据加仿真运动，并以模型化、运动感知重建替代图像合成。

Result: 旋转MSE<0.2 deg²、平移MSE<0.2像素²，Pearson相关约0.96与0.94；全部在体采集均超过75%层面相对未校正重建获得信息增益，伪影减少且解剖细节保留。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的k空间运动轨迹估计可实现标准临床TSE脑MRI的快速回顾性校正。

Abstract: Object:
  Motion artefacts remain a major limitation of routine two-dimensional T2-weighted turbo spin-echo (TSE) brain MRI, reducing diagnostic confidence and often necessitating repeated acquisitions. MoSAIK (Motion Search with AI in K-space) retrospectively estimates in-plane rigid-body motion directly from standard multi-coil TSE k-space. Without a motion-free reference, external navigators, or additional acquisitions, MoSAIK infers motion from k-space self-consistency violations and incorporates predicted parameters into model-based, motion-aware reconstruction rather than synthesizing corrected images.
  Materials and Methods:
  Selected complex coil data were represented as log-magnitude and phase channels. A decoupled convolutional embedding and Transformer encoder estimated phase-encoding-line-indexed rotation and two-dimensional translations. Training used motion-free clinical acquisitions with simulated motion; four held-out subjects were evaluated. Component-wise errors assessed estimation; SSIM and PSNR in silico and entropy focus criterion (EFC) in vivo assessed reconstruction.
  Results:
  Motion estimation errors were low for both rotation (MSE $<$ 0.2 deg$^2$) and translation (MSE $<$ 0.2 pixels$^2$), with Pearson correlations of approximately 0.96 and 0.94, respectively. In every in-vivo acquisition, more than 75% of slices showed information gain relative to uncorrected reconstructions. MoSAIK reduced visible artefacts while preserving anatomical detail.
  Discussion:
  Transformer-based k-space motion-trajectory estimation enables fast retrospective correction of standard clinical TSE brain MRI.

</details>


### [159] [Recoverability and Verifier Strength: Two Measured Preconditions for Unattended Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27465)
*De Wang,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"验证器强度σ"与"可恢复性"两个可测量指标，主张只有两者都达标的自改进步骤才能无人值守运行；实验显示LLM评委可"健康却什么都批"，且只有保留旧版本的变更才能真回滚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent平台已开始自动读取执行轨迹、生成变更、由自动验证器批准并发布，全程无人参与。但"这个闭环能安全无人化到什么程度"缺乏可度量依据，尤其LLM评委作为验证器时其可靠性未知，回滚能否真正还原旧行为也未经验证。

Method: 形式化定义两个量：验证器强度σ=可用验证器（常为LLM评委）与可信参考验证器的一致程度，它限制单步可提升幅度；可恢复性=撤销坏变更的代价，包括是否需要重新获得验证器批准、回滚能恢复多少旧行为。判据为二者皆充分才可无人值守。据此构建应用该规则的平台，并在平台自有审批门与τ²-bench基准上做受控实验，另设计两个简单测试检测验证器失效。

Result: 验证器强度：平台LLM评委在37个设置中有9个对任何输入给出相同分数且不报错，即验证器可"表面正常却一律批准"，两个简单测试即可捕获。可恢复性：恢复已保存副本可将agent还原至原始行为，而重新生成的撤销操作不能；某变更在线期间使17%任务的结果偏离正常波动范围，且任何撤销都无法修复。

Conclusion: 规则得到支持：变更只有在其旧版本被保留、且验证器强度已被实测时才可无人值守。据此平台允许prompt、工具与skill编辑无人值守，权重更新仍需人工签核。

Abstract: Agent platforms are starting to improve their own agents: they read a deployed agent's execution traces, derive a change, have an automated verifier approve it, and release it with no human author. How much of this loop can safely run without a human? We propose two quantities a platform can measure. Verifier strength $σ$ is how well the available verifier -- often an LLM judge -- agrees with a trusted reference verifier; it limits how much a step can improve the agent. Recoverability is how cheaply a bad change can be undone: whether undoing it needs a new verifier approval, and how much of the old behavior a rollback restores. A step should run unattended only if both are adequate. On our platform this rule allows prompt, tool and skill edits to run unattended and keeps weight updates under human sign-off.
  Our main contribution is these two definitions and how to measure them. We built a platform that applies the rule and tested it in two ways: on the platform's own approval gate, and in controlled experiments on the $τ^2$-bench agent benchmark. On verifier strength, the platform's LLM judge sometimes gave the same score no matter what it was shown (9 of 37 settings) and reported no error, so a verifier can look healthy while approving anything; its strength must be measured, and two simple tests catch the failure. On recoverability, restoring a saved copy brought the agent back to its original behavior, but a regenerated undo did not, so only changes whose old version is kept can be reversed without a human; and while a change was deployed, it altered the outcome of 17 percent of tasks beyond normal run-to-run variation, which no undo repairs. Together these support the rule: a change can run unattended only when its old version is kept and its verifier's strength has been measured.

</details>


### [160] [Learned Control Variates for Rare Grid-Outage Estimation at Electric Vehicle Charging Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27159)
*Sulyman Iyanda,Mansur Arief*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于学习分类器的控制变量（CV）方法降低馈线过载罕见事件蒙特卡洛估计的仿真次数，并证明方差缩减由学习器的召回率与假阳性率显式决定，在印尼泗水真实网络中将全市停电天数降低约三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车公共充电给配电馈线带来大规模同步负荷，馈线过载后果严重（变压器应力、连锁停电），规划者需估计极小的停电概率。单纯蒙特卡洛配合昂贵的电网仿真器需要天文数字的运行次数，成本不可接受。

Method: 构建由停电分类器（利用温度、负荷预测等廉价可观测特征训练）导出的控制变量估计器；理论证明对任意二分类学习器，方差缩减量是其召回率与假阳性率的显式函数；并比较高斯负荷代理CV与学习型CV在稀有事件极限下的行为。实验使用馈线过载仿真器覆盖三个数量级的停电概率，并在泗水馈线网络上做规划应用。

Result: 高斯代理CV在停电变得越稀有时的效果衰减，而具有非消失精度的学习器即使在极端稀有事件区间仍能消除固定比例的方差；即使仅用单一特征的弱学习器也可消除约25%方差，提升树学习器可消除约60%；泗水中高斯负荷模型使每条馈线达到十日一停目标的3–7倍，同等仿真预算下用学习CV加95%置信限规划备用容量，全市停电天数较蒙特卡洛减少约三分之一。

Conclusion: 学习型控制变量能有效缓解稀有停电事件的仿真成本瓶颈，帮助规划者设计对罕见过载仍保持可靠的配电网；其方差缩减可由学习器性能指标预测，具备理论保证和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Public charging of electric vehicles (EVs) adds a large and synchronized load to the distribution feeders. In most networks, it is rare to overload a feeder at the same time. When it does happen, the consequences can be severe: an overloaded feeder stresses its transformers and can trigger cascading outages across a city. Planners who design for high reliability therefore need to estimate very small outage probabilities. To that end, plain Monte Carlo estimation with an expensive grid simulator can require a prohibitively large number of runs for this task. To reduce the number of runs, we propose a control variate (CV) built from a learned classifier of the outage, trained on cheap observable features such as temperature and load forecasts. We show that for any binary learner, the variance reduction is an explicit function of its recall and false-positive rate. Furthermore, we show that control variate based on a Gaussian proxy of the load loses its effect as outages become rarer, while a learner with non-vanishing precision keeps removing a fixed fraction of the variance even in the extreme rare-event regime. We verify these results in numerical experiments using a feeder-overload simulator across three orders of magnitude of outage probability and obtain that even weak learner with a single feature can remove a quarter of the variance, while a boosted-tree learner is able to remove about 60%. We then apply the estimator to the feeder network of Surabaya, Indonesia, in which the Gaussian load model leaves every feeder three to seven times the one-day-in-ten-years blackout target. At the same simulation budget, planning the reserve with the learned CV and a 95% confidence limit reduces the city-wide blackout days by a third compared with Monte Carlo. These results show that learned control variates can help planners design distribution grids that stay reliable against rare outages.

</details>


### [161] [LSA-PAST: Layer-Specific Adaptation and Prototype Transport for Compositional Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27203)
*Kyle Stein,Andrew Arash Mahyari,Guillermo Francia,Eman El-Sheikh,Jiangpeng He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LSA-PAST参数高效方法用于组合增量学习（composition-IL），通过深度专属LoRA适配器与原型锚定的适配器空间传输，实现无回放的类增量学习并取得最优的精度-遗忘权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实问题常具组合性：视觉概念由状态（如red、shiny）与物体（如shoe、car）等基元组合而成。标准CIL把每个组合当作独立类，忽略共享基元，且缺乏在新组合到来时稳定基元表示的机制，导致参数更新使基元纠缠、产生漂移，损害旧组合性能。

Method: LSA-PAST由两部分组成：(1) LSA（Layer-Specific Adaptation）：在冻结的ViT中引入LoRA，并按深度分别为状态、物体、组合设置专门化适配器；(2) PAST（Prototype-Anchored Adapter-Space Transport）：用当前任务数据拟合无回放的仿射变换，将过去锚定的原型映射到当前适配器空间，使旧原型与新适配器保持兼容。

Result: 大量实验表明，LSA-PAST在composition-IL上取得了当前最优（SOA）的精度-遗忘权衡表现。

Conclusion: 将组合增量学习形式化为在任务间引入新状态-物体组合的设定，并证明参数高效、无回放的适配器深度特化加原型空间传输可有效缓解基元漂移与遗忘，为组合式类增量学习提供有效方案。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) enables models to recognize new classes over time while retaining prior knowledge, but many real-world problems are compositional, where visual concepts arise from combinations of primitives such as states (i.e., red, shiny) and objects (i.e., shoe, car). Standard CIL methods treat each composition as an independent class, ignoring shared primitives and providing no mechanism to stabilize their representations as new compositions arrive. This causes parameter updates to entangle primitives and induce drift that degrades performance on earlier compositions. Compositional Incremental Learning (composition-IL) formalizes this setting by introducing new state-object compositions across tasks. In this paper, we propose Layer-Specific Adaptation with Prototype-Anchored Adapter-Space Transport (LSA-PAST), a parameter-efficient method tailored to composition-IL. LSA integrates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) into a frozen ViT with depth-specialized adapters for states, objects, and compositions. PAST then maps past, anchored prototypes into the current adapter space through a rehearsal-free affine transform fit on current-task data, enabling old prototypes to remain compatible with new adapters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LSA-PAST achieves state-of-the-art (SOA) accuracy-forgetting trade-off performance for composition-IL.

</details>


### [162] [Sparse RL Relaxation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27220)
*Jiabin Fan,Matthias Lindemann,Zita Marinho,André FT Martins,Lili Mou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpaRLax，一种对软思维链（soft CoT）采用稀疏松弛的强化学习方法，利用跨时间步的可微性传播梯度，并用entmax自适应稀疏化，在九个推理基准上取得最高平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软CoT以连续表示替代离散token，理论上可在单步内表示多种推理可能性的叠加，但训练大模型实现这一潜力仍很困难。现有RL式训练把软token当作MDP中的动作而非可微中间计算步骤，因此不跨软token传播梯度，丢失了"前一个软token选择如何影响下一个"的信号。

Method: 提出SpaRLax，利用软CoT跨时间步的可微性进行稀疏松弛式RL训练，使训练更稳定有效；并采用entmax稀疏化方法自适应决定"软"的程度——在置信步骤截断长尾噪声，在不确定时保留多个合理候选。

Result: 在九个推理基准上，平均性能优于各基线方法；进一步分析验证了软token叠加的有效使用，并证明自适应稀疏性的贡献。

Conclusion: 将软token视为可微中间计算步骤而非离散动作，并配合自适应稀疏化，是可稳定、有效训练软CoT推理的可行路径。

Abstract: Recent work has explored continuous representations in place of discrete tokens during Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, often referred to as soft CoT. This is motivated by the idea that a mixture of token embeddings can represent a superposition of multiple reasoning possibilities within a single step. However, it remains challenging to train large language models to realize this potential. Existing work has explored reinforcement learning (RL)-style training for soft CoT. However, these approaches treat soft tokens as actions in a Markov decision process rather than as differentiable intermediate computation steps. Thus, they do not propagate gradients across the soft tokens and miss out on the signal of how the choice of one soft token affects the next. In this paper, we propose a sparse relaxation method for RL (called SpaRLax), which leverages the differentiability of soft-CoT reasoning over time steps and makes training more stable and effective. We adopt an entmax sparsity method that adaptively determines the degree of softness, which truncates long-tail noise at confident steps while retaining multiple plausible candidates when uncertain. Across nine reasoning benchmarks, our method achieves the highest average performance compared with baseline methods. Further analyses validate the effective use of soft-token superposition in our approach, and also demonstrate the contribution of adaptive sparsity in our approach.

</details>


### [163] [Magnolia: A Persistent-Memory Agent Framework for Computational Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27230)
*Tao Jiang,Benjamin Navet,Yousouf Taghzouti,Fabien Gandon,Louis-Félix Nothias,Élise Dumont*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了持久记忆AI智能体框架Magnolia，采用会话级—项目级—跨项目规则的三级记忆体系，整合对接、量子化学、分子动力学等计算流程与LLM，在四个案例中追踪317次运行、积累116条项目级条目并提升4条跨项目规则，回放实验中带记忆运行通过率显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学科学进入高数据量阶段，高通量平台与计算研究产生大量分子数据库，自动化推动化学走向数字化与精准化；科研人员需在日益增长的数据与结论（含负面结果）中被引导，因此需要专用的科学智能体来整合计算协议、实验记录与对话信息。

Method: 构建Magnolia持久记忆LLM智能体框架：集成对接至量子化学的计算协议、实验室笔记本与聊天记录；采用三级记忆系统——会话级观察自动捕获并蒸馏为结构化学习条目，经确认提升为项目级条目，在人类验证后升格为跨项目规则以主动指导后续工作并提供化学反馈；在四个案例（芳香体系量子化学评估COMPAS数据库、丁烷与修饰DNA的分子动力学、蛋白—肽对接）上验证，并进行四项任务的受控回放对比实验。

Result: 在四个项目中追踪317次运行，累积116条项目级条目，从中提炼4条跨项目规则；四项任务受控回放中，带记忆的运行通过检查而无记忆运行失败，例如对接配置检查10/10通过，对照组仅1/10至4/10通过。

Conclusion: 通过结合自动化、可重复性与人在环控制，Magnolia框架简化了对接、分子动力学与量子化学计算，并提供直接分析与LLM增强反馈，加速计算（生物）化学的获取与应用。

Abstract: Chemistry has recently entered a high-data regime as molecular databases from high-throughput platforms or computational studies have become available. Increasing automation within the chemical sciences leads to a more digital and precision chemistry. Dedicated scientific agents are becoming central to guiding the chemist through an ever-increasing amount of data and findings, including negative ones. Here, we introduce Magnolia, a persistent-memory artificial intelligence agent framework that integrates computational protocols, from docking to quantum chemistry, lab notebooks and chat logs relying on large language models (LLMs). It relies on a three-tier memory system: session-level observations are automatically captured and distilled into structured learnings, confirmed and elevated to project-tier entries and, when human-validated, promoted to cross-project rules that actively guide subsequent work and provide direct chemical feedback. We illustrate Magnolia on four case studies: quantum-chemical evaluation of aromatic systems (COMPAS database), molecular dynamics of butane and of modified DNA, and a protein-peptide docking campaign. Across these projects, Magnolia tracked 317 runs and accumulated 116 project-tier entries, from which four cross-project rules were promoted; in controlled replays of four tasks, runs with memory passed the checks that runs without it failed (for example, ten of ten docking-configuration checks, against one to four of ten). By combining automation, reproducibility, and human-in-the-loop control, our Magnolia framework streamlines docking, molecular dynamics and quantum chemistry calculations with direct analysis and LLM-augmented feedback, offering accelerated access to computational (bio)chemistry.

</details>


### [164] [EGI-BENCH: An Enterprise General Intelligence Benchmark Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27234)
*Zhiwei Liu,Zhujun Lan,Jielin Qiu,Zixiang Chen,Liangwei Yang,Ming Zhu,Akshara Prabhakar,Wenting Zhao,John Emmons,Jason Wu,Shafiq Rayhan Joty,Junnan Li,Shelby Heinecke,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发布 EGI-BENCH 统一智能体基准套件，整合八个企业级基准并提供统一 CLI 与两种运行时预算模式，同时提出六维 EGI 评分框架，对 14 个前沿/低成本模型进行了评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业采用 LLM 智能体工作流需跨 CRM 操作、异构文档检索、个人数据理解、多轮规划、工具调用、长程研究、长上下文软工等多项能力评估，但这些评测分散于不同代码库，运行器、依赖与评分标准各异，导致跨基准对比困难，单一汇总分数含义不清。

Method: 构建统一套件 EGI-BENCH，将 CRMARENA、HERB、PERSONABENCH、USERBENCH、MCP-UNIVERSE、MCPEVAL、BENCHFORCE、LOCOBENCH 八个基准整合到单一命令行接口，提供 quick/deep 两种运行时预算执行模式；并定义 EGI Score 六维框架（M1–M6），各维度由从各基准路由而来的细粒度子指标填充，而非采用基准级平均值。

Result: 在 quick 模式下评测 GPT、Claude、Gemini 家族共 14 个模型，EGI 总分区间为 39.6（GPT-4o mini）至 65.6（gpt-5.5）；无单一模型在所有能力轴上占优：gpt-5.5 在任务完成、规划与检索接地领先，Claude Sonnet 4.5 在工具执行与安全/弃答领先，Claude Haiku 4.5 在工程质量领先。

Conclusion: 统一套件与子指标路由使跨基准比较成为可能，并使聚合分数的语义更透明；结果显示企业级智能体能力呈多维互补格局，需按能力维度而非单一总分选型。代码已开源。

Abstract: Enterprise adoption of LLMs in agentic workflows is gated by evaluations that span a wide arc of abilities: CRM operations, heterogeneous-document retrieval, personal data understanding, multi-turn planning, tool use, function calling, long-horizon research, and long-context software engineering. These evaluations live today in separate repositories with divergent runners, dependencies, and scoring conventions, which makes cross-benchmark comparison difficult and obscures what a single aggregate number actually represents. We release EGI-BENCH, a unified LLM agent benchmark suite that consolidates eight enterprise facing agent benchmarks (CRMARENA, HERB, PERSONABENCH, USERBENCH, MCP-UNIVERSE, MCPEVAL, BENCHFORCE, LOCOBENCH) behind a single command-line interface with two runtime-budgeted execution modes. On top of the suite we define the Enterprise General Intelligence (EGI) Score, a six-dimensional framework (M1-M6) whose buckets are populated by fine-grained sub-metrics routed from each benchmark rather than by benchmark-level averages. We describe the suite, the sub-metric routing, and the quick/deep budgets, and report results for fourteen frontier and cost-efficient LLMs spanning the GPT, Claude, and Gemini families under quick mode. Top-line EGI ranges from 39.6 (GPT-4o mini) to 65.6 (gpt-5.5), and no single model dominates every capability axis: gpt-5.5 leads task outcome, planning, and retrieval grounding; Claude Sonnet 4.5 leads tool actioning and safety/abstention; and Claude Haiku 4.5 leads engineering quality. Code is available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/egi-bench.

</details>


### [165] [TxnQA: Benchmarking Analytical Reasoning and Evidence Grounding over Transaction Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27256)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yiming Bian,Yun-Shiuan Chuang,Jenna L. Ballard,Zijie Pan,Shivani Shekhar,Yixi Li,Yirou Ge,Avinash Thangali,Richard Williamson,Linsey Pang,Prakhar Mehrotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TxnQA 基准，用于评估模型在商户交易流水上的时序过滤、实体消歧与聚合问答能力，并将性能拆解为格式遵循率(FFR)、条件内容准确率(CA)与端到端正确率(E2E)；结果显示格式合规易达成，但算术计算与证据归因仍是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析商户交易历史需要对不规则事件流做动态时序过滤、实体消歧与聚合，这类操作难以被静态表格或时间序列预测类基准充分刻画，因此缺乏针对交易序列分析的评测手段。

Method: 构建 TxnQA 分析型问答基准，含 27 个任务族（81 个模板）及 5 场景欺诈风险推理套件，覆盖结论与支撑证据，数据为合成的隐私缓解交易数据；答案程序化生成，并将模型表现分解为 FFR、CA、E2E 三项指标。评测对象包括提示式闭源基线及六个 LoRA 适配的开源模型。

Result: LoRA 适配能稳定约束输出模式，但算术瓶颈依旧：Gemma-4-31B-IT 与 Qwen3-8B 的 E2E 分别为 0.867 与 0.816，被测 MoE 检查点仅 0.632–0.713；在 500 例欺诈套件上，结论准确率常超 80%，但要求精确证据归因的联合准确率最高仅约 23%。

Conclusion: 交易序列问答存在三个相互独立的瓶颈：模式合规、语义计算与证据归因；高判定准确率并不保证可追溯的证据支撑。

Abstract: Analyzing merchant transaction histories requires dynamic temporal filtering, entity resolution, and aggregation over irregular event streams--operations poorly captured by static tabular or time-series forecasting benchmarks. We introduce TxnQA, an analytical QA benchmark comprising 27 task families (81 templates) alongside a 5-scenario fraud-risk reasoning suite that evaluates both verdicts and supporting evidence over synthetic, privacy-mitigated transaction data. Ground-truth answers are computed programmatically, and model performance is decomposed into format-following rate (FFR), conditional content accuracy (CA), and end-to-end correctness (E2E). Evaluating prompted proprietary baselines and six LoRA-adapted open-weight models shows that adaptation reliably enforces output schemas but leaves arithmetic bottlenecks: Gemma-4-31B-IT and Qwen3-8B attain E2E scores of 0.867 and 0.816, whereas evaluated MoE checkpoints reach only 0.632-0.713. Furthermore, on the 500-instance fraud suite, verdict accuracy frequently exceeds 80%, yet joint accuracy (requiring exact evidence attribution) collapses to at most ~23%. These findings identify schema compliance, semantic calculation, and evidence attribution as three distinct bottlenecks in transaction-sequence QA.

</details>


### [166] [A latent control model for realistic rodent motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27279)
*Aidan Sirbu,Charles Y. Zhang,Yuanjia Yang,Bence P. Ölveczky,Talmo D. Pereira,Blake A. Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCAMPER系统，通过双先验变分架构训练实体化虚拟大鼠模型，使其在目标导向行为与随机探索中均产生自然运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经AI领域需要能与生物智能体直接比较的实体化动物模型，而有效比较的前提是模型运动方式与动物相似。已有虚拟动物身体与模仿学习技术可生成真实运动，但尚无系统能在训练后的目标导向行为和随机生成驱动的探索中同时产生自然运动。

Method: SCAMPER（Simulated Control via Augmented Motor Priors for Embodied Reinforcement learning）采用变分架构，包含两个不同先验：用于控制的运动先验与用于探索的行为先验，结合强化学习训练实体化虚拟大鼠。

Result: 同时结合两种先验使SCAMPER在探索中生成真实行为并有效利用任务结构；行为先验诱导的多样运动使其能够从稀疏奖励中学习，从而可建模神经科学实验中的真实稀疏奖励任务。

Conclusion: SCAMPER为神经AI社区提供了一个有效的新系统，可在真实任务上训练啮齿类动物的实体化模型。

Abstract: The development of embodied models of animal behavior is a major goal of the neuro-AI community. Embodied models will enhance our ability to make direct comparisons between biological and artificial agents. To make such comparisons effective, though, we need embodied models that actually move like animals. Previous work has developed virtual animal bodies and techniques for training realistic motion through imitation. However, to date, there is no system for embodied animal modeling that produces natural movements both during trained goal-directed behavior, and during exploration driven by random generation. Here, we introduce Simulated Control via Augmented Motor Priors for Embodied Reinforcement learning (SCAMPER), a system for training an embodied virtual rat model, enabling natural movements during both goal-directed behavior and random exploration. SCAMPER uses a variational architecture with two distinct priors, a motion prior for control and a behavior prior for exploration. We show that combining both of these priors allows SCAMPER to generate realistic behavior during exploration and exploit the task structure effectively. SCAMPER can also learn from sparse rewards as a result of the diverse movements induced by the behavior prior, allowing the system to model realistic sparse-reward tasks from experimental neuroscience. Altogether, SCAMPER provides the neuro-AI community with an effective new system for training embodied models of rodents on realistic tasks.

</details>


### [167] [TEX: Test-Time Scaling Testing Agents via Execution-based Cross-Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27290)
*Akash Gokul,Srijan Bansal,Yingbo Zhou,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TEX：基于执行驱动交叉验证的协同测试时扩展框架，并行生成测试与补丁并构建K×K反馈矩阵，在SWT-Bench Verified达86.4% SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试用例生成对语言模型仍具挑战性；现有测试时扩展方法或隔离候选、或聚合轨迹，难以扩展到长时程任务。

Method: 并行化多个智能体分别生成测试与补丁，将每个测试与每个补丁交叉执行，形成K×K反馈矩阵；各智能体仅使用自身的矩阵切片进行迭代精化，以客观执行结果替代冗长的轨迹共享。

Result: 在SWT-Bench Verified上达到86.4%，较单智能体基线提升+9.0%，刷新SOTA；从TEX输出中随机选取（84.4%）亦超过此前SOTA（84.0%）。

Conclusion: 以执行为依据的反馈是长时程智能体任务中有效的扩展机制，其收益来自整体解质量的提升而非仅最优样本筛选。

Abstract: Test-case generation is critical but remains challenging for language models. Existing test-time scaling methods either isolate candidates or aggregate traces, which do not scale to long-horizon tasks. We present TEX, a collaborative test-time scaling framework based on execution-driven cross-validation. TEX parallelizes agents to generate tests and patches, cross-executing each test against every patch to form a $K \times K$ feedback matrix. Agents use their matrix slices for refinement, enabling objective validation without verbose trace sharing. On SWT-Bench Verified, TEX achieves 86.4%, establishing a new state-of-the-art and improving +9.0% over single-agent baselines. Moreover, even random selection from TEX outputs (84.4%) surpasses the previous SOTA (84.0%), showing collaborative execution feedback improves solution quality, not just best-case samples. Our results highlight execution-grounded feedback as an effective scaling mechanism for long-horizon agentic tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Vulnerability Analysis for Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27293)
*Taozheng Zhu,Shixiong Jiang,Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MinMax Attack，针对安全多智能体强化学习（SMARL）的推理时观测攻击框架，通过联合选择被攻陷智能体的对抗目标动作并考虑良性智能体的协作抵抗，在保持任务奖励的同时显著提高安全代价，兼顾攻击有效性与隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMARL方法主要关注策略优化阶段的安全性约束，其在推理阶段的脆弱性尚未被充分研究；攻击者可通过扰动部分智能体的观测改变其策略输出，进而破坏系统安全。

Method: 提出MinMax Attack攻击框架：在推理阶段对部分智能体的观测进行扰动，联合优化被攻陷智能体的对抗目标动作选择，并将良性智能体的协作抵抗（Cooperative Resistance）纳入min-max博弈建模；同时推导诱导安全代价的上界，并刻画其与Compromised-Agent Budget和Perturbation Budget的关系。

Result: 在多个安全关键的多智能体基准与代表性SOTA SMARL算法上，MinMax Attack诱导的安全代价显著高于现有攻击基线，同时保持任务奖励基本不变，从而降低被检测概率，实现攻击有效性与隐蔽性的统一。

Conclusion: 揭示了SMARL在推理阶段的观测攻击脆弱性，MinMax Attack作为一种兼顾有效性与隐蔽性的攻击框架，可用于评估与提升SMARL系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (SMARL) aims to learn cooperative control policies under prescribed safety constraints. Al- though existing SMARL methods focus on enforcing safety during policy optimization, their vulnerability remains largely unexplored. This paper investigates inference-time observation attacks against SMARL, where an adversary perturbs the observations of a subset of agents, thereby altering the actions produced by their policies. We propose MinMax Attack, an attack framework that jointly selects adversarial target actions for compromised agents while accounting for the Cooperative Resistance of benign agents. We further derive a bound on the induced safety cost and characterize its relation- ship with the Compromised-Agent Budget and the Perturbation Budget. Experiments across multiple safety-critical multi-agent benchmarks and representative state-of-the-art SMARL algorithms show that MinMax Attack induces substantially higher safety costs than existing attack baselines while preserving task reward, thereby reducing its detectability and achieving both attack effectiveness and stealthiness.

</details>


### [169] [Stronger artificial intelligence breakthroughs hasten their own exhaustion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27296)
*Beichen Lu,Honglin Bao,Qi Huang,Qingguo Meng,James A. Evans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文基于36年计算机科学数据（720万论文、7.3万同行评审、370万研究者），提出"突破依赖于多样化注意力"这一机制，指出规模与想法空间都不足以解释发现难度；集体注意力集中会耗竭想法多样性，形成"突破—注意力集中—想法枯竭"的循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点认为AI进展随投入规模（研究者、论文、资金）线性扩张；另一类技术增长理论则将发现难度定位于"想法空间"。作者认为二者都不充分，试图揭示被忽视的第三要素——注意力的多样性，以及集体集中如何对其造成耗竭。

Method: 大规模计量分析：分析36年计算机科学领域的720万篇论文、73,089条同行评审及370万研究者的主题迁移；结合结构生物学（cryo-EM、AlphaFold后）的跨领域对照；并以模拟方法评估互惠评审等政策对注意力集中与接纳范围的影响。

Result: 领域规模扩张的同时注意力显著收窄（Transformer后尤为明显）；超过半数发生主题迁移的研究者流入AI，且以职业中期研究者为主；评审者信心在5分量表上下降整整1分；从AlexNet到LLM，每次突破提升了领域对想法的"承载能力"，但随后想法耗竭更陡；突破越大，注意力捕获越彻底，对后续多样化来源的耗竭越严重。结构生物学在cryo-EM与AlphaFold后呈现同一循环；模拟显示互惠评审等广泛使用的善意政策反而放大了高产作者优势并进一步收窄领域接纳范围。

Conclusion: 发现难度并非单纯由投入规模或想法空间决定，而取决于多样化注意力这一可被集体集中所耗竭的资源。作者呼吁建立保护AI领域注意力多样性的制度安排，而非奖励对"获胜范式"的集中投入。

Abstract: Progress in artificial intelligence is widely assumed to scale with effort: more researchers, papers and investment should accelerate discovery. Theories of technological growth instead locate the difficulty of discovery in the space of ideas. Here we show that neither scale nor space is enough: breakthroughs depend on diverse attention, which collective concentration can deplete. Across 36 years of computer science, we analyze 7.2 million papers, 73,089 peer reviews and 3.7 million researchers and their topic shifts. The field expanded while its attention narrowed sharply after the Transformer. Over half of topic-shifting researchers moved into AI, especially mid-career ones, and reviewer confidence fell by a full point on a 5-point scale. Each successive breakthrough, from AlexNet to large language models, raised the field's carrying capacity for ideas but was followed by steeper exhaustion of those ideas. Bigger breakthroughs capture attention more completely, depleting the diversity their successors may draw upon. Structural biology shows the same cycle after cryo-EM and AlphaFold. Simulations show how well-intentioned and widely used policies such as reciprocal reviewing empower prolific authors and further narrow what the field accepts. We call for institutions that protect diverse attention in artificial intelligence rather than reward concentration on the winning paradigm.

</details>


### [170] [When Does an Added Planning Capability Create Operational Value? An Observable Evidence Standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27305)
*Yihua Xu,Yan Lyu,Jize Li,Jiani He,You Lin,Yidan Hu,Rubing Qiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可观测的"证据标准"以解决工业AI规划系统中单次改进独立贡献的归因问题，并在Favorita、M5、Walmart数据上以预测到计划回放揭示单指标评估的三重局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业AI规划系统融合预测模型、优化逻辑、控制与治理规则，但整体策略的提升无法判断某项新增是否产生独立贡献，也无法判断该贡献是否值得其负担，即存在归因问题。

Method: 建立可观测的证据标准，区分行动机会、执行计划变化、运营后果、等价性、不确定性与运行范围；在Favorita、M5、Walmart数据上采用forecast-to-plan replay，对异构规划增件进行应用与比较（含低成本代理诊断与完整评估对照）。

Result: 单指标评估暴露三点局限：(1) 复合策略掩盖可忽略的分量贡献——一项控制仅改变681个执行计划中的15个；(2) 改变执行不保证稳定收益——多周期能力在若干长提前期设定下有效，但在紧容量下成本高7.6%；(3) 间接证据可用于优先复核但不足以完成归因——规则检查预测不变的599例中有19例独立运行路径不同；低成本诊断与完整评估胜者仅55.0%一致，但在$10^{-6}$成本等价容差内选中比例达79.5%。

Conclusion: 实验确立了能力层级的贡献证据；价值判断由组织结合实施与维持负担完成。由此形成的"能力证据记录"将观测结果链接到验证、部署范围、回退、监控、重设计与退役决策。

Abstract: Industrial artificial intelligence (AI) planning systems combine predictive models, optimization logic, controls, and governance rules, but improvement in the complete policy does not show whether one addition made an independent contribution or whether that contribution justifies its burden. We address this attribution problem with an observable evidence standard that separates opportunity to act, executed-plan change, operational consequence, equivalence, uncertainty, and operating scope. Using forecast-to-plan replay on Favorita, M5, and Walmart data, we apply the standard to heterogeneous planning additions. Three limits of single-indicator evaluation emerge. First, a composite policy can conceal negligible component contribution: one control changed only 15 of 681 executed plans. Second, changing execution does not guarantee stable benefit: the evaluated multi-period capability helped in several long-lead settings but was 7.6% more costly under tight capacity. Third, indirect evidence can prioritize review but cannot complete attribution. Independent runs produced different paths in 19 of 599 cases that a rule-based check predicted would be unchanged. Although a lower-cost diagnostic matched the full-evaluation winner in only 55.0% of cases, it selected an option within the $10^{-6}$ cost-equivalence tolerance in 79.5%; the remaining cases prevented the proxy from authorizing acceptance. These experiments establish capability-level contribution evidence; the organization completes the value decision by weighing it against implementation and sustaining burden. The resulting capability evidence record links the observations to validation, deployment scope, fallback, monitoring, redesign, and retirement decisions.

</details>


### [171] [Evolving Self-tuning Benchmarks for Long-Horizon Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27307)
*Hyunji Alex Nam,Michael Dennis,Natasha Jaques,Minsuk Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TURTLE，一种基于无监督环境设计（UED）的工具使用评测任务自动进化框架，通过 proposer、solver 和 feedback generator 三个 LLM 智能体协作，生成可扩展、难度自适应的测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用（tool-use）基准测试依赖人工监督或局限于特定领域，难以规模化，无法满足 AI 助手在 Gmail、Venmo、Slack 等真实应用中可靠使用工具的需求。

Method: TURTLE（Tool Use Related Tasks with Long-Horizon Evolution）采用 Unsupervised Environment Design 范式：proposer 根据 solver 的实际表现获得奖励，生成"有难度但可解"的任务；feedback generator 总结 solver 的执行轨迹，供 proposer 利用以迭代改进任务；三者协同进化现有任务，形成自适应不同环境需求（用户模拟器、密集文档检索、多应用交互）的动态测试套件。

Result: 在 AppWorld、τ-Bench 和 AutomationBench 上使用 Gemma 4 31B-it 评测显示：进化后的任务命中目标难度区间（如 AppWorld 上困难任务占比提升 165%），任务多样性提高（推理与并行工具交互维度），并激发出比原始任务更广泛的失败模式。

Conclusion: TURTLE 提供了一种可扩展、无需人工监督的工具使用评测任务生成方法，能自动调节任务难度、提升多样性并揭示更多失败模式，为评估和改进 LLM 智能体的工具使用能力提供了有效手段。

Abstract: For AI assistants to move beyond simple chat interfaces and reliably assist users with applications like Gmail, Venmo, and Slack, we need to improve their tool-use capabilities. However, existing tool use benchmarks are still limited and difficult to scale because they rely on human supervision or are too domain-specific. To make data generation scalable, we propose, TURTLE (**T**ool **U**se **R**elated **T**asks with **L**ong-Horizon **E**volution), an Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) approach to evolve existing tasks using a proposer, a solver, and a feedback generator large language model agents. The proposer is rewarded for difficult yet solvable tasks based on the solver's empirical performance, and the feedback generator summarizes the solver's execution traces for the proposer to exploit. TURTLE produces evolving, self-tuning test suites adaptable to different environment requirements, from supporting user simulators and dense document retrieval to interacting across multiple apps. Empirical results on AppWorld, $τ$-Bench, and AutomationBench using Gemma 4 31B-it show that the evolved tasks target the desired difficulty range (e.g., a 165\% increase in the percentage of hard tasks on AppWorld), increase task diversity (in terms of reasoning and simultaneous tool interactions), and elicit a broader range of failure modes than the original tasks.

</details>


### [172] [Unsupervised Tool Evolution for Coding Agents in Data-Lake Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27327)
*Yuqing Yang,Yurong Liu,Deqing Fu,Manav Garg,Sravan Babu Bodapati,Fatma Ozcan,Alon Y. Halevy,Jiani Zhang*
