A shared 18-layer trunk with a separate 4-layer top per head, and a cross-encoder distilled into the fused score, turn the two heads into one retriever. On BEIR, UIBK-Embedding-v1 reaches 59.79 nDCG@10. This is 2.57 points above LateOn, the strongest model of its size, and 2.10 points above it when both models read documents of the same length; UIBK-Embedding-v1 is ahead on all 15 datasets. On a decontaminated version of BEIR it stays ahead on average (61.55 against 60.36) and on 8 of 12 datasets. Among the 156 models with complete BEIR results in the MTEB results repository, only models with at least 1.5B parameters score higher. Code, model weights, and a technical blog post are available at https://github.com/DataScienceUIBK/uibk-embedding , https://huggingface.co/DataScience-UIBK/uibk-embedding-v1 , and https://huggingface.co/blog/abdoelsayed/uibk-embedding-v1 .

</details>


### [271] [Neighborhood-Aware Unlearning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27232)
*Pierre Lubitzsch,Yubao Tang,Maarten de Rijke,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文揭示基于语义标识符(SID)的生成式推荐中，遗忘操作的负面影响在局部结构化且被全局指标掩盖；提出邻域感知遗忘(NAU)，通过显式控制遗忘物品邻域的概率质量，恢复被遗忘交互邻域的效用而不损失整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐通过自回归生成语义标识符(SID)预测物品，SID的token在语义相关物品间共享。当需要移除训练交互(如注入的垃圾数据或用户不再想要的物品)时，这种共享导致遗忘更新意外影响邻域物品的预测。现有全局指标掩盖了这一局部效应，使遗忘在整体测试集效用几乎不变，但邻域效用显著下降。

Method: 提出邻域感知遗忘(NAU)：在交互级遗忘的基础上，增加对分配给遗忘物品SID邻域的概率质量的显式控制，抑制因token共享引发的局部概率重分配。

Result: 在三个数据集和四种SID分词器上，NAU将垃圾数据诱导的曝光恢复至重训练参考水平，同时保留超过95%的重训练效用；并能在无全局代价的情况下恢复“遗忘并修复”方法损失的邻域效用。

Conclusion: 局部概率重分配是SID-based生成式推荐器遗忘的关键失效模式，显式控制该重分配可有效缓解其影响。

Abstract: Generative recommendation predicts items by autoregressively generating semantic identifiers (SIDs), whose tokens are shared across semantically related items. With this paradigm gaining adoption, the question of how to support unlearning in it has become pressing. If training interactions must later be removed, such as injected spam or interactions with items a user no longer wants, this sharing lets an unlearning update unintentionally affect the predictions for the interactions' neighbors. We show that this effect is locally structured and hidden by global metrics: unlearning can leave recommendation utility on the whole test set nearly unchanged while substantially degrading it on neighborhoods of the forgotten interactions' SIDs. We propose Neighborhood-Aware Unlearning (NAU), which augments interaction-level unlearning with explicit control over the probability mass assigned to the forgotten items' neighborhoods. Across three datasets and four SID tokenizers, NAU restores spam-induced exposure toward the retrained reference while retaining more than 95% of retrained utility, and recovers the neighborhood utility that forget-and-repair unlearning loses at no global cost. We identify local probability redistribution as an important failure mode of unlearning in SID-based generative recommenders and show that explicitly controlling this redistribution can mitigate its effects.

</details>


### [272] [FLINT: Fixed-budget Late Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27273)
*Himank Handa,Negar Arabzadeh,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FLINT 通过对查询与文档双方做注意力池化、各以固定数量向量表示，在保持可比排序效果的同时大幅降低存储与检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索模型（如 ColBERT）依靠 token 级匹配优于同规模单向量模型，但为每个 token 保留向量显著增大存储需求并拖慢检索，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出 FLINT（Fixed-budget Late INTeraction）：用基于注意力的池化将每条查询和文档表示为固定数量的嵌入向量；与仅在文档侧池化而保留 token 级查询的架构不同，FLINT 对查询与文档对称地施加池化，从而同时限制文档存储量和检索时的向量比较次数。并在 BERT 与 ModernBERT 两种骨干网络上验证。

Result: 在 BEIR 上与最接近的基线排序效果相当，并在查询更长更复杂的基准（BrowseComp+、CRUMB、TREC Tip-of-the-Tongue 2025）上优于基线；长查询语料上端到端检索比 ConstBERT 快 1.5–2.8 倍；索引规模相比 ColBERTv2 等 token 级后期交互模型降低 5.3–9.3 倍。

Conclusion: 对称式的固定预算池化是兼顾多向量检索效果与效率的可行方案，其效果-效率优势可跨编码器骨干迁移。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT often outperform single-vector models of comparable scale by matching queries and documents at the token level. However, retaining a vector for every token substantially increases storage requirements and slows retrieval compared to single-vector bi-encoders, limiting their scalability. We introduce FLINT (Fixed-budget Late INTeraction), an architecture that uses attention-based pooling to represent each query and document with a fixed number of embedding vectors. Unlike architectures that pool only document representations while retaining token-level queries, FLINT applies pooling symmetrically to both sides, limiting document storage and the number of vector comparisons needed during retrieval. FLINT achieves comparable ranking effectiveness to the closest baselines on BEIR and outperforms them on several benchmarks with longer and more complex queries, including BrowseComp+, CRUMB, and TREC Tip-of-the-Tongue 2025. We demonstrate that FLINT's effectiveness-efficiency benefits extend across encoder architectures, testing it on two separate backbones (BERT and ModernBERT). On long-query corpora, FLINT delivers 1.5-2.8 times faster end-to-end retrieval than ConstBERT, the closest baseline. FLINT also reduces index size by 5.3-9.3 times relative to token-level late-interaction models such as ColBERTv2.

</details>


### [273] [Conversational AI for Perceived Cognitive Decline Screening in Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27344)
*Nikita Gautam,Moana Sargent,Kaitlyn Sievers,Ashley Sylvara,Doina Caragea,Heather Bailey,Tianjun Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究评估基于聊天机器人的智能认知助手（ICA）开放式问答访谈能否用于筛查老年人自报认知下降，比较传统机器学习、冻结/微调Transformer与LLM推理策略在多条Q/A流水线下的表现，文本方法最佳macro-F1为0.728，但结构化问卷与认知任务模型更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经心理学评估资源消耗大，老年人认知下降的早期识别仍具挑战性；聊天机器人式ICA可通过开放式作答获取丰富的语言、语义与自传体线索，具备可扩展筛查潜力，但缺乏对其筛查效能的系统评估。

Method: 以老年人ICA问答访谈数据为对象，比较传统机器学习、冻结Transformer嵌入、微调Transformer与LLM推理四类方法，并在Global Q/A、Single Q/A置信度平均、Aggregated Q/A三种流水线配置下评测；以自报感知认知下降为主要结局，并结合认知、功能与人格结局进行二分类与连续回归分析，另开展提示级探索分析。

Result: 文本方法中Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite（每类1示例、Aggregated Q/A）留出测试表现最佳（准确率0.742、平衡准确率0.739、macro-F1 0.728），但测试集仅n=31，结果仅作描述性解读；非LLM文本方法中冻结临床Transformer嵌入+随机森林头最优，微调模型泛化有限；使用结构化问卷、认知任务与人格特质的模型优于纯文本模型；回归分析在AD8、FAQ、IQCODE、神经质与尽责性上显示较弱但可辨的预测信号。

Conclusion: ICA聊天机器人访谈可捕获有意义的自报认知下降信号，支持可扩展筛查研究；但由于样本量小、缺乏已验证临床结局，结论仅为探索性，尚需更大规模、临床多样性更高的队列以推动临床转化。

Abstract: Early detection of cognitive decline in older adults remains challenging due to the resource demands of standard neuropsychological assessments. Chatbot-based Intelligent Cognitive Assistants (ICAs) provide a scalable alternative by eliciting open-ended responses rich in linguistic, semantic, and autobiographical cues. This study evaluates whether ICA question-answer (Q/A) interviews with older adults can screen for participant-reported perceived cognitive decline and related cognitive, functional, and personality outcomes. We compare traditional machine learning, frozen transformer embeddings, fine-tuned transformers, and large language model (LLM) reasoning strategies across Global Q/A, Single Q/A confidence averaging, and Aggregated Q/A pipelines. Among text-based methods, Gemini 3.1 Flash Lite (1-example-per-class, Aggregated Q/A) achieved the highest held-out test performance (0.742 accuracy, 0.739 balanced accuracy, 0.728 macro-F1). Given the small test set (n = 31), these results are interpreted descriptively. Frozen clinical transformer embeddings with a random forest head led non-LLM text methods, whereas fine-tuned models showed limited generalization. Models utilizing structured questionnaires, cognitive tasks, and personality traits outperformed text-based models in classification. Continuous regression analyses revealed modest predictive signals for AD8, FAQ, IQCODE, neuroticism, and conscientiousness. Exploratory prompt-level analyses identified both direct symptom-related and indirect autobiographical questions with strong test metrics, though sample sizes varied across prompts. These findings indicate that ICA chatbot interviews capture meaningful signals of perceived cognitive decline, supporting scalable screening research. Larger, clinically diverse cohorts with validated clinical outcomes are needed for clinical translation.

</details>


### [274] [Baseline and Incremental: User, Item, and Request Debiasing in Instagram Feed Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27423)
*Hsin-Chiang Chen,Hao Wang,Gedi Zhou,Mingze Gao,Abhishek Kumar,Yiben Yang,Haoyu Wang,Hongjing Xia,Bo Chen,Zhengyu Su,Xian Sun,Chang Xu,Kevin Han,Weiwei Li,Zhongchang Sun,Yao Zhang,Qifei Wang,Peggy Yang,Yihan Wu,Haoran Liu,Siyang Sun,Xingyu Pan,Shannon Phu,Haoming Chen,Kevin Zhao,Chen Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出多信号价值模型应保留原始预测并将增量部分作为独立加权项，在Instagram Feed的大规模四臂消融中，二项（两端）方案在26组直接对比中17组显著占优、仅1组（展示量）显著劣于单增量方案，该算子已推广至Meta多应用与两阶段排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feed排序模型的价值函数由各互动预测加权求和构成，而每个预测都把"基线"（该用户、物品或请求本来就会产生的量）与"增量"混在一起。现有文献多在去基线，忽略了两者分离可能带来的收益，因此作者主张保留原始预测并显式加入增量项。

Method: 1) 将用户习惯、物品热度、请求上下文三类去偏族统一为一个单参数化算子，并由设计流程在该算子族中选点；2) 媒体类型（格式）偏差改由同一流程选择的去偏标签处理；3) 在已部署Feed项上做受控四臂消融（两项皆无、各自单独、二者兼有）。

Result: 在生产权重下，双项方案在13个被判定的（项，指标）对中11对点估计优于两个单一项方案；26组直接对比中17组显著支持双项，仅1组（展示量）显著反对。消融还表明离线诊断应用于信号的筛选而非排序。

Conclusion: 基线—增量分离的双项价值模型在线上大规模场景有效，相关项已在Instagram Feed生产环境运行近一年；该算子已被Meta推广至多界面、多应用及两阶段排序，上线显著提升会话数与日活用户等核心访问与留存指标。

Abstract: A feed ranker scores candidates with a value model, a weighted sum of engagement predictions. Each prediction conflates a baseline, what this viewer, item or request would produce anyway, with the incremental amount by which this candidate exceeds it. The literature mostly removes the baseline. We argue that a multi-signal value model should keep the raw prediction and add the incremental part as a separately weighted term. On Instagram Feed, three debiasing families deployed independently, for viewer habit, item popularity and request context, are one parameterised operator, and a design procedure picks a point in it. Media-type (format) bias goes instead to a debiased label chosen by the same procedure. In a controlled four-arm ablation of deployed Feed terms (neither term, each alone, both), the two-term arm beats both single-term arms at production weights on point estimates in 11 of 13 judged (term, metric) pairs. Of the 26 direct contrasts, 17 are significantly for it and only 1 against it, on impressions. The ablation also suggests that the offline diagnostic should screen signals, not rank them. Such terms have served Instagram Feed production for most of a year. Beyond Feed, Meta, whose apps serve billions of people, has adopted the operator across surfaces, apps and both ranking stages. Its launches there drive sessions and daily active users, the core visitation and retention metrics.

</details>


### [275] [Robustness Analysis of Pareto-based Joint Evaluation of Fairness and Relevance in Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27850)
*Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Maria Maistro,Tuukka Ruotsalo,Christina Lioma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Distance to Pareto Frontier (DPFR)，一种基于相关性-公平性 Pareto 前沿的联合评估方法，用于同时评价推荐系统的相关性与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两类评估方式均存在缺陷：分别评估相关性与公平性可能得到不一致的模型排序；单一“相关性感知”公平指标往往对两方面评价不均衡，无法同时、平衡地评估二者。

Method: DPFR 基于现有指标构建用户-物品交互数据上的相关性-公平性 Pareto 前沿，该前沿表示两者间多种 Pareto 最优权衡；将待评估点与前沿的（到前沿）距离作为联合评分。方法模块化、可复用现有指标且直观。实验使用 4 个推荐器、3 个公平性感知重排器、6 个真实数据集，并结合人工推荐、不同权衡设置以及与简单公平/相关性指标的一致性分析。

Result: 实验显示现有指标与 DPFR 之间的关联不一致，说明它们并非基于 Pareto 最优性评估的可靠代理；人工推荐与多权衡实验表明 DPFR 比现有联合评估方法更稳健。

Conclusion: DPFR 提供了一种稳健、模块化、可解释的公平性与相关性联合评估方案，并给出使用指南。

Abstract: Recommender Systems (RSs) can be evaluated for many aspects, two of them being recommendation relevance and fairness. These two aspects can be evaluated either in isolation, using a measure for each aspect; or jointly with a single`relevance-aware' fairness measure that quantifies fairness with respect to relevance. Yet, neither approach provides a simultaneous evaluation of both relevance and fairness: the first approach may result in two different rankings of models, each corresponding to an evaluation aspect; the second approach often provides an imbalanced evaluation of the two aspects. To address this gap, we propose Distance to Pareto Frontier (DPFR), a new approach to jointly evaluate RS fairness and relevance. DPFR is based on a Pareto frontier of relevance and fairness scores from existing measures. The frontier is generated from user-item interaction data and represents various Pareto-optimal trade-offs between the two aspects. Thus, the distance from a point in the frontier provides a joint score of fairness and relevance. As DPFR can be used with existing measures, it is modular and intuitive. Our experiments with 4 recommenders and 3 fairness-aware re-rankers on 6 real-world datasets reveal inconsistent associations between existing measures and DPFR, showing that they are not reliable proxies for evaluations based on Pareto optimality. We further examine the robustness of DPFR as a joint evaluation approach for fairness and relevance by conducting experiments with artificial recommendations and with various fairness-relevance trade-offs, as well as by analysing DPFR's agreement to simple measures of fairness and relevance. These analyses show that DPFR is more robust than existing joint evaluation approaches. Finally, we provide guidelines for evaluating fairness and relevance jointly with DPFR. Our code: https://github.com/theresiavr/robustness-DPFR-recsys-evaluation.

</details>


### [276] [SPAR: Stepwise Personalized Autoregressive Recommendation with Prefix-Balanced Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27898)
*Chunrui Zhang,Gaoyun Lin,Chang Zhou,Zheng Hu,Qiran Song,Shimin Cai,Tao Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SPAR生成式推荐框架，通过PATH Decoder实现逐token前缀感知个性化证据检索，并用RQ-PIB平衡候选分支的历史交互负载，在优酷大规模数据与线上A/B实验中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语义ID（SID）的生成式推荐通过逐级token决策检索item，因此每个解码步骤的个性化至关重要。但现有方法未能充分利用前缀相关的用户证据，且候选分支间历史交互负载不均衡，导致不可逆的分支选择错误并偏向热门路径。

Method: SPAR框架包含两个模块：(1) PATH Decoder：从压缩的长期用户记忆中检索与前缀相关的证据，并与近期行为、用户画像和事件上下文结合，指导每个token的决策；(2) RQ-PIB模块：在每个父前缀下平衡兄弟分支的历史交互负载，同时保持语义结构。

Result: 优酷大规模生产数据上，HitRate较最强基线平均相对提升2.66%；目标前缀保留率提升5.79个百分点；按流量加权的最小子分支占比下降32.84%。7天线上A/B实验中承载优酷10%生产流量，人均观看时长提升3.95%，播放转化率提升3.38%。

Conclusion: SPAR实现了更可靠的层级解码、更均衡的SID分支分布，并在工业推荐中提升了用户参与度，验证了逐步骤个性化与SID构建联合优化的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID)-based generative recommendation retrieves items through successive token-level decisions, making personalization at each decoding step essential. However, existing methods insufficiently account for prefix-dependent user evidence and imbalanced historical interaction loads among candidate branches. This can cause irreversible branch-selection errors and favor popular paths. We propose SPAR, a generative recommendation framework that jointly improves stepwise personalization and SID construction. Its PATH Decoder retrieves prefix-relevant evidence from compressed long-term user memories and combines it with recent behavior, user profiles, and event context to guide each token decision. Its RQ-PIB module balances historical interaction loads among sibling branches under each parent prefix while preserving semantic structure. Experiments on large-scale Youku production data show an average relative HitRate improvement of 2.66% over the strongest baseline. SPAR also increases target-prefix retention by 5.79 percentage points and reduces the traffic-weighted largest child-branch share by 32.84% across SID levels. In a 7-day online A/B test, SPAR serves 10% of Youku's production traffic and improves average watch time per user by 3.95% and play conversion rate by 3.38%. These results demonstrate more reliable hierarchical decoding, balanced SID branches, and improved user engagement in industrial recommendation.

</details>


### [277] [AutoSim: A Self-Improving User Simulator for Conversational Search Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28054)
*Saber Zerhoudi,Jelena Mitrovic,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究对话式检索用户模拟器 AutoSim 的真实性与评估准确性，发现权重微调（而非提示）才能让模拟器行为贴近真实用户，从而得到更准确的系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器有望以人力研究无法达到的规模评估对话式检索系统，但前提是其行为足够真实可信。需要验证模拟器与真实用户的一致性，并探究如何提升其真实性。

Method: 以 AutoSim 为对象，在三个 TREC iKAT 年度的受控检索系统池上，将其相关性判断排序与 NIST 人工判断进行 Kendall's τ 一致性检验；去除 persona 后检验稳健性；利用真实用户的留存信号对模拟器进行自提升（权重微调），并分别比较提示工程与权重微调在真实/模拟会话判别任务上的效果（AUC 及人类阅读评判）。

Result: AutoSim 与 NIST 排序一致性 τ 为 0.66、0.91、0.91（均 p≤0.002），去除 persona 后仍保持。判别实验中，任何提示均无法使真实/模拟分类器 AUC 低于 0.85，而权重微调可降至 0.45，使分类器和人类均无法区分。真实用户检索较少时，提示型模拟器将检索有效性高估 +0.136 nDCG，并在真实用户未找到相关段落的 57% 轮次中返回相关段落，足以改变部署决策；仅权重级模拟器规避该偏差。

Conclusion: 模拟器的真实性直接决定评估准确性；提示层面无法达到真实用户行为，只有权重级微调才能消除偏差并给出可靠评估。作者公开框架与代码。

Abstract: User simulators promise to evaluate conversational search systems at a scale human studies cannot reach, but only if their behavior is realistic enough to trust. We study AutoSim, a conversational-search user simulator whose relevance judgments rank a controlled pool of retrieval systems in agreement with NIST across three TREC iKAT years (Kendall's $τ$ of $0.66$, $0.91$, and $0.91$; all $p{\le}0.002$), an agreement that survives removing its persona. AutoSim self-improves against a held-out signal from real users, and we find that fine-tuning its weights makes it behave like real users, which in turn gives a more accurate estimate of how well a system serves them. Reaching that behavior takes more than prompting: when discriminating between real and simulated user sessions, no prompt we tried lowers a held-out real-versus-simulated classifier below AUC $0.85$, whereas fine-tuning the weights drives it to $0.45$, where neither the classifier nor a human reading full dialogues can tell it from a real user. That added realism is what makes the evaluation more accurate: where real users retrieve little, a prompted simulator over-estimates retrieval effectiveness by $+0.136$ nDCG and returns a relevant passage on $57\%$ of the turns where real users find none, enough to change a deployment decision. Only the weight-level simulator escapes this bias. We release the framework and code.

</details>


### [278] [Multimodal Reranking Is a System One Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28435)
*Zhuchenyang Liu,Yao Zhang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将多模态重排序视为一种"System One决策"：用通用VLM以单次前向的多选概率对候选打分，零样本即可超越专用重排器，且仅用文本决策数据做决策微调还能进一步提升页面重排效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态重排器多为专用模型（在检索数据上训练VLM打分），成本高、通用性差。作者观察到"对选项打分"并非重排专属，System One决策模型（如Jev）可将状态+类型化问题映射为各选项概率且无需生成文本，通用模型几乎无需训练即可完成。因此重排可被视为以query及候选为状态、候选为选项的决策任务，从而论证通用VLM可作重排器，且决策调优能增强其重排能力。

Method: 对第一阶段检索器返回的top-16候选构造一个多选（one-choice）问题，由VLM一次前向读取各选项标签概率并据此排序；与逐候选yes/no提问对比。进一步在仅文本决策数据上对同一骨干做决策调优（JEV-27B-VL）。评测于MMEB-V2图像检索、视觉文档页面重排等基准，并与监督式Qwen3-VL-Reranker-2B/8B比较hit@1、nDCG@5及延迟。

Result: MMEB-V2图像检索上，多选比逐候选yes/no高5.4–7.3 hit@1，而yes/no甚至低于第一阶段。多选下零样本Qwen3.8-27B在三项基准上均超过监督的Reranker-2B，并在每项上距Reranker-8B不超过1.6分；在MMEB-V2视觉文档上与Reranker-8B持平（84.79 vs 84.78 nDCG@5），延迟为其1.1–1.5倍。JEV-27B-VL（仅文本决策调优）使页面重排提升0.6–1.1分，多数任务显著（符号检验p≤0.017），自然图像任务保持不变。

Conclusion: 通用VLM可通过System One多选决策充当多模态重排器，且决策调优可迁移提升重排性能。作者承认零样本模型更大且代际更新，与监督基线比较存在混淆，并在文中讨论该混杂及其他替代解释以及可验证该主张的后续实验。

Abstract: Multimodal rerankers are usually dedicated models: a vision-language model (VLM) is trained on retrieval data to score the candidates of a query. Scoring options is not specific to reranking. System One decision models such as Jev map a state and a typed question to one probability per option without generating text, and general models can make such decisions with little or no training. Reranking is one such decision, with the query and its candidates as the state and the candidates as the options. We therefore argue that a general VLM can serve as a multimodal reranker through System One decisions, and that decision tuning can improve its reranking. We ask a VLM one choice question over the top 16 candidates of a first-stage retriever and rank the candidates by the probabilities of their labels, read from one forward pass. On MMEB-V2 image retrieval, this choice scores 5.4 to 7.3 hit@1 above a yes/no question per candidate, and the yes/no question falls below the first stage. With the choice, zero-shot Qwen3.8-27B beats the supervised Qwen3-VL-Reranker-2B on all three benchmarks and comes within 1.6 points of Reranker-8B on each. It matches Reranker-8B on MMEB-V2 visual documents (84.79 vs. 84.78 nDCG@5), at 1.1 to 1.5 times its latency. JEV-27B-VL, the same backbone tuned on text decisions only, improves page reranking by 0.6 to 1.1 points, with gains on most tasks (sign test, p <= 0.017), and leaves natural images unchanged. The zero-shot model is larger than the supervised rerankers and of a newer generation, so these comparisons are confounded; we discuss this and other alternative views, and the experiments that would test the position.

</details>


### [279] [ECHO: Efficient Cascade with Homogeneous Operators for Distillation-Guided 100K-Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28569)
*Wenhao Li,Qinglin Jia,Chenghou Jin,Zihong Xian,Jiezhou Ji,Yanzhi Liu,Zezheng Wang,Ruiming Tang,Chaoyi Ma,Zhaojie Liu,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ECHO框架，通过蒸馏式选择器与目标感知细粒度检索器级联，在10万长度用户序列下兼顾效率与细粒度检索，在线序列分支FLOPs降低约160倍，并取得GAUC与A/B指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐需建模长程用户行为序列以捕获长期偏好，但10万长度序列使目标感知排序计算代价过高。基于搜索的原始序列检索成本随序列长度增长且依赖候选，隐式压缩方法虽降低计算却牺牲了item级可寻址性，二者难以兼得。

Method: ECHO保留每用户部分原始事件记录：蒸馏式选择器（DS）由候选感知的全历史教师经逐目标蒸馏监督，实现无需对每个候选做全历史计算的目标感知用户级选择；再以目标感知细粒度检索器（TFR）为每个候选检索更小的目标相关记录集。检索器与目标注意力打分器共享教师学到的相关性参数，保证级联中相关性估计一致。另有三项部署优化：公共用户输入、紧凑记录编码、key侧投影吸收，降低内存、存储与数据搬移开销。

Result: 序列长度L=100K时，在线序列分支FLOPs相比代表性端到端模型降低约160倍；在KuaiRand-1K与工业级短视频平台上，多任务相对GAUC较SOTA基线提升0.113%–1.520%；线上A/B测试应用使用时长提升0.613%。

Conclusion: 通过"用户级蒸馏选择+候选级细粒度检索"的两级同构算子级联，ECHO在超长序列场景下同时获得计算效率与item级可寻址的检索精度，并可通过工程优化落地工业系统。

Abstract: Modeling lifetime user behavior sequences is essential for capturing long-range preferences in industrial recommendation, yet 100K-length user sequences make target-aware ranking prohibitively expensive. Search-based methods that retrieve from raw sequences incur candidate-dependent costs that grow with sequence length, whereas latent compression methods reduce computation at the expense of item-level addressability. We propose ECHO (Efficient Cascade with Homogeneous Operators), a framework that balances efficiency and fine-grained retrieval by retaining a subset of original event records per user via a Distilled Selector (DS) and retrieving a smaller set of target-relevant records per candidate via a Target-aware Fine-grained Retriever (TFR). DS is supervised by a candidate-aware full-history teacher via per-target distillation, enabling target-aware user-level selection without incurring full-history computation for every candidate. The retriever and target-attention scorer share the teacher's learned relevance parameters, ensuring consistent relevance estimation across the cascade. At a sequence length of L = 100K, ECHO reduces online sequence-branch FLOPs by approximately 160x compared with a representative end-to-end model. Three deployment optimizations, including common user inputs, compact record encoding, and key-side projection absorption, further reduce memory, storage, and data-movement overhead. Experiments on KuaiRand-1K and an industrial-scale short-video platform demonstrate relative GAUC improvements of 0.113%-1.520% over state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines across multiple tasks. Furthermore, online A/B testing shows a 0.613% improvement in app usage time.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [Learning from Past Mistakes: AI-Assisted Analytics of Software Defects and Their Fixes for Future Defect Prevention - An Industrial Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26889)
*Vehid Goruslu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种AI辅助、人工在环的缺陷数据分析方法，按失效类型与全生命周期（SDLC）中的人为错误根源对历史缺陷及修复数据分类，并给出预防建议与工程能力建设洞见；在大型软件企业的工业案例研究中验证了分类准确性、结果可用性，并观察到引入反馈后缺陷密度下降（仅相关，非因果证据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业实践中缺陷仍反复出现；缺陷数据虽被例行采集于问题跟踪系统，但用途多局限于检测、报告与短期修复，历史缺陷及其修复中蕴含的知识未被充分利用于预防与长期质量改进。

Method: 构建AI辅助、human-in-the-loop的分析流程：对缺陷按失效类型和SDLC各阶段的人为错误根源进行分类，判定各类缺陷本可如何被预防，并综合形成面向工程师的能力建设洞见。在大型软件企业开展工业案例研究评估，采用从业者问卷与访谈、分类可靠性检验，以及按工作量归一化的缺陷密度分析。

Result: 从业者与独立评审认为分类准确；输出被评价为有用且可付诸行动；引入反馈后缺陷密度下降——作者将其解释为与正向效果相符的初步迹象，但受非对照的前后测设计限制，不构成因果证明。

Conclusion: AI辅助、人工治理的缺陷-修复数据分析可推动组织从缺陷检测转向缺陷预防、持续学习与质量改进；但需注意研究设计的局限，结论为初步性证据。

Abstract: Context. Despite advances in software testing and quality assurance, defects continue to recur in industrial software systems. Defect data are routinely collected in issue-tracking systems, but their use is largely confined to detection, reporting, and short-term resolution, leaving the knowledge embedded in historical defects and their fixes under-exploited for prevention and long-term improvement. Objective. This paper investigates how historical defect and defect-fix data can be analyzed, with AI assistance under human oversight, to identify the human-error origins of recurring defects and to feed that understanding back into prevention and QA improvement. Method. We developed an AI-assisted, human-in-the-loop analytics approach that classifies defects by failure type and by human-error origin across the SDLC, determines how each could have been prevented, and synthesizes engineer-focused capability-building insights. It was deployed and evaluated in a large software company through an industrial case study, using practitioner surveys and interviews, classification-reliability checks, and an effort-normalized analysis of defect density. Results. Practitioners and independent review judged the classifications accurate; the outputs were rated useful and actionable; and defect density decreased after the introduction of the feedback, a tentative association we interpret as consistent with, but not proof of, a positive effect, given the study's uncontrolled pre/post design. Conclusion. AI-assisted, human-governed analysis of defect-fix data can help shift organizations from defect detection toward prevention, continuous learning and improvement.

</details>


### [281] [Certifying Repeatable Entry-Replay Witnesses for COBOL-to-Java Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27272)
*Andras L. Ferenczi,Mariya Bessonov,Allwyn Ponnudurai,Chandrakanth R. Mamillapalli,Krishna K. Maddula,Krishna K. Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出通过"外部操作磁带"代理程序回放生成合成端到端测试用例的方法，对33,429行COBOL实现78.1%分支与95.7%段落覆盖，849个认证用例全部通过Locksmith Loop验证，并揭示门控对舍入/精度缺陷的检测盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL应用迁移中生成用于对比测试的合成数据是当前agentic迁移的核心难题；需要一种能获得高分支与段落覆盖、且可确定性验证的合成端到端测试用例生成方法，以支撑迁移等价性验证。

Method: 构建mock外部交互的替代COBOL程序，以"外部操作磁带"按时间顺序记录外部交互；在代码执行轨迹上观察并做entry-replay式的测试用例认证，从而生成合成端到端测试用例；再用Locksmith Loop的projected-agreement验证生成Java与COBOL替代程序在指定观测上的一致性；并以450个种子缺陷（删除写操作、删除段落调用、改变舍入等）评估门控检出能力。

Result: 对33,429行COBOL程序生成849个合成端到端用例，覆盖2,973/3,806源码分支（78.1%）与989/1,033主AST段落（95.7%）；849个认证用例上Java与COBOL替代程序观测一致；过程中发现61个缺陷并修复Java生成器；种子缺陷门控总检出率47.6%，执行代码中删除写操作与段落调用全部检出，但舍入与精度缺陷未被检出。

Conclusion: entry-replay认证结合外部操作磁带可在大规模COBOL上高效获得高覆盖率且可确定性验证的合成端到端测试用例；但现有门控对舍入与精度类业务逻辑缺陷存在系统性盲区，需补充针对性oracle或差分精度检查。

Abstract: Producing synthetic data for comparison testing a migrated COBOL application is a major challenge that agentic migrations face today. Our prior paper, the Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration, presents a deterministic oracle to facilitate such migrations, the Locksmith Loop. We now propose a novel method for achieving high branch and paragraph coverage through generated synthetic end-to-end test cases, by means of entry-replay test-case certification by observing the code execution trace on a surrogate COBOL program that mocks external interactions via a chronological external-operation tape. We applied our method to a very large COBOL program with 33,429 lines of code to generate 849 synthetic end-to-end test cases covering 2,973/3,806 source branches (78.1%) and 989/1,033 main-AST paragraphs (95.7%). Using the Locksmith Loop's projected-agreement validation, the generated Java code matched the COBOL surrogate on the specified observations across all 849 certified synthetic end-to-end test cases. During the campaign we detected 61 defects and fixed the Java generator accordingly. To test whether the coding agent could silently introduce business logic bugs, we seeded 450 defects into the Java code, such as deleted writes, deleted paragraph calls, and altered rounding, to verify how many of these would be detected by the gating methodology. The gate detected 47.6%, including all deleted writes and paragraph calls in executed code, but was unable to detect the rounding and precision defects.

</details>


### [282] [A Hybrid Information Flow Analysis Framework for Android Hybrid Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27288)
*Jyoti Prakash,Mikkel Baun Kjærgaard,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MifDroid是一个静态分析引导的动态分析框架，通过生命周期感知的per-WebView调用图、桥接感知的类型与效应系统以及模块化JavaScript分析引擎，实现Android混合应用中Java与JavaScript之间的跨语言信息流分析；在338个F-Droid应用上把桥接解析率从36.5%提升到74.5%，并报告14处跨语言信息流漏洞，其中5处已被维护者修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android混合应用的WebView接口在原生侧（Java）与Web侧（JavaScript）之间形成跨语言信息流通道，现有单语言分析器无法分析。已有混合应用分析工作只解决部分问题：有的构建了桥接感知调用图但信息流分析有限，有的依赖粗粒度假设，有的只关注Web漏洞，因此需要一个覆盖完整跨语言信息流的分析框架。

Method: 提出MifDroid，一个静态分析引导的动态分析框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 生命周期感知的per-WebView调用图；(2) 桥接感知的类型与效应系统；(3) 模块化JavaScript分析引擎。选取F-Droid仓库中4,126个应用里338个使用addJavascriptInterface（启用Java与JavaScript主动通信）的应用进行评测。

Result: 引导式动态阶段在静态分析产生的444个条目基础上新增662个条目，将解析率从36.5%提升至74.5%；为115个静态分析结果为空的应用产出了已解析的桥接相关条目；共报告14处存在漏洞的跨语言信息流，其中5处已被维护者接受并修复。

Conclusion: 静态分析与动态分析互补：静态分析覆盖不足（解析率仅36.5%），动态引导显著提升桥接解析覆盖率；MifDroid能够有效发现真实世界中跨语言信息流漏洞，并获维护者认可，验证了生命周期感知调用图与桥接感知类型效应系统在混合应用安全分析中的实用价值。

Abstract: The WebView interface in Android (hybrid) applications enables the native side (e.g., Java) to exchange data with the Web (e.g., JavaScript). This creates cross-language information flow channels that existing single-language analyzers cannot analyze. Existing hybrid app analyses address only part of the problem: some build bridge-aware call graphs but provide limited information-flow analysis, some rely on coarse assumptions, and some focus only on web vulnerabilities. We present MifDroid, a static-analysis- guided dynamic-analysis framework with a lifecycle-aware per-WebView call graph, a bridge-aware type-and-effect system, and a modular JavaScript analysis engine. We evaluated MifDroid on 338 apps from the F-Droid repository (out of 4,126 total) that use addJavascriptInterface, enabling active communication between Java and JavaScript. Our guided dynamic phase adds 662 entries to the 444 produced by static analysis, raising the resolution rate from 36.5% to 74.5%, produces the resolved bridge-related entries for 115 apps that static analysis leaves empty. MifDroid reported 14 vulnerable cross-language information flows, five of which have been accepted and fixed by the maintainers.

</details>


### [283] [XRust: Detecting Logical Races in Rust Linux Drivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27325)
*Qingxiao Xu,Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Arjun Manikandan,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XRust 提出基于逻辑 happens-before 分析的自动化技术，在无需手工规范的前提下检测 Linux Rust 驱动中的逻辑竞态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust 虽提升内存安全，但无法防止逻辑竞态：各操作虽单独同步，交错执行仍可能违反高层不变量。现有检测通常依赖手工编写规范和严格受控执行环境，成本高、难扩展。

Method: 采用 Logical Happens-Before (LHB) 分析识别与逻辑顺序相关的共享状态访问；自动构造操作对 harness；使用串行差分 oracle 检测与任一串行顺序都不一致的并发执行；并借助轻量级用户态抽象在可复现调度下运行驱动代码。

Result: 在 Linux 7.3-rc2 上，从十个驱动的 102 个 Rust 文件中识别出 17 个共享对象，构造 1,654 个可执行 harness，发现两个此前未知的逻辑竞态；其中 rnull 块驱动的竞态已被维护者确认，修复已合入 Linux 块子系统树。

Conclusion: XRust 证明逻辑竞态可在无需手工不变量的情况下被自动化检测，并在真实内核 Rust 驱动中具备实用性与发现新缺陷的能力。

Abstract: Rust is increasingly used in Linux to improve memory safety, but its guarantees do not prevent logical races: individually synchronized operations can still interleave in ways that violate higher-level invariants. Detecting such bugs typically requires manually written specifications and carefully controlled execution environments.
  We present XRust, a technique for detecting logical races in Linux Rust drivers without manually specified invariants. Using Logical Happens-Before (LHB) analysis, XRust identifies shared-state accesses relevant to logical ordering, automatically constructs operation-pair harnesses, and uses serial differential oracles to detect concurrent executions inconsistent with either serial order. XRust also uses lightweight userspace abstractions to execute driver code under reproducible schedules. Applied to Linux 7.3-rc2, XRust identifies 17 shared objects across 102 Rust files from ten drivers, constructs 1,654 executable harnesses, and discovers two previously unknown logical races. The rnull block-driver race has been confirmed by Linux maintainers, with a fix accepted into the Linux block subsystem tree.

</details>


### [284] [Characterizing Post-Incident Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27380)
*Matt Pope,Jonathan Sillito*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文首次对"事故后软件演化"进行实证刻画：基于Wikimedia十年纵向数据（1,302个任务、2,203次代码评审），从目标、投入、弃置率、优先级和解决时长五个维度对比事故后变更与常规维护/演化变更，发现事故后变更更耗时（中位完成时间超过其他变更两倍）且高度集中于服务器配置仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在软件事故后通过事后分析识别预防或缓解措施来提升系统可靠性，但学界对"由分析过程触发的软件演化"的性质与特征理解有限，缺乏对其与标准维护及演化变更差异的实证认识。

Method: 采用混合方法，对Wikimedia为期10年的纵向数据集进行分析，覆盖1,302个任务与2,203次代码评审；按五个维度对变更进行分类：目标（target）、投入（effort）、弃置率（abandonment rate）、优先级（prioritization）与解决时长（time-to-resolve）。

Result: 事故后演化区别于其他变更类型：事故后变更更耗时，中位完成时间超过其他变更的两倍，并过度集中于服务器配置类仓库。

Conclusion: 通过识别事故后演化的若干特征，为可靠性工程与"从失败中学习"的后续研究奠定基础；指出需进一步探索的路径以全面理解事故后演化；并使研究者能够超越高层分析，着手开发契合事故后演化性质的专业化方法与工具。

Abstract: BACKGROUND: After a software incident, organizations attempt to improve the reliability of their software systems by conducting post-incident analysis and identifying actions to prevent or mitigate similar future incidents. Despite the importance of this activity, there is a limited understanding of the nature and characteristics of the software evolution triggered by the analysis process. AIMS: This study seeks to bridge this gap by providing the first empirical characterization of post-incident software evolution. We aim to establish an understanding of how these changes manifest, specifically focusing on how their characteristics (such as effort, location, and timing) differ from standard maintenance and evolutionary changes. METHOD: We conducted a mixed-methods analysis of a 10-year longitudinal dataset from Wikimedia, examining 1,302 tasks and 2,203 code reviews. We categorized these changes across five dimensions: target, effort, abandonment rate, prioritization, and time-to-resolve. RESULTS: Our results show that the post-incident evolution is distinct from other change types. Post-incident changes are more time-consuming, with a median completion time over twice that of other changes and are disproportionately concentrated in a server configuration repository. CONCLUSION: By identifying a few of the characteristics of post-incident evolution, this work provides a foundation for future research into reliability engineering and learning from failure. We highlight further avenues for exploration necessary to fully understand post-incident evolution. Lastly, our work enables researchers to move beyond high-level analysis and begin developing specialized methods and tooling tailored to the nature of post-incident evolution.

</details>


### [285] [Documentation Drift in AI-Generated Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27381)
*Will De Michele,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 分析 29,669 个 AI 生成的 PR，发现 63.2% 的 AI 代码改动未同步更新文档，且未文档化的文件后续更易被修复、更难被补文档，文档债务呈累积趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编程智能体已大规模向软件仓库提交代码，但其文档实践尚无系统性刻画，缺少对"代码变更是否伴随文档共变"的实证认识。

Method: 基于 AIDev 数据集中 2,585 个仓库的 29,669 个 AI 生成 PR，定义并测量"文档共变"（同一 PR 中代码文件修改是否伴随文档文件更新）；按任务类型（feature、refactoring）分层统计；以 30–90 天窗口追踪同一文件后续修复率；对 PR 规模暴露做控制以纠正原始 PR 级比较中的混杂偏差；并统计合并时延与 90 天内再文档化率。

Result: 63.2% 的 AI PR 只改代码不更新文档；按任务类型差异显著（feature 55.2%，refactoring 77.5%）。在 30–90 天窗口内，未文档化文件后续被修复的概率比已文档化文件高 8.6%–11.7%；原始 PR 级比较结论相反，原因是已文档化 PR 规模大 3.9 倍、吸引更多修复，控制规模暴露后结论反转。已文档化变更尽管规模更大，合并反而更快（中位数 40 秒 vs 184 秒），但该数据集合并多已自动化。文档漂移具有累积性：90 天内未文档化文件的后续再文档化率约为已文档化文件的一半（24% vs 48%）。

Conclusion: AI 编码智能体的文档实践存在系统性缺口，且文档债务倾向累积而非被回溯补足；简单 PR 级统计会因规模混杂而产生误导，需进行暴露控制。研究提示应关注 AI 生成代码的文档同步机制。

Abstract: AI coding agents now contribute to software repositories at scale, yet their documentation practices remain uncharacterised. We analyse 29,669 AI-generated pull requests from 2,585 repositories in the AIDev dataset, measuring documentation co-change: whether code-file modifications are accompanied by a documentation-file update in the same PR. We find that 63.2% of AI PRs change code without updating any documentation. This varies sharply by task type: 55.2% for features and 77.5% for refactoring. Undocumented code files are followed by a fix to the same file 8.6 to 11.7% more often than documented files across 30 to 90-day windows. A raw PR-level comparison shows the opposite because documented PRs are 3.9 times larger and therefore attract more fixes. Controlling for size exposure reverses this result. Documented changes also merge faster despite their larger size (median: 40 seconds versus 184 seconds), although merging is largely automated in this dataset. Documentation drift also compounds: within 90 days, a file changed without documentation is later re-documented at roughly half the rate of a file changed with documentation (24% versus 48%). This suggests that documentation debt tends to accumulate rather than be revisited.

</details>


### [286] [Standards-Guided and Dependency-Aware Code-Level Documentation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27634)
*Tingxu Han,Wei Song,Ziming Li,Yuchen Chen,Weisong Sun,Zhenpeng Chen,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CODEDOC：一个标准引导、依赖感知的代码文档生成框架，通过静态调用图与AST分析构建依赖感知提示，并结合交互式LLM-as-a-judge反馈按质量准则迭代精炼文档，在10个Python开源仓库上质量优于RepoAgent与长上下文基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码文档生成方法将文档视为无约束文本生成，缺乏显式质量标准，过度依赖对原始代码的长上下文提示，且难以进行可复现评估。作者主张代码级文档生成应是标准引导的软件工程任务，即依据来自权威标准的显式、可复用质量要求来生成与评估文档。

Method: 将代码文档定义为按源码路径组织的、对仓库函数与类的自然语言描述。CODEDOC通过静态调用图与AST分析识别代码实体间依赖，构造融合局部代码上下文、仓库路径与来自权威标准和风格指南的文档准则的依赖感知结构化提示；并引入交互式LLM-as-a-judge协议，沿形式化与实践导向标准中的细粒度维度提供与准则对齐的反馈以精炼文档。

Result: 在10个开源Python仓库上，符合标准对齐评估下，CODEDOC相对RepoAgent提升11.6%–17.0%，相对长上下文基线提升21.4%–29.6%，且每个文档实体的token消耗约比长上下文基线低一个数量级。人类研究显示LLM评判与人类判断高度一致（Spearman's rho = 0.72），相对结论在不同评判LLM下均成立。

Conclusion: 将文档生成重构为标准引导、依赖感知的软件工程任务可行且有效：显式质量准则与结构化依赖上下文可在显著降低token开销的同时提升文档质量，且LLM评判可作为可靠、可复现的评估手段。代码已开源。

Abstract: Automatically generating high-quality documentation for software repositories remains challenging: existing approaches largely treat documentation as unconstrained text generation, lacking explicit quality specifications, depending heavily on long-context prompts over raw code, and offering limited support for reproducible evaluation. We argue that code-level documentation generation should instead be a standards-guided software engineering task, in which documentation is generated and assessed against explicit, reusable quality requirements derived from authoritative standards. We define code documentation as natural-language descriptions of repository functions and classes, organized by source-code path. We present CODEDOC, a standards-guided and dependency-aware framework that identifies dependencies between code entities via static call-graph and AST analysis and constructs dependency-aware structured prompts combining local code context, repository paths, and documentation criteria drawn from authoritative standards and style guides. We further introduce an interactive LLM-as-a-judge protocol that supplies criterion-aligned feedback to refine generated documentation along fine-grained dimensions from formal and practice-oriented standards. Experiments on ten open-source Python repositories show that CODEDOC produces higher-quality documentation than RepoAgent and a long-context baseline under standards-aligned evaluation, with relative improvements of 11.6%-17.0% and 21.4%-29.6%, respectively, while consuming roughly an order of magnitude fewer tokens per documentation entity than the long-context baseline. A human study confirms substantial alignment between LLM-based and human judgments (Spearman's rho = 0.72), and the relative findings hold across different judge LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/ControllableModel/CodeDoc.

</details>


### [287] [Knowledge as Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27693)
*Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"代码即知识"的认识论框架：将知识代码定义为以编程语言或机器可解析数据格式书写、可通过执行检验其有效性的知识，并论证代码是首个同时具备十三项认识论属性的知识媒介。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言长期是知识的载体，大语言模型仍以语言为中心，但基于其构建的编码智能体（coding agents）主要产出代码。作者由此追问：代码承载的是何种知识、如何获得，以及为何代码值得被当作独立的知识媒介来研究。现有认识论缺少对"代码作为知识媒介"的系统刻画，尤其缺乏对可执行性这一独特属性的分析。

Method: 提出一套概念性认识论分析框架：(1) 界定"知识代码"——用编程语言或机器可解析数据格式书写、其有效性可由执行检验的知识；(2) 提出知识媒介的十三项认识论属性（epistemic properties），逐一比较代码与其他媒介；(3) 区分知识进入代码的两种方式——编码（encoding）与"通过执行的习得"（learning by execution），后者中代码既是实验本身又是实验结果的记录；(4) 借助智能体轨迹（trajectory）使执行式习得可被观察。

Result: 论证表明代码是第一个具备全部十三项认识论属性的媒介；知识代码的有效性可由执行直接检验；智能体轨迹记录了其每一次试探、观察与保留的代码行，从而使"通过执行的习得"过程可观测、可追溯。

Conclusion: 代码不仅是知识工作的工具，更是自成一体的知识媒介，且是第一个能够自我检验其所承载内容的媒介。该视角为以代码为中心的智能体研究提供了认识论基础。

Abstract: For millennia, knowledge has been carried by language, and large language models still put language at the center. Yet the coding agents built on these models mostly write code. This paper is a contribution to epistemology, which asks what kind of knowledge code is and how it is obtained. We define knowledge as code as knowledge written in a programming language or in a machine-parsable data format, whose validity can be checked by executing it. We define thirteen epistemic properties of knowledge media and show that code is the first medium to have all of them. We distinguish two ways in which knowledge enters code, encoding and learning by execution, where code is both the experiment and the record of its result. Agents make learning by execution observable, since their trajectories record every probe, observation and line of code they keep. Code is thus not only a tool of knowledge work but a knowledge medium in its own right, the first that can check what it holds.

</details>


### [288] [Enhancing Bug-Exposing Test Generation via Step-Level Credit Assignment and Self-Distillation from Valuable Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27694)
*Wenjie Gan,Zhaoxi Zhang,Jiahui Liang,Weikang Li,Wanyuan Wang,Li Zhang,Jian Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 SCALF，一种过程监督强化学习方法，通过步级信用分配与对有价值失败轨迹的目标性学习，提升大模型自动生成触发bug的测试的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL的测试生成方法对所有推理步施加同一结果奖励，会抑制有用推理并强化错误步骤；同时无法有效利用"找到触发bug输入但输出预测错误"的轨迹，浪费了已验证的触发bug进展。

Method: SCALF = 步级信用分配（从过程奖励推导每步特定优势，使各推理阶段获得独立学习信号）+ 针对性失败轨迹学习（将暴露bug但输出预测错误的轨迹送入on-policy自蒸馏，利用执行反馈学习其有价值的触发bug进展）。

Result: 在三个基准上，相比强提示与后训练基线，联合成功率、输出正确率、bug触发率分别最高提升2.97、5.26、3.53个百分点。

Conclusion: 过程级奖励与失败轨迹的定向自蒸馏能有效改进LLM的测试生成质量，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown strong code generation capabilities, yet their outputs often contain subtle bugs. Automatically repairing such code requires informative tests: inputs that expose faulty behavior, paired with correct expected outputs. Recent RL-based test generation methods apply the same outcome reward to all reasoning steps, which can discourage useful reasoning and reinforce flawed steps. They also fail to effectively learn from trajectories that find bug-triggering inputs but predict incorrect outputs, leaving their verified bug-triggering progress insufficiently exploited. To address these limitations, we propose SCALF, a process-supervised RL approach that combines Step-level Credit Assignment with targeted Learning from valuable Failed trajectories. SCALF derives step-specific advantages from process rewards, allowing each reasoning stage to receive its own learning signal. It further routes trajectories that expose bugs but predict incorrect outputs to on-policy self-distillation, using execution feedback to learn from their valuable bug-triggering progress. Across three benchmarks, SCALF outperforms strong prompting and post-training baselines by up to 2.97, 5.26, and 3.53 percentage points in joint success rate, output correctness rate, and bug-triggering rate, respectively. Our code and datasets are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SCALF-F4D1.

</details>


### [289] [RELAY: Scaling Historical Build Restoration through Rule Reuse and Agentic Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27784)
*Olivier Nourry,Yoshiki Higo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究历史软件重建中的"环境腐化"问题：在14,159个OSS-Fuzz案例中69.7%已无法完成重建与验证，且失败症状高度集中在少数几类。作者提出Relay混合框架：用LLM从代表性失败中推导并验证确定性修复规则，规则可直接复用无需模型调用，规则无法解决的案例再升级给逐案agent处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞基准与软件工程研究依赖重建历史软件，但构建过程依赖基础镜像、软件包等源代码之外的外部资源，这些资源会独立演进而导致构建失效（环境腐化）。由于失败症状具有重复性，同类失败可只诊断一次并复用修复方案，从而避免对每个案例单独求解的高成本。

Method: 在14,159个OSS-Fuzz案例上度量环境腐化的规模与失败症状分布；提出Relay混合框架：由LLM从代表性失败中推导并验证确定性修复规则，以无模型调用的方式复用规则，并将规则未解决的案例升级至逐案agent。实验设置人工引导与全自动两轮规则推导campaign，并与逐案agent基线对比。

Result: 69.7%的案例不再能完成重建与验证，且大多数失败归因于少量反复出现的失败症状。规则推导可完全自动化；可复用规则以更少的构建执行次数恢复与逐案agent相当数量的构建，但逐案agent能恢复规则无法处理的案例。

Conclusion: "规则优先、剩余案例升级给agent"的混合方法比任一单独方法恢复更多构建，且成本低于仅使用agent的方案；规则的可复用性与agent的覆盖能力形成互补。

Abstract: Vulnerability benchmarks and software engineering research depend on rebuilding historical software. Yet builds rely on external resources, such as base images and packages, that change independently of the source code. In this study, we examine this environment decay on 14,159 OSS-Fuzz cases. We find that 69.7% no longer complete reconstruction and verification, and that most failures share a small number of recurring failure symptoms. A recurring failure can therefore be diagnosed once and its repair reused. We present Relay, a hybrid framework that uses an LLM to derive and validate deterministic repair rules from representative failures, reuses them without model calls, and escalates cases unresolved by rules to a per-case agent. We evaluate Relay in human-guided and fully automated rule-derivation campaigns and compare it with a per-case agent approach. We find that rule derivation can be fully automated, and that reusable rules restore as many builds as per-case agents with fewer build executions. However, agents restore cases that the rules cannot. Our results show that a hybrid approach that applies rules first and escalates the remaining cases to an agent restores more builds than either approach alone and at a lower cost than using agents alone.

</details>


### [290] [RAG-NIMBF: A Non-Invasive, Modular Benchmarking Framework for Automated RAG Configuration Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27816)
*Niclas Hart,Nicolas Weeger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 RAG-NIMBF，一个以非侵入方式挂载到现有 RAG 流水线的模块化评测框架，通过受控消融与端到端实验追踪系统评估配置空间，并在 SQuAD 上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 已成为 LLM 外部知识落地的主流范式，但实际部署面临分块、嵌入、检索、重排、提示与生成模型组成的庞大配置空间，各组件间如何交互缺乏指导，实践中多依赖经验性选择。

Method: RAG-NIMBF 作为非侵入式评测层附加于现有 RAG 流水线；以受控消融遍历配置空间并做端到端实验追踪；评测指标体系融合三类信号：基于 LLM 的答案质量评分、检索正确性度量与运行时指标；在 SQuAD 数据集上跨多个生成模型验证。

Result: 揭示了非显而易见的组件交互。检索相关性与多样性的平衡显著优于传统相似度检索，MMR 在所扫描模型上将 nDCG@5 提升 +4 至 +11 个百分点；更细化的提示指令可明显提升答案对检索证据的忠实度，在 Qwen3.6-27B、GPT-4o-mini 与 Qwen3.5-think 上提升 +10 至 +13pp，而对 20B 生成器几乎无效果。

Conclusion: RAG-NIMBF 将 RAG 配置空间转化为可复现、可比较的实验；凭借非侵入挂载方式，可在不干扰既有工作流的前提下对已部署系统进行系统化评估与改进，以基于证据的决策替代临时性选择。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become the dominant paradigm for grounding the outputs of large language models (LLM) in external knowledge. However, practitioners are faced with a vast configuration space comprising chunking, embedding, retrieval, reranking, prompting and generation models, and there is limited guidance on how these components interact. We present RAG-NIMBF, a modular benchmarking framework that attaches to existing RAG pipelines as a non-invasive evaluation layer. It evaluates the configuration space through controlled ablation with end-to-end experiment tracking. The evaluation combines LLM-based answer quality scores, retrieval correctness measures, and runtime indicators. This study validates the framework across multiple generation models on the SQuAD dataset. This process reveals non-obvious interactions and demonstrates how the experiments are tracked. The findings indicate that the retrieval balance of relevance and diversity substantially enhances the relevance and coverage of retrieved context in comparison to a conventional similarity search, with MMR raising nDCG@5 by +4 to +11 percentage points across the swept models. More detailed prompt instructions meaningfully increase how faithfully generated answers stick to the retrieved evidence, especially for capable and reasoning models (+10 to +13pp on Qwen3.6-27B, GPT-4o-mini and Qwen3.5-think, but negligible on the 20B generator). Overall, RAG-NIMBF transforms the configuration space of RAG into reproducible, comparable experiments. By attaching to existing production pipelines in a non-invasive way, it enables practitioners to systematically evaluate and improve deployed systems without disrupting existing workflows. This replaces ad hoc choices with evidence-based decisions.

</details>


### [291] [MIMOS-Tools: An Integrated Toolchain for Model-Based Design of Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27826)
*Wang Yi,Chengzi Huang,Behnam Khodabandeloo,Duc Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出MIMOS-Tools，一个覆盖建模、仿真、形式验证、实时调度、可调度性与端到端延迟分析、异构多核代码生成的嵌入式实时系统全生命周期工具链，统一于具有形式化操作语义并保证功能与时序确定性的MIMOS计算模型，支持异步时间触发通信与模块化组合设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的方法与工具虽发展数十年，仍缺乏支持嵌入式实时系统完整开发生命周期的集成环境；且SCADE、Simulink等同步设计环境依赖全局基准时钟与离线静态调度，导致全局紧耦合，组件增改需重算系统级调度，难以支持可扩展的增量设计与动态更新。

Method: 构建MIMOS-Tools工具链，覆盖建模、仿真、形式验证、实时调度、可调度性与端到端延迟分析及异构多核代码生成；所有阶段统一基于单一、连贯且确定性的MIMOS计算模型，给出其形式化操作语义并证明功能确定性与时序确定性；采用时间触发异步通信结合灵活的计算实时调度，解耦同步范式的全局时钟耦合，并兼容截止时间为零的Simulink模型以模拟同步零时间语义。

Result: 形式化语义基础保证模型层验证的性质在开发全过程及生成实现中得以保持；组件可增删改而无需重算系统级调度，实现可扩展、增量的设计与全生命周期动态更新；工具链已公开于 uu-mimos.github.io。

Conclusion: MIMOS-Tools提供了以确定性语义为核心的集成化实时系统开发环境，兼顾模块化组合设计与形式化保证，克服了同步设计环境的全局耦合限制，并可作为同步范式的兼容替代方案。

Abstract: Despite decades of progress in model-based methods and tools for embedded real-time systems, there is still no integrated environment that supports the entire development lifecycle. This paper presents MIMOS-Tools, a comprehensive toolchain spanning the key phases of development, including modeling, simulation, formal verification, real-time scheduling, schedulability and end-to-end latency analysis, and code generation for heterogeneous multicore platforms. All phases are grounded in a single, coherent, and deterministic model of computation, the MIMOS model, for which we present a formal operational semantics and establish both functional and timing determinism. This semantic foundation ensures that properties verified at the model level are preserved throughout the development process and in the generated implementation.
  A distinguishing feature of MIMOS-Tools is its support for not only deterministic but also modular, compositional design. Unlike synchronous design environments such as SCADE and Simulink, which rely on a global base clock and offline static scheduling, MIMOS-Tools supports time-triggered asynchronous communication together with flexible real-time scheduling of computation. This removes the tight global coupling of the synchronous paradigm: components can be added, modified, or updated without recomputing a system-wide schedule, enabling scalable and incremental design and dynamic updates throughout the system lifecycle. However, MIMOS-Tools also supports Simulink models when the deadlines of tasks are set to zero to emulate the zero-time semantics of synchronous design.
  The toolchain is available at uu-mimos.github.io.

</details>


### [292] [Raise or Check? Predicting the Necessity of Additional Fuzzing for Continuous Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27828)
*Riku Kato,Tatsuya Shirai,Olivier Nourry,Yutaro Kashiwa,Kenji Fujiwara,Yasutaka Kamei,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出RAISE框架，在项目-天粒度做二分类预测漏洞引入，用覆盖率等特征指导连续模糊测试资源的风险感知分配，跨项目ROC-AUC达0.74，模拟中较启发式策略成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续模糊测试（如OSS-Fuzz）虽已发现上万漏洞，但漏洞引入到被检测之间存在较长的暴露窗口；额外模糊测试计算成本高，因此需要依据漏洞风险估计来高效分配模糊测试资源。

Method: RAISE为即时（JIT）优先调度框架：在项目-天粒度进行二分类，判断某天是否引入漏洞。数据集将OSV数据库中漏洞引入提交与OSS-Fuzz的每日覆盖率及变更指标结合，覆盖15个C/C++项目、15,210个项目-天（347个即2.28%为漏洞引入日）。采用跨项目验证，比较结合VCCFinder静态特征与动态模糊测试特征的随机森林等模型，并通过覆盖968个漏洞的模拟对比五种资源分配策略。

Result: 最优模型（随机森林，融合VCCFinder静态特征与模糊测试动态特征）跨项目ROC-AUC为0.74，显著优于随机基线；覆盖率指标（以PatchCoverage为首）最为重要。模拟中Simple-Regression策略每检测一个漏洞平均花费91.60次额外模糊测试会话，约为Median启发式（171.89）的一半，显著优于与预测无关的启发式策略。

Conclusion: 基于覆盖率感知模糊测试特征的JIT漏洞预测可有效指导连续模糊测试资源的风险感知分配，提升检测成本效率。

Abstract: Continuous fuzzing is used for vulnerability detection in Open Source Software (OSS). Large-scale services such as Google's OSS-Fuzz fuzz over 1,300 projects and have revealed more than 10,000 vulnerabilities. Despite this, a considerable time lag often exists between when a vulnerability is introduced and when it is detected (i.e., the window of exposure). Given the computational cost of additional fuzzing, we aim to allocate fuzzing resources efficiently using vulnerability-risk estimates. We propose RAISE (Risk-Aware Intensive Supplemental Execution Framework), a Just-In-Time (JIT) framework that prioritizes additional fuzzing for high-risk project-days. RAISE performs binary classification at the project-day granularity to estimate whether a vulnerability is introduced on a given day. We construct a dataset combining vulnerability-introducing commits from the OSV database with daily coverage and change metrics from OSS-Fuzz, covering 15 C/C++ projects and 15,210 project-days, of which 347 (2.28%) are vulnerability-introducing. In a cross-project validation setting, our best model, a Random Forest combining VCCFinder-based static features with dynamic fuzzing features, achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.74, substantially outperforming a random baseline; coverage metrics, led by PatchCoverage, are the most important features. In a simulation over 968 vulnerabilities comparing five allocation strategies, Simple-Regression was significantly more cost-efficient than the prediction-agnostic heuristics, spending 91.60 additional fuzzing sessions per detected vulnerability, roughly half the cost of the Median heuristic (171.89). RAISE shows that JIT vulnerability prediction with coverage-aware fuzzing features can effectively guide risk-aware allocation of continuous fuzzing resources.

</details>


### [293] [From Tokens to Files: Multi-Granularity Detection and Localization of AI-Involved Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27863)
*Yue Xu,Xiyang Wang,Hui Liu,Bolei He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种token级AI代码作者归属框架：在冻结代码大模型上挂接两个低秩适配器（分别训练于机器写/人类写文件），以逐token对数似然比作为局部证据，经轻量级头在行、块内聚合并汇总为文件级判定，实现行/块/文件多粒度AI代码检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将整个文件压缩为单一分数（基于表层文体特征或全局代码表示），当文件中只有少量块由AI生成时，证据会被平均稀释，既难以定位AI片段，也影响文件级判断。因此需要既能标记AI参与文件、又能定位具体行或块的方法。

Method: 1) 冻结的代码语言模型加装两个低秩适配器，分别在人写与机器写文件上独立训练；2) 用两者的逐token对数似然比作为AI作者归属的局部证据；3) 轻量级预测头结合上下文，在行和块级别聚合这些证据，再将局部预测汇总为文件级决策；4) 使用由人类原始文件与其AI生成/AI修改版本构建的混合作者文件训练，涵盖续写与fill-in-the-middle场景。

Result: 内部测试集（6种生成机制下11,773个AI参与文件 vs 2,541个人类文件）在1% FPR操作点达到72.4 macro F1；在无适配的外部人机协同写作基准HybridCodeAuthorship上取得62.5行级F1，超过该基准原文报告的56最优行级结果；在2022年前创建的19,445个人类文件上，迁移1%阈值后假阳率维持在0.55%–0.81%。

Conclusion: token级建模加多粒度聚合能有效缓解全局打分导致的证据平均化问题，在文件、行、块多粒度上兼顾检测性能与定位能力，且在无训练集的外部基准和无适配条件下具备良好泛化性，操作点在真实人类代码上保持稳定。

Abstract: The increasing use of large language models for code generation calls for methods that distinguish purely human-written code from AI-involved code: files in which any part, however small, was generated or substantially modified by a model. Beyond flagging such files, we aim to localize the lines or blocks attributable to AI. Existing approaches compress a file into a single score, from surface stylistic features or a global code representation, which averages away the evidence when only a few blocks are AI-involved. We propose a framework that models AI authorship at the token level and predicts at the line, block, and file levels. A frozen code language model is equipped with two low-rank adapters, trained separately on machine-written and human-written files; their per-token log-likelihood ratio provides local evidence of AI authorship. Lightweight heads aggregate this evidence within lines and blocks, using the surrounding code context, and their local predictions are aggregated into a file-level decision. We train on mixed-authorship files built from human originals and their AI-generated or AI-modified counterparts, including continuation and fill-in-the-middle scenarios. On an internal test set of 11,773 AI-involved files across six generation regimes against 2,541 human files, our method reaches 72.4 macro F1 at a 1% FPR operating point. With no adaptation, it reaches 62.5 line-level F1 on HybridCodeAuthorship, an external human-AI co-writing benchmark with no training split, above the 56 its own paper reports as the best line-level result. On 19,445 human-written files from repositories created before 2022, the false-positive rate stays between 0.55% and 0.81% under the transported 1% threshold, so the operating point holds on real human code.

</details>


### [294] [Hallucinations of Large Language Models in Cross-Language Code Translation: Phenomena and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27868)
*Yali Du,Yunji Zhang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM代码翻译中的幻觉进行系统实证研究，构建三类幻觉分类体系，人工标注13,919个案例并分析其跨模型/数据集/语言方向的分布，进而提出HaluCodeTran缓解框架（BM25示例检索+分类感知反思式精炼）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代码翻译虽已改进，但输出仍存在幻觉：与源程序冲突、违反目标语言事实、引入上下文不一致依赖；因代码翻译中细微语义偏差会静默改变程序行为，危害尤大。既有幻觉研究集中于自然语言生成或代码生成，跨语言代码翻译中幻觉的形式、分布与缓解尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 系统实证研究：覆盖3个基准数据集、6个语言对、7个LLM；基于执行测试与人工标注构建三大类幻觉分类体系（语义一致性冲突、事实知识冲突、项目上下文偏离），标注13,919个幻觉案例并分析其随模型、数据集、翻译方向的变化；据此提出HaluCodeTran缓解框架，结合BM25检索相关翻译示例与分类感知的反思式精炼。

Result: 构建并验证了包含三大类别的代码翻译幻觉分类体系；完成13,919例幻觉的人工标注；揭示了幻觉类型在模型、数据集与语言方向上的分布差异；HaluCodeTran作为消解基线被验证有效（提供经执行测试检验的幻觉刻画）。

Conclusion: 论文给出了经执行测试检验的代码翻译幻觉特征刻画，并提供可实践基线，为构建更可靠的LLM代码迁移工具奠定基础。

Abstract: Code translation converts source code from one programming language to another while preserving its functionality. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have improved code translation, their outputs still contain hallucinations: generated code that conflicts with the source program, violates target-language facts, or introduces contextually inconsistent dependencies. These failures are especially harmful in translation because small semantic deviations can silently change program behavior. Existing hallucination studies mainly focus on natural-language generation or code generation, leaving the forms, distribution, and mitigation of hallucinations in cross-language code translation insufficiently characterized. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of LLM hallucinations in code translation. Across three benchmark datasets, six language pairs, and seven LLMs, we construct a taxonomy with three major categories: Semantic Consistency Conflicts, Factual Knowledge Conflicts, and Project Context Deviations. We manually annotate 13,919 hallucination cases and analyze how hallucination types vary across models, datasets, and language directions. Guided by the taxonomy, we further propose HaluCodeTran, a mitigation framework that combines BM25-based retrieval of relevant translation examples with taxonomy-aware reflective refinement. The study provides an execution-tested characterization of code-translation hallucinations and a practical baseline for building more reliable LLM-based code migration tools.

</details>


### [295] [A Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning Engine for Compilation-Free Patch Presence Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27871)
*Siyuan Li,Jiayi Zhou,Zhiyuan Xia,Weijie Wang,Yaowen Zheng,Weiran Tang,Hong Li,Hongsong Zhu,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SyLares将无编译补丁存在性检测重构为神经-符号推理：LLM仅作特征提取器，把异构源码/反编译伪代码转成合规SMT-LIB，由Z3做确定性判定，实现"神经感知、符号决策"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码复用使1-day漏洞扩散，补丁存在性判定至关重要；传统方法可用性与准确性受限且依赖脆弱的编译流程。前作Lares虽免编译，但将LLM与SMT求解器作为并行独立决策通道：Z3符号通道仅覆盖20%用例，其余回退给LLM终裁，导致验证非形式化、非确定、易幻觉，两通道亦无法互 reinforcement（相互强化）。

Method: 提出SyLares，把无编译补丁存在性检测形式化为神经-符号推理。LLM被限定为神经特征提取器，感知异构源代码与反编译伪代码并生成符合规范的SMT-LIB输入；最终确定性判定交由符号Z3求解器。该"神经感知—符号决策"耦合由Code2SMT推理引擎实现，结合确定性语法解析与LLM驱动的代码归一化、符号对齐，将每个用例归约为形式化等价性查询。

Result: 摘要未给出实验数据；仅指出前作Lares中符号通道仅20%代码用例成功，其余依赖LLM回退决策。SyLares预期以形式等价查询取代不透明的LLM猜测。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM降级为特征提取器、由Z3承担终裁，SyLares消除了LLM幻觉导致的非确定性判定，并使神经与符号通道形成互补而非割裂的并行结构。

Abstract: Code reuse in modern software ecosystems facilitates the spread of 1-day vulnerabilities, posing significant security risks. Determining if patches are present is crucial, but traditional methods suffer from limited usability and accuracy, often depending on a fragile compilation process. In our prior work, we introduced Lares, the first compilation-free framework for patch presence testing that combines large language models (LLMs) with SMT solvers. However, Lares treats the LLM and the SMT solver as two separate, parallel decision channels: the symbolic channel (Z3) succeeded in only 20% of code cases, and the remaining cases fell back to the neural channel (the LLM) for the final decision. Letting the LLM act as the final arbiter turns verification into an informal, non-deterministic process that is prone to hallucination errors, while the two channels never reinforce each other. To overcome these limitations, we propose SyLares, which reformulates compilation-free patch presence testing as neuro-symbolic reasoning. Instead of letting the LLM emit a final verdict, SyLares uses the LLM as a neural feature extractor that perceives heterogeneous source code and decompiled pseudocode and produces specification-compliant SMT-LIB inputs, and delegates the final, deterministic judgment to the symbolic Z3 solver. This neural-perceives, symbolic-decides coupling is realized by a Code2SMT reasoning engine that combines deterministic syntactic parsing with LLM-driven code normalization and symbol alignment, so that every case is reduced to a formal equivalence query rather than an opaque LLM guess.

</details>


### [296] [How Developers Configure LLM Inference: A Large-Scale Study of Real-World Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27945)
*Nada Zine,Matias Martinez,Romain Rouvoy,Clément Quinton*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对 Hugging Face Transformers、vLLM 与 OpenAI API 三种 LLM 推理后端在开源项目中的配置实践进行大规模实证研究，分析 5,398 个 GitHub 仓库中的 345,117 次显式配置项出现，揭示开发者仅使用配置空间的少量部分、配置规模小但组合高度分散、取值选择差异明显，且不同应用场景间配置行为存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已被广泛集成进软件系统，部署与推理配置成为反复出现的软件工程任务，推理后端暴露大量影响模型加载、生成、服务与请求处理的选项，但开发者实际显式配置了哪些选项缺乏认识，因此需要实证刻画真实的配置行为。

Method: 基于 Hugging Face Transformers、vLLM 与 OpenAI API 三个后端，采集使用其中至少一个的 5,398 个 GitHub 仓库，识别 345,117 次显式配置项出现，并对选项覆盖率、配置规模、选项组合签名、选项取值及跨应用场景差异进行统计分析。

Result: 开发者仅规律使用配置空间的一小部分：98.8% 的后端专属文档化选项至少出现一次，但 78.9% 的选项在对应后端仓库中最多仅 10% 使用；配置规模小，Transformers 中位数 2 个选项、vLLM 与 OpenAI 为 4 个；组合高度分散，56.8%–61.6% 的多选项签名只出现在单一仓库；观察到重复出现的选项取值，但取值方式差异显著；不同应用场景的选项采纳差异最高达 30.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究刻画的是开发者显式暴露的配置面，而非各选项的重要性或最优性；结果为后续推理配置研究提供优先级依据，并为改进后端文档与配置支持提供实证基础。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly integrated into software systems, making deployment and inference configuration a recurring software engineering task. Inference backends expose many options that affect model loading, generation, serving, and request processing. Yet little is known about which of these options developers explicitly configure in practice. In this paper, we present a large-scale empirical study of LLM inference configuration practices in open-source software using Hugging Face Transformers, vLLM, and the OpenAI API. We analyze 5,398 GitHub repositories using at least one of these backends and identify 345,117 explicitly configured option occurrences. Our results show that developers use only a small part of the available configuration space regularly. Although 98.8% of the documented backend-specific options appear at least once, 78.9% are configured in at most 10% of repositories using the corresponding backend. Configurations are also small, with a median of 2 options for Transformers and 4 for vLLM and OpenAI, but the combinations of options vary widely: 56.8-61.6% of multi-option signatures appear in only one repository. We also observe recurring option values as well as substantial variation in how values are chosen. Finally, configuration practices differ across application contexts, with option-adoption differences reaching 30.7 percentage points. These findings characterize the configuration surface developers make explicit rather than the importance or optimality of individual options. They provide empirical evidence for prioritizing future studies of inference configuration and for improving backend documentation and configuration support.

</details>


### [297] [LiveUIBench: Evaluating Agent as App Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27977)
*Tong Zhang,Yuxuan Cheng,Ruimin Zhang,Yun Peng,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"Agent as App"任务：用户用自然语言表达需求，由智能体生成个性化应用并在使用过程中持续提供其行为；并实现LiveUIAgent（生成连接界面、保存信息与服务动作的任务编排，由共享运行时渲染更新界面）与LiveUIBench基准，实证表明现有模型难以可靠完成此类工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同用户对同一应用的需求可能不同甚至冲突，同一用户需求也会随时间变化；agent-to-app或UI-to-code方式生成的应用在需求变化时需改动实现，个性化成本高。

Method: 提出Agent as App任务范式（自然语言表达需求、智能体生成个性化应用并持续供给其行为）；实现LiveUIAgent：生成任务专属编排，连接用户界面、已保存信息与服务动作，由共享智能体运行时渲染并更新界面同时执行所请求操作；需求演进时仅修订编排与界面而复用运行时，形成轻量个性化基础。同时构建LiveUIBench基准，覆盖多样需求与多项个人服务间的有序交互。

Result: 实证发现所评估模型难以可靠完成这些工作流：应用可通过许多局部检查但整体任务未完成；更高的局部覆盖率可能伴随更少的已完成工作流。

Conclusion: 以"编排+共享运行时"复用的方式为需求演进下的应用个性化提供了轻量路径；但现有模型在端到端可靠完成个性化服务工作流方面仍存在明显差距，局部正确性不能代表任务完成度。

Abstract: Applications provide convenient features for users to complete daily tasks. However, different users may have different or even conflicting requirements for the same application, while the requirements of the same user may change over time. Personalizing applications produced by agent-to-app or UI-to-code approaches can require changes to the generated implementation. We propose Agent as App, a task in which users express their requirements in natural language and an agent creates a personalized application while continuing to supply its behavior during use. We implement LiveUIAgent, which generates a task-specific orchestration connecting the user interface, saved information, and service actions. A shared agent runtime renders and updates the interface while carrying out the requested operations. Revising the orchestration and interface while reusing that runtime provides a lightweight basis for personalization as requirements evolve. We introduce LiveUIBench, a benchmark with diverse requirements and ordered interactions across personal services. The empirical findings show that the evaluated models struggle to complete these workflows reliably. Applications can satisfy many local checks while leaving the overall task unfinished, and greater partial coverage can coincide with fewer completed workflows.

</details>


### [298] [Training the Whole Agent, Not Just the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28069)
*Lingxiao Tang,Wei Tao,Yiwen Guo,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessAdapt-RL，在强化学习中联合训练策略并自适应调整其 harness（技能、工具与钩子），形成“模型-框架”配对，在 SWE-bench 三个基准上显著超过 GRPO 及人工设计 harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体训练只更新策略而固定 harness，但训练轨迹暴露出随任务变化、随策略演化的修复策略缺陷与交互问题，固定 harness 无法针对性解决。

Method: 在 RL 过程中同步适配 harness：对任务特定的修复策略缺陷，用冻结教师生成机制级技能；对跨任务的交互问题，用独立教师模型演化工具与钩子，仅在提升观测通过率或可训练率且不降低另一指标时保留改动；策略在适配后的 harness 中训练，新轨迹再驱动进一步适配。

Result: 9B 基座模型上，pass@1 在 SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual、Pro 上较 GRPO 分别提升 9.8、12.6、6.4 个百分点，并优于使用人工设计 OpenHands harness 的 GRPO；机制级技能在评估时移除后收益仍保留；随策略演化重新演化工具与钩子优于冻结早期版本。

Conclusion: 训练产出的是“模型-harness 对”：训练后的策略与演化出的工具/钩子；部署时应移除技能，若同时移除工具与钩子则最多损失 7.7 个百分点。

Abstract: Training coding agents typically updates the policy while keeping the harness fixed. During training, trajectories reveal flawed repair strategies and interaction problems that vary across tasks and change as the policy learns. We introduce HarnessAdapt-RL, which trains the policy and adapts its harness during reinforcement learning. For flawed repair strategies, a frozen teacher generates task-specific mechanism-level skills. For interaction problems recurring across tasks, a separate teacher model evolves tools and hooks, keeping each change only if it improves the observed pass rate or the observed trainable rate without lowering either. The policy trains with the adapted harness, and its new trajectories guide further adaptation. With a 9B base model, HarnessAdapt-RL improves pass@1 over GRPO by 9.8, 12.6, and 6.4 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, Multilingual, and Pro, and outperforms GRPO with the human-designed OpenHands harness. Gains from mechanism-level skills persist after their removal at evaluation. Re-evolving tools and hooks as the policy changes outperforms freezing early versions. Removing them at deployment, in contrast, costs up to 7.7 percentage points. Training therefore produces a model-harness pair: the trained policy and the evolved tools and hooks, with skills removed at deployment.

</details>


### [299] [AgenTest: Mining Business Rules to Test LLM Agents for Business Logic Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28116)
*Lili Quan,Longtian Wang,Yuelin Wang,Mingfei Cheng,Chao Shen,Sen Chen,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 AgenTest，从系统提示、工具定义与领域知识中挖掘业务规则，精化为 Given-When-Then 规范并逐条系统性测试 LLM 智能体，在 tau2-bench 三类场景中检出远超基线的业务规则违规（3.4X/2.5X），且发现跨用户数据越权访问问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体通过工具执行业务操作时须遵守领域业务规则，但这类规则多分散于系统提示与工具定义中、部分仅隐含于领域知识，缺乏显式可测规范；违规则导致任务"看似成功"却产生业务逻辑缺陷。现有测试方法仅关注任务成功率、预定义策略或已知攻击场景，难以系统发现未显式规定的业务规则违规。

Method: 1) 从系统提示、工具定义和领域知识中挖掘业务规则；2) 将规则精化为 Given-When-Then 规范；3) 对每条规范，构造满足 Given 条件的初始状态与模拟用户，通过多轮交互驱动智能体；4) 用混合预言机（hybrid oracle）在消息、工具调用与状态变更三层面上检验 Then 条件。

Result: 在 tau2-bench 的航空、零售、电信三类智能体、两种底层模型上，共生成 551 条精化规范，分别检出 89 和 121 条不同规范的违规，为所有基线合并结果的 3.4 倍与 2.5 倍；仅 AgenTest 能检出智能体不当访问或修改其他用户数据的情形；约 60% 的触发缺陷的运行无法仅凭最终状态识别。

Conclusion: 基于显式业务规则规范的规约化测试能有效弥补任务成功率导向测试的盲区；必须对完整交互过程（而非仅最终状态）进行核验，才能揭示业务规则违规，尤其涉及跨用户数据访问的场景。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly execute business operations through tools and interact directly with application data and services. Beyond completing user requests, these agents must obey domain-specific business rules that constrain when actions are permitted and what data or resources may be affected. Violating such rules can lead to business logic bugs, even when a task appears successful. Detecting these bugs is difficult because the rules are rarely available as explicit, testable specifications. They are distributed across system prompts and tool definitions, while others remain implicit in domain knowledge. Existing agent testing approaches typically evaluate task success, predefined policies, or known attack scenarios. This limits their ability to systematically uncover previously unspecified business-rule violations.
  We present AgenTest, which mines business rules from system prompts, tool definitions, and domain knowledge, refines them into Given-When-Then specifications, and systematically tests each specification. For each extracted specification, AgenTest instantiates its Given conditions by constructing a satisfying environment state and simulated user, exercises the agent through multi-turn interactions, and checks its Then conditions over messages, tool calls, and state changes using a hybrid oracle. On the airline, retail, and telecom agents of tau2-bench with two underlying models, AgenTest produces 551 refined specifications, and detects violations of 89 and 121 distinct specifications, respectively -- 3.4X and 2.5X as many as all baselines combined. Only AgenTest detects cases where an agent improperly accesses or modifies another user's data. Moreover, about 60% of bug-triggering runs cannot be detected from the final state alone, highlighting the need to check the full interaction against explicit business rules.

</details>


### [300] [From Code Generator to Engineering Companion: A Plant-aware Agent for PLC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28160)
*Joschka Kersting,Gesa Benndorf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种"工厂感知"的对话式PLC编程助手：通过工厂专属上下文注入、对话优先的交互模式以及从聊天到部署的分级工具访问权限，将一次性代码生成扩展为可持续的、人在闭环的工程协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLC编程仍是依赖IDE的手工任务，工程师需在文档、工程文件与代码编辑器之间频繁切换上下文。已有检索增强大模型可生成高编译率的厂商合规结构化文本（Structured Text），但采用一次性流水线范式：提交查询即得到代码，系统对具体工厂缺乏持久知识。

Method: 在既有检索增强生成框架上做三方面扩展：（1）工厂专属上下文摄取；（2）以对话为中心的交互模型，支持代码解释与调试；（3）从纯聊天到实际部署的分级工具访问阶梯（graduated tool-access ladder）。系统面向真实工厂基础设施设计。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，仅说明系统设计目标：适配真实工厂基础设施，并在每个产生现实影响的步骤上保持工程师的控制权。

Conclusion: 将PLC代码助手从一次性生成管道升级为具备工厂知识、可对话、权限渐进的工程协同助手，并以人在闭环为安全与责任边界。

Abstract: Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) coding remains a manual, IDE-centric task that demands constant context switching between documentation, project files, and code editors. Recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented large language models can generate vendor-compliant Structured Text at high compilation rates \cite{pred}, yet these systems treat code generation as a one-shot pipeline: the engineer submits a query and receives code, with no persistent knowledge of the specific plant. We present a plant-aware conversational copilot that extends this paradigm in three directions: plant-specific context ingestion, a chat-first interaction model for explanation and debugging, and a graduated tool-access ladder from chat to deployment. The system is designed for real plant infrastructure and keeps the engineer in control of each step that produces real-world effects.

</details>


### [301] [Foreword: Pre-Generation Prediction and Characterisation of LLM Test Failures using the Jacobian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28187)
*Somin Kim,Robert Feldt,Shin Yoo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Foreword 是一个基于"同时学习通过与失败参考"和"J-lens 可解释性表征"的预生成充分性框架，配合稀疏 LR 探针或双 GMM 估计器，在十个任务、三个开源权重 LLM 上一致优于 Clotho。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 软件测试成本高：每个输入需多次采样响应并由昂贵 oracle 判定，且提示或模型变更都需重测。现有预生成充分性方法（如 SOTA 的 Clotho）仅对通过参考输入建模，其隐状态表征无法解释失败原因，也无法判断输入是否超出参考覆盖范围。

Method: 提出 Foreword，两项核心设计：(1) 同时利用通过和失败的参考输入进行学习；(2) 用 Jacobian lens（J-lens）将中间激活读作词表 token 上的分数，形成可解释表征。表征之上配两种估计器：稀疏逻辑回归探针或双高斯混合模型（dual GMM）；dual GMM 额外提供参考密度（标记参考未覆盖的输入）与失败类分量（聚类相似失败）。

Result: 在 10 个任务、3 个开放权重 LLM 上，表征与估计器的四种组合平均均优于 Clotho：失败预测 ROC-AUC 平均提升 0.14，最高 0.31；测试优先级排序中前 100 个输入的平均失败数增加 15%，最高 33%；小模型得到的优先级排序向专有模型迁移更好（前四分之一优先级输入中失败数平均多 22%，最高 41%）。定性案例表明 Foreword 可对失败做语义刻画，指出标签或任务规约中的歧义。

Conclusion: Foreword 无需生成输出即可支持测试者进行输入优先级排序、预测支持性检查与具体失败刻画，是一种低成本、可解释且可迁移的 LLM 软件预生成测试辅助框架。

Abstract: Testing LLM-based software is expensive: each input needs several sampled responses, each judged by a costly oracle. Further, every prompt or model change calls for re-testing. Pre-generation adequacy measures cut this cost by ranking inputs by failure risk based on the model's internal state. However, the state of the art, Clotho, models only passing reference inputs, and its hidden-state representation says nothing about why inputs fail. We present Foreword, a framework built on two ideas: learn from both passing and failing references, and represent inputs through the Jacobian lens (J-lens), an interpretability technique that reads intermediate activations out as scores over vocabulary tokens. Foreword pairs these representations with two estimators: a sparse logistic-regression probe or dual Gaussian Mixture Models (dual GMMs), which also yield a reference density (to flag inputs the references do not cover) and failing-class components (to group similar failures). On ten tasks and three open-weight LLMs, all four combinations of representations and estimators outperform Clotho on average, both for prediction of failure (ROC-AUC improves by 0.14 on average and by up to 0.31) and test prioritisation (15% more failures among the 100 top-ranked inputs on average, up to 33%). Prioritisation obtained using small models also transfers better to proprietary models (on average 22% and up to 41% more failures in the first quarter of prioritised inputs). Importantly, our qualitative case study shows that Foreword supports semantic characterisation of failures by pointing to ambiguities in labels or task specifications. Combined, Foreword can support testers for prioritising inputs, checking support for a prediction, and characterising specific failures, all without generating output.

</details>


### [302] [ExeBench-FHD: Addressing Fidelity Issues in Decompiler Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28212)
*Akarsh Ashok,Dinil Mon Divakaran,Vivek Balachandran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析ExeBench基准的九个缺陷，并提出向后兼容的修订版ExeBench-FHD，新增16个字段与五类警告标志，显著提升LLM反编译器的可评测性与复现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ExeBench是评估LLM反编译器最常用的基准之一，但其样本存在三类阻碍LLM从汇编恢复C代码的结构性问题，以及六类影响测试有效性的问题，导致测量结果不能真实反映反编译器的能力。

Method: 系统性地分析并归纳ExeBench的九个问题：①整数与浮点混用时的参数顺序歧义；②被优化汇编丢弃的未使用参数；③存储在函数汇编之外的常量；④非void函数缺少return；⑤占位类型；⑥被优化器移除的static/inline函数；⑦名为main的函数；⑧平凡函数体；⑨被截断的定义。据此构建ExeBench-FHD，新增16个字段（调用点汇编、数据段、测试包装器、链接二进制反汇编及五个警告标志）。

Result: 在ExeBench中38.9%的函数至少带有一个警告标志；剔除四个警告标志（unused_param除外，因其标记的函数可被新包装器评测）后保留63.9%的语料。在四种架构、-O2下评测，新字段使GPT 6 Sol在读取数据段、混用参数类型、含未使用参数的函数上的重执行率分别提升最高26.5%、19.1%和21.0%。

Conclusion: ExeBench存在影响反编译器评测保真度的系统性缺陷；ExeBench-FHD通过补充调用上下文、数据段与警告标志，在保持向后兼容的同时有效提升了评测有效性与LLM反编译器的可复现率。

Abstract: ExeBench is one of the most widely used benchmarks for evaluating LLM-based decompilers. We analyze ExeBench and identify nine issues that affect how faithfully a test measures a decompiler. Three of them prevent LLM decompilers from recovering C code from ExeBench's function. These are ambiguous parameter order when integer and floating-point types are mixed, unused parameters that optimized assembly may drop, and constants stored outside the function's assembly code. Six more affect test validity: non-void functions without a return, placeholder types, static or inline functions removed by the optimizer, functions named main, trivial bodies and truncated definitions. We present ExeBench-FHD, a backward-compatible revision of ExeBench that adds 16 new fields: call-site assembly, data sections, test wrappers, linked-binary disassembly and five warning flags. In ExeBench, 38.9% of functions have at least one of the five flags; dropping records with any of the four warning flags (all except unused_param, which marks functions that the new wrappers make evaluable) keeps 63.9% of the corpus. In an evaluation at -O2 on four architectures, the new fields raise GPT 6 Sol's re-execution rate by up to 26.5%, 19.1% and 21.0% on functions that read data sections, mix parameter types and have unused parameters, respectively.

</details>


### [303] [InterWebCoder: Learning Interactive Webpage Generation from Executable Tests via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28610)
*Jingyu Xiao,Dasen Dai,Yuxuan Wan,Jen-tse Huang,Yintong Huo,Hongyu Zhang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 InterWebCoder 框架，用于交互式网页代码生成：通过测试规范引导与执行验证的数据合成流程产出可执行测试脚本，结合测试ID加权监督微调与组相对策略优化直接优化功能正确性和视觉保真度，以可执行反馈替代 GUI agent 验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型难以同时复现交互式网页的视觉外观与多步行为；可扩展训练数据需涵盖用户动作、状态转移与预期结果，而真实网页代码常与无关脚本和外部依赖纠缠；现有基于 GUI agent 的验证反馈慢且不稳定。
