Method: 1) Test-Specification-Guided and Execution-Verified 数据合成流程：记录真实交互轨迹并合成自包含网页代码与可执行测试脚本；2) 失败归因修复机制：利用运行时证据定位并迭代修复网页代码或测试脚本中的错误；3) 训练策略：测试ID加权监督微调 + 组相对策略优化（GRPO），以测试通过率和状态级视觉相似度作为奖励。

Result: 从 2,058 个任务产出 1,736 个已验证样本，合成成功率 84.35%；在 IWR-Bench 与 Vision2Web 上以 9B 骨干分别取得 48.54 与 48.42 的最终分数，超过 30B 级模型与 Gemini-3.7-Flash，并与 Claude-Sonnet-5 保持竞争力；奖励计算延迟降低 97% 以上，消除外部 MLLM 验证成本，端到端训练加速最高 1.4 倍。

Conclusion: 以快速、可复现的可执行测试反馈替代昂贵的 GUI agent 验证，是小模型在交互式网页代码生成上实现高效且强竞争力的有效路径。

Abstract: Interactive webpages underpin modern applications, but reproducing both their visual appearance and multi-step behavior remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Scalable training data must capture user actions, state transitions, and expected outcomes, while real-world webpage code is often entangled with irrelevant scripts and external dependencies. Moreover, existing GUI-agent-based verification provides slow and potentially unstable functional feedback. To address these challenges, we propose InterWebCoder, an efficient framework for interactive webpage code generation. InterWebCoder introduces a Test-Specification-Guided and Execution-Verified Data Synthesis pipeline that records real-world interaction trajectories and synthesizes self-contained webpage code with executable test scripts. A failureattributed repair mechanism uses runtime evidence to locate and iteratively fix errors in either the webpage code or test script. We then combine test-ID-weighted supervised fine-tuning with group-relative policy optimization, directly optimizing functional correctness and visual fidelity using test pass rates and state-level visual similarity. This replaces costly GUI-agent verification with fast, reproducible executable feedback. Our pipeline produces 1,736 verified samples from 2,058 tasks, achieving an 84.35% synthesis success rate. On IWR-Bench and Vision2Web, InterWebCoder achieves final scores of 48.54 and 48.42 with a 9B backbone, outperforming 30B-level models and Gemini-3.7-Flash while remaining competitive with Claude-Sonnet-5. It reduces reward-computation latency by over 97%, eliminates external MLLM verification costs, and accelerates end-to-end training by up to 1.4x.

</details>


### [304] [TraceLite: Efficient Trace Reduction for SWE Agents via Optical Compression and Redundancy Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28612)
*Jingyu Xiao,Qianxi Liu,Yintong Huo,Yuan Jiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceLite：无需训练、无需辅助LLM的软件工程智能体交互轨迹压缩框架——近期观测保留原文，历史观测渲染为图像，去除重叠内容，并在代码编辑后丢弃阶段过期负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE智能体通过迭代工具使用解决仓库级任务，交互轨迹不断增长，导致大量token消耗与推理开销；现有压缩方法依赖模型训练、辅助LLM调用，或依赖源代码等特定内容假设，通用性受限。

Method: 先做初步研究：光学渲染在中度压缩下能保留较多文本与语义信息；智能体轨迹存在跨步骤冗余与阶段级冗余。据此设计四步流程：(1) 近期观测保留高保真文本；(2) 历史观测渲染为紧凑图像；(3) 删除重叠内容；(4) 代码编辑后丢弃阶段过期的观测负载。全程无离线训练、无辅助LLM推理。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified与DeepSWE上，以GPT-5.6-Luna和Gemini-3.8Flash为骨干模型评测。SWE-bench Verified上resolve率为78.25%，优于所评压缩基线；平均输入token消耗与API成本分别降低33.05%和21.30%；每个观测仅引入0.438秒压缩延迟。

Conclusion: TraceLite以低处理开销提升SWE智能体效率，且不损害问题解决能力，为异构观测压缩提供了一种简单有效的无训练方案。

Abstract: Software engineering agents solve repository-level tasks through iterative tool use, but their continuously growing interaction traces incur substantial token consumption and inference overhead. Existing compression approaches often require model training, auxiliary LLM calls, or assumptions about specific content such as source code. We present TraceLite, a simple yet effective, training-free framework for compressing heterogeneous SWE-agent observations. Our preliminary study shows that optical rendering preserves substantial textual and semantic information under moderate compression, while agent trajectories exhibit both cross-step and phase-level redundancy. Motivated by these findings, TraceLite preserves recent observations as high-fidelity text, renders historical observations as compact images, removes overlapping content, and discards phase-expired observation payloads after code editing. It requires neither offline training nor auxiliary LLM inference. We evaluate TraceLite on SWE-bench Verified and DeepSWE using GPT-5.6-Luna and Gemini-3.8Flash as backbone models. On SWE-bench Verified, TraceLite achieves 78.25% resolve rate, outperforming the evaluated compression baselines while reducing average input-token consumption and API cost by 33.05% and 21.30%, respectively. Moreover, it introduces only 0.438 seconds of compression latency per observation. These results demonstrate that TraceLite improves SWE-agent efficiency with low processing overhead and without compromising issue-resolution capability. Our code is available at https://github.com/xjywhu/TraceLite.

</details>
